根据上述分析可画出 $\pmb { \sigma } ^ { * }$ 提和 $\pmb { \sigma }$ 的空间位置示意图，如图 9.5.3 所示，图中单股线表示 $\pmb { \sigma }$ 的变

化轨迹，双股线表示 $\pmb { \sigma } ^ { * }$ 樨的变化轨迹，箭头表示对应的变化方向，虚线表示在单位球内的轨迹，实线表示在单位球外的轨在球心。处， ${ \pmb \sigma } = { \bf 0 }$ 对应于 $\phi = \boldsymbol { 0 } ^ { \circ } , \pmb { \sigma } ^ { \ast } = \mathbf { 0 }$ 对应于 $\phi =$ $\pm 3 6 0 ^ { \circ }$ 。当 $\pmb { \sigma }$ 自球心向外运动时，相对应的 $\overleftarrow { \pmb { \sigma } } ^ { \ast }$ 自元穷远处向单位球运动;当 $\pmb { \sigma }$ 穿出单位球时， $\mathbf { \nabla } _ { \mathbf { \sigma } } \sigma ^ { * }$ 进入单位球，在球面穿越点 ${ \mathbf { \sigma } } , { \pmb { \sigma } } ^ { * } = - { \pmb { \sigma } }$ ， 即两者相对球，心中心对称;当 $\pmb { \sigma }$ 运动至无穷远， $\pmb { \sigma } ^ { * }$ 运动至球心

上所述， ${ \bf \nabla } \cdot \pmb { \sigma }$ 和 $\pmb { \sigma } ^ { * }$ 的切换点可选择在单位球面上，即:当$\pmb { \sigma } ^ { \mathrm { T } } \pmb { \sigma } { \leqslant } 1$ 时，姿态解算采用 ${ \pmb \sigma } ;$ 当 $\mathrm { \bf \delta } _ { [ \pmb { \sigma } } ^ { \mathrm { T } } \pmb { \sigma } ^ { \mathrm { > } 1 }$ 时， 态解算采用 $\pmb { \sigma } ^ { * }$

$\pmb { \sigma } ^ { * }$ 的微分方程推导如下。

由式 (9. 5. 35a) ,

$$
\sigma_ {i} ^ {*} = \frac {q _ {i}}{q _ {0} - 1} \quad i = 1, 2, 3\tag{9.5.37}
$$

![](images/a256929567c1f4af98922450d108adf7141d2beb278d879d00e67e9dd9139051.jpg)  
改进型罗德和 参数其影 数的变化关系

上式对时间求导，得:

$$
\dot {\sigma} _ {1} ^ {*} = \frac {\dot {q} _ {1} (q _ {0} - 1) - \dot {q} _ {0} q _ {1}}{(q _ {0} - 1) ^ {2}}, \quad \dot {\sigma} _ {2} ^ {*} = \frac {\dot {q} _ {2} (q _ {0} - 1) - \dot {q} _ {0} q _ {2}}{(q _ {0} - 1) ^ {2}}, \quad \dot {\sigma} _ {3} ^ {*} = \frac {\dot {q} _ {3} (q _ {0} - 1) - \dot {q} _ {0} q _ {3}}{(q _ {0} - 1) ^ {2}}
$$

即

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left[ \begin{array}{c} \dot {\sigma} _ {1} ^ {*} \\ \dot {\sigma} _ {2} ^ {*} \\ \dot {\sigma} _ {3} ^ {*} \end{array} \right] = \frac {1}{(q _ {0} - 1) ^ {2}} \left[ \begin{array}{c c c c} - q _ {1} & q _ {0} - 1 & 0 & 0 \\ - q _ {2} & 0 & q _ {0} - 1 & 0 \\ - q _ {3} & 0 & 0 & q _ {0} - 1 \end{array} \right] \left[ \begin{array}{c} \dot {q} _ {0} \\ \dot {q} _ {1} \\ \dot {q} _ {2} \\ \dot {q} _ {3} \end{array} \right] \\ = \frac {1}{2 (q _ {0} - 1) ^ {2}} \left[ \begin{array}{c c c c} - q _ {1} & q _ {0} - 1 & 0 & 0 \\ - q _ {2} & 0 & q _ {0} - 1 & 0 \\ - q _ {3} & 0 & 0 & q _ {0} - 1 \end{array} \right] \\ = \frac {1}{2 (q _ {0} - 1) ^ {2}} \left[ \begin{array}{c c c c} \times & q _ {1} ^ {2} + q _ {0} (q _ {0} - 1) & q _ {1} q _ {2} - q _ {3} (q _ {0} - 1) & q _ {1} q _ {3} + q _ {2} (q _ {0} - 1) \\ \times & q _ {1} q _ {2} + q _ {3} (q _ {0} - 1) & q _ {2} ^ {2} + q _ {0} (q _ {0} - 1) & q _ {2} q _ {3} - q _ {1} (q _ {0} - 1) \\ \times & q _ {1} q _ {3} - q _ {2} (q _ {0} - 1) & q _ {2} q _ {3} + q _ {1} (q _ {0} - 1) & q _ {3} ^ {2} + q _ {0} (q _ {0} - 1) \end{array} \right] \left[ \begin{array}{c} 0 \\ \omega_ {x} \\ \omega_ {y} \\ \omega_ {z} \end{array} \right] \\ = \frac {1}{2} \left[ \begin{array}{c c c c} \sigma_ {1} ^ {* 2} + \frac {q _ {0}}{q _ {0} - 1} & \sigma_ {1} ^ {*} \sigma_ {2} ^ {*} - \sigma_ {3} ^ {*} & \sigma_ {1} ^ {*} \sigma_ {3} ^ {*} + \sigma_ {2} ^ {*} \\ \sigma_ {1} ^ {*} \sigma_ {2} ^ {*} + \sigma_ {3} ^ {*} & \sigma_ {2} ^ {* 2} + \frac {q _ {0}}{q _ {0} - 1} & \sigma_ {2} ^ {*} \sigma_ {3} ^ {*} - \sigma_ {1} ^ {*} \\ \sigma_ {1} ^ {*} \sigma_ {3} ^ {*} - \sigma_ {2} ^ {*} & \sigma_ {2} ^ {*} \sigma_ {3} ^ {*} + \sigma_ {1} ^ {*} & \sigma_ {3} ^ {* 2} + \frac {q _ {0}}{q _ {0} - 1} \end{array} \right] \left[ \begin{array}{c} \omega_ {x} \\ \omega_ {y} \\ \omega_ {z} \end{array} \right] \end{array}
$$

上式推导利用了式(9.5.37)

根据(9. 5. 37)式和四元数的归一化特性，有

$$
\begin{array}{r l} q _ {i} & = (q _ {0} - 1) \sigma_ {i} ^ {*} \quad i = 1, 2, 3 \\ & q _ {0} ^ {2} + q _ {1} ^ {2} + q _ {2} ^ {2} + q _ {3} ^ {2} = 1 \end{array}\tag{a}
$$

(b)

将式 (a) 代人式 (b) ,

$$
q _ {0} ^ {2} + (q _ {0} - 1) ^ {2} \left(\sigma_ {1} ^ {* 2} + \sigma_ {2} ^ {* 2} + \sigma_ {3} ^ {* 2}\right) = 1
$$

RP

$$
q _ {0} + 1 + (q _ {0} - 1) \boldsymbol {\sigma} ^ {* T} \boldsymbol {\sigma} ^ {*} = 0
$$

所以

$$
q _ {0} = \frac {\pmb {\sigma} ^ {* \mathrm{T}} \pmb {\sigma} ^ {*} - 1}{\pmb {\sigma} ^ {* \mathrm{T}} \pmb {\sigma} ^ {*} + 1}\tag{9.5.38a}
$$

代人式(a)

$$
\begin{array}{c} q _ {i} = - \frac {2}{\boldsymbol {\sigma} ^ {* \mathrm{T}} \boldsymbol {\sigma} ^ {*} + 1} \sigma_ {i} ^ {*} \\ \frac {q _ {0}}{q _ {0} - 1} = \frac {\boldsymbol {\sigma} ^ {* \mathrm{T}} \boldsymbol {\sigma} ^ {*} - 1}{\boldsymbol {\sigma} ^ {* \mathrm{T}} \boldsymbol {\sigma} ^ {*} - 1 - \boldsymbol {\sigma} ^ {* \mathrm{T}} \boldsymbol {\sigma} ^ {*} - 1} = \frac {1}{2} - \frac {1}{2} \boldsymbol {\sigma} ^ {* \mathrm{T}} \boldsymbol {\sigma} ^ {*} \end{array}\tag{9.5.38b}
$$

因此

$$
\left[ \begin{array}{c} \dot {\sigma} _ {1} ^ {*} \\ \dot {\sigma} _ {2} ^ {*} \\ \dot {\sigma} _ {3} ^ {*} \end{array} \right] = \frac {1}{4} \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} 1 + \sigma_ {1} ^ {* 2} - \sigma_ {2} ^ {* 2} - \sigma_ {3} ^ {* 2} & \sigma_ {1} ^ {*} \sigma_ {2} ^ {*} - \sigma_ {3} ^ {*} & \sigma_ {1} ^ {*} \sigma_ {3} ^ {*} + \sigma_ {2} ^ {*} \\ \sigma_ {1} ^ {*} \sigma_ {2} ^ {*} + \sigma_ {3} ^ {*} & 1 - \sigma_ {1} ^ {* 2} + \sigma_ {2} ^ {* 2} - \sigma_ {3} ^ {* 2} & \sigma_ {2} ^ {*} \sigma_ {3} ^ {*} - \sigma_ {1} ^ {*} \\ \sigma_ {1} ^ {*} \sigma_ {3} ^ {*} - \sigma_ {2} ^ {*} & \sigma_ {2} ^ {*} \sigma_ {3} ^ {*} + \sigma_ {1} ^ {*} & 1 - \sigma_ {1} ^ {* 2} - \sigma_ {2} ^ {* 2} + \sigma_ {3} ^ {* 2} \end{array} \right] \left[ \begin{array}{c} \omega_ {x} \\ \omega_ {y} \\ \omega_ {z} \end{array} \right]\tag{9.5.39}
$$

根据式(9. 2. 34) 和式(9. 5. 38) ，可得由改进型罗德利格参数的影像求取姿态阵的公式

$$
\mathbf {C} _ {b} ^ {n} = \frac {1}{(1 + \sigma_ {1} ^ {* 2} + \sigma_ {2} ^ {* 2} + \sigma_ {3} ^ {* 2}) ^ {2}} \\ \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} \Sigma^ {* 2} + 4 (\sigma_ {1} ^ {* 2} - \sigma_ {2} ^ {* 2} - \sigma_ {3} ^ {* 2}) & 8 \sigma_ {1} ^ {*} \sigma_ {2} ^ {*} - 4 \Sigma^ {*} \sigma_ {3} ^ {*} & 8 \sigma_ {1} ^ {*} \sigma_ {3} ^ {*} + 4 \Sigma^ {*} \sigma_ {2} ^ {*} \\ 8 \sigma_ {1} ^ {*} \sigma_ {2} ^ {*} + 4 \Sigma^ {*} \sigma_ {3} ^ {*} & \Sigma^ {* 2} + 4 (\sigma_ {2} ^ {* 2} - \sigma_ {1} ^ {* 2} - \sigma_ {3} ^ {* 2}) & 8 \sigma_ {2} ^ {*} \sigma_ {3} ^ {*} - 4 \Sigma^ {*} \sigma_ {1} ^ {*} \\ 8 \sigma_ {1} ^ {*} \sigma_ {3} ^ {*} - 4 \Sigma^ {*} \sigma_ {2} ^ {*} & 8 \sigma_ {2} ^ {*} \sigma_ {3} ^ {*} + 4 \Sigma^ {*} \sigma_ {1} ^ {*} & \Sigma^ {* 2} + 4 (\sigma_ {3} ^ {* 2} - \sigma_ {1} ^ {* 2} - \sigma_ {2} ^ {* 2}) \end{array} \right]\tag{9.5.40}
$$

式中

$$
\boldsymbol {\Sigma} ^ {*} = 1 - \boldsymbol {\sigma} ^ {* T} \boldsymbol {\sigma} ^ {*}\tag{9.5.41}
$$

## 3. 非对称型投影参数

如果取投影超平面通过超球球心，且超平面法线与 $q _ { 1 }$ 轴平行，投影点取 (0 -1 时，如9.5.4 所示，其中 i=0 则所得技影参数为非对称型技影参数 由图得投影参数为

$$
\eta = \tan \alpha = \frac {q _ {i}}{q _ {1} + 1} \quad i = 0, 2, 3
$$

即

$$
\eta_ {1} = \frac {q _ {0}}{q _ {1} + 1}
$$

$$
\eta_ {2} = \frac {q _ {2}}{q _ {1} + 1}\tag{9.5.42a}
$$

(9 . 5. 42b)

$$
\eta_ {3} = \frac {q _ {3}}{q _ {1} + 1}\tag{9.5.42c}
$$

![](images/e1936da8aa3acc392ff668ff94841727e2618976129abacec94afe542c30e86c.jpg)

显然，当四元数在超球面上越靠近投影点$( 0 , - 1 , 0 , 0 )$ ，在技影超平面上的技影 数就越远离超球球心，对于四元数 $Q = [ 0 , - 1 , 0 , 0 ] ^ { \prime }$ 投影参数∞，即技影参数出现奇异值。由

非对称型投影参9.

$$
\boldsymbol {Q} = \left[ \begin{array}{c} 0 \\ - 1 \\ 0 \\ 0 \end{array} \right] = \left[ \begin{array}{c} \cos \frac {\phi}{2} \\ l \sin \frac {\phi}{2} \\ m \sin \frac {\phi}{2} \\ n \sin \frac {\phi}{2} \end{array} \right]
$$

所描述的刚体等效旋转为:旋转方向为 $\pmb { \imath } = [ 1 \quad 0 \quad 0$ Y 旋转角为 $\phi _ { s 1 } = - 1 8 0 ^ { \circ }$ 即绕 轴的负方向旋转过 $1 8 0 ^ { \circ }$ 。根据 9.2 节所述，这 角位置也可用旋转角 $\phi _ { s 2 } = 3 6 0 ^ { \circ } - \phi _ { s 1 } = 5 4 0 ^ { \circ }$ 来表示。因此用此非对称型投影参数描述刚体的等效旋转 ，确保投影参数非奇异的最大转角范围为$( - 1 8 0 ^ { \circ } , 5 4 0 ^ { \circ } )$ 可见此范围是不对称的，因此称投影参数为非对称型技影参数

对称型投影 数的推导相仿，可得非对称型投影 数的微分方程:

$$
\dot {\boldsymbol {\eta}} = \frac {1}{4} \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} - 1 - \eta_ {1} ^ {2} + \eta_ {2} ^ {2} + \eta_ {3} ^ {2} & 2 (\eta_ {1} \eta_ {3} - \eta_ {2}) & - 2 (\eta_ {1} \eta_ {2} + \eta_ {3}) \\ - 2 (\eta_ {1} \eta_ {2} - \eta_ {3}) & 2 (\eta_ {2} \eta_ {3} + \eta_ {1}) & - 1 + \eta_ {1} ^ {2} - \eta_ {2} ^ {2} + \eta_ {3} ^ {2} \\ - 2 (\eta_ {1} \eta_ {3} + \eta_ {2}) & 1 - \eta_ {1} ^ {2} - \eta_ {2} ^ {2} + \eta_ {3} ^ {2} & - 2 (\eta_ {2} \eta_ {3} - \eta_ {1}) \end{array} \right] \left[ \begin{array}{l} \omega_ {x} \\ \omega_ {y} \\ \omega_ {z} \end{array} \right]\tag{9.5.43}
$$

由非对称型投影参数确定的姿态阵为

$$
\boldsymbol {C} _ {b} ^ {n} = \frac {1}{(1 + \boldsymbol {\eta} ^ {\mathrm{T}} \boldsymbol {\eta}) ^ {2}} \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} 4 (\eta_ {1} ^ {2} - \eta_ {2} ^ {2} - \eta_ {3} ^ {2}) + H ^ {2} & 8 \eta_ {1} \eta_ {2} + 4 \eta_ {2} H & 8 \eta_ {1} \eta_ {2} + 4 \eta_ {3} H \\ - 8 \eta_ {1} \eta_ {3} + 4 \eta_ {2} H & 4 (\eta_ {1} ^ {2} + \eta_ {2} ^ {2} - \eta_ {3} ^ {2}) - H ^ {2} & 8 \eta_ {2} \eta_ {3} + 4 \eta_ {1} H \\ 8 \eta_ {1} \eta_ {2} + 4 \eta_ {3} H & 8 \eta_ {2} \eta_ {3} - 4 \eta_ {1} H & 4 (\eta_ {1} ^ {2} - \eta_ {2} ^ {2} + \eta_ {3} ^ {2}) - H ^ {2} \end{array} \right]\tag{9.5.44}
$$

式中

$$
H = 1 - \boldsymbol {\eta} ^ {\mathrm{T}} \boldsymbol {\eta}\tag{9.5.45}
$$

投影超平面的法线还可以选择 $q _ { 2 }$ 轴或 $q _ { 3 }$ 轴，也可选择除 $q _ { 0 }$ 轴之外的任意方向上的轴，都可得到不同的非对称型投影参数，这些参数具有相似的特性，读者可根据需要选择投影超平面。

## 9.5.4 姿态更新各种算法的比较

欧拉角算法通过求解欧拉角微分方程直接计算航向角、俯仰角和横滚角。欧拉角微分方程关系简单明了，概念直观，容易理解，解算过程中元需作正交化处理，但方程中包含有三角运算，这给实时计算带来一定困难。而且当俯仰角接近 $9 0 ^ { \circ }$ 时方程出现退化现象，这相当于平台惯导中惯性平台的锁定，所以这种方法只适用于水平姿态变化不大的情况，而不适用于全姿态运载体的姿态确定。

方向余弦法对姿态矩阵微分方程作求解，避免了欧拉角法中方程的退化问题，可全姿态工作。但姿态矩阵微分方程实质上是包含九个未知量的线性微分方程组，与四元数法相比，计算量大，实时计算困难，所以工程上并不实用。

四元数法只需求解四个未知量的线性微分方程组，计算量比方向余弦法小，且算法简单，易于操作，是较实用的工程方法。但四元数法实质上是旋转矢量法中的单子样算法，对有限转动引起的不可交换误差的补偿程度不够，所以只适用于低动态运载体〈如运输机等)的姿态解算.而对高动态运载体，姿态解算中的算法漂移会十分严重。

旋转矢量法可采用多子样算法实现对不可交换误差作有效补偿，算法关系简单，易于操作，并且通过对系数的优化处理使算法漂移在相同子样算法中达到最小，因此特别适用于角机动频繁激烈或存在严重角振动的运载体的姿态更新。

四元数法和旋转矢量法都通过计算姿态四元数实现姿态更新，但前者直接求解姿态四元数微分方程，而后者通过求解姿态变化四元数再求解姿态四元数，两者的算法思路并不相同。

对于诸如人造卫星等角运动比较规则的运载体，常采用罗德里格参数实现姿态确定和控制。罗德里格参数实质上是四元数在三维超平面上的投影，它去除了四元数的一个冗余度，降低了计算量。

## 9. 6 捷联惯导系统的速度算法

在捷联惯导系统中，陀螺和加速度计的输出经计算机按一定的算法作处理后，获得离散时间点上的航向、姿态、速度及位置导航解。所以捷联惯导系统实质上是一种数字信号处理系统，系统的输入和输出是离散时间点上的数字信号。为了实现对陀螺和加速度计的输出信号在时间上的无遗漏采样和利用，系统采集的是在确定时间间隔内陀螺的角增量输出和加速度计的速度增量输出，而不是离散时间点上的角速度输出和比力输出。在工程上，常对挠性陀螺及加速度计再平衡回路中的力反馈电流作 I-F变换，转换成脉冲输出(激光陀螺输出本身就是脉冲) ，采用可逆计数器对脉冲在确定的时间段内作计数，所得脉冲计数乘以相应的脉冲当量即得角增量和速度增量。同时为了避免噪声放大效应，角增量和速度增量不应该也无必要折算成角速度和比力。

当运载体同时存在线运动和角运动时，采用速度增量解算速度时必须考虑速度的旋转效应和划桨效应补偿。本节将详细分析旋转效应和划桨效应的产生机理和相应的补偿算法。

## 9.6.1 速度计算中的旋转效应和划桨效应分析

取地理坐标系为导航坐标系，根据式(7. 4. 7) ，速度方程为

$$
\dot {\mathbf {V}} ^ {n} = \mathbf {C} _ {b} ^ {n} \mathbf {f} ^ {b} - (2 \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i e} ^ {n} + \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {e n} ^ {n}) \times \mathbf {V} ^ {n} + \mathbf {g} ^ {n}\tag{9.6.1}
$$

设速度的更新周期为 在每一个更新周期内对角增量和速度增量作 次采样。对上式作积分，得 $t _ { m }$ 时刻 运载体在导航坐标系内的速度=

$$
\mathbf {V} _ {m} ^ {n} = \mathbf {V} _ {m - 1} ^ {n} + \mathbf {C} _ {b (m - 1)} ^ {n (m - 1)} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} \mathbf {C} _ {b (t)} ^ {b (m - 1)} f ^ {b} d t + \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} \left[ \mathbf {g} ^ {n} - \left(2 \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i e} ^ {n} + \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {e n} ^ {n}\right) \times \mathbf {V} ^ {n} \right] d t\tag{9.6.2}
$$

式中 $, \pmb { V _ { m } }$ 和 $\mathbf { V } _ { m - 1 }$ 分别为 $t _ { m }$ 和 $t _ { m - 1 }$ 时刻运载体的速度; $\displaystyle C _ { b ( m - 1 ) } ^ { n ( m - 1 ) }$ 是 $t _ { m } .$ -l时刻的姿态矩阵，为书写方便，简记为 $C _ { m - 1 }$ ;将 $n ( m )$ 与 $n ( m { - } 1 )$ 视为重舍。

根据式(9.2.4纱，对于 $t _ { m - 1 } { \leqslant } t { \leqslant } t _ { m }$

$$
\boldsymbol {C} _ {b (t)} ^ {b (m - 1)} = \boldsymbol {I} + \frac {\sin \Phi}{\Phi} (\boldsymbol {\Phi} \times) + \frac {1 - \cos \Phi}{\Phi^ {2}} (\boldsymbol {\Phi} \times) (\boldsymbol {\Phi} \times)\tag{9.6.3}
$$

式中， $\pmb { \phi } _ { ; }$ 是 $b ( m { - } 1 )$ 坐标系至 $b ( t )$ 坐标系的等效旋转矢量， $\Phi { = } \left| \Phi \right| , \left( \Phi \times \right)$ 表示 $\Phi$ 的各分量构造成的叉乘斜对称矩阵。旋转矢量 $\Phi ,$ 应根据 &rtz 方程确定。但对于速度更新周期 $T = t _ { m } - t _ { m - 1 }$ 较短， $\varPhi$ 非常微小的情况，可以有如下近似

$$
\begin{array}{c} \frac {\sin \Phi}{\Phi} \approx 1 \\ \frac {1 - \cos \Phi}{\Phi^ {2}} \approx \frac {1}{2} \\ \boldsymbol {\Phi} \approx \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} \end{array}
$$

式中

$$
\Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} = \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t} \boldsymbol {\omega} (\tau) \mathrm{d} \tau \quad (t _ {m - 1} \leqslant t \leqslant t _ {m})\tag{9.6.4}
$$

并且 $( { \pmb { \phi } } \times ) ( { \pmb { \phi } } \times )$ 可视为二阶小量.这样式(9.6.3)可近似为

$$
\boldsymbol {C} _ {b (t)} ^ {b (m - 1)} \approx \boldsymbol {I} + (\Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} \times)\tag{9.6.5}
$$

将式(9. 6. 5)代人式(9.6. 刀，为书写简沽，略去表示投影坐标系的上标，得

$$
\mathbf {V} _ {m} = \mathbf {V} _ {m - 1} + \mathbf {C} _ {m - 1} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} (\boldsymbol {f} + \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} \times \boldsymbol {f}) \mathrm{d} t + \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} [ \mathbf {g} - (2 \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i e} + \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {e n}) \times \mathbf {V} _ {m - 1} ] \mathrm{d} t\tag{9.6.6}
$$

记

$$
\Delta \mathbf {V} _ {s f m} = \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} (\boldsymbol {f} + \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} \times \boldsymbol {f}) \mathrm{d} t\tag{9.6.7}
$$

$$
\Delta \mathbf {V} _ {\mathrm{g/corm}} = \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} \left[ \mathbf {g} - \left(2 \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i e} + \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {e n}\right) \times \mathbf {V} _ {m - 1} \right] \mathrm{d} t
$$

则式(9.6. 的可写成

(9. 6. 8)

$$
\mathbf {V} _ {m} = \mathbf {V} _ {m - 1} + \mathbf {C} _ {m - 1} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {s f m} + \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {g / \mathrm{corm}}\tag{9.6.9}
$$

式中 $, \mathbf { V } _ { m } .$ -1和 $C _ { m } .$ -1分别是前一速度更新时间点上的运载体速度和姿态阵 $\Delta { \mathbf V } _ { g / \mathrm { c o r } m }$ 是在 $\left[ t _ { m - 1 } , t _ { m } \right]$ 时间段内由有害加速度引起的速度补偿量 $\Delta V _ { s f m }$ 是由比力引起的速度补偿量，下面对此项作详细分析.

由式 (9. 6. 7)

$$
\Delta \mathbf {V} _ {s f m} = \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} + \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} (t) \times \boldsymbol {f} (t) d t\tag{9.6.10}
$$

式中

$$
\Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} = \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} \boldsymbol {f} (t) \mathrm{d} t\tag{9.6.11}
$$

是加速度计在 $\sum { t _ { m - 1 } } , t _ { m _ { - } }$ 时间段内输出的速度增量。记

$$
\Delta \mathbf {V} (t) = \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t} \mathbf {f} (\tau) \mathrm{d} \tau\tag{9.6.12}
$$

则由式 (9. 6. 4) 和式 (9.6.1纱，有

$$
\Delta \dot {\pmb {\theta}} (t) = \pmb {\omega} (t)\tag{9.6.13}
$$

$$
\Delta \dot {\boldsymbol {V}} (t) = \boldsymbol {f} (t)\tag{9.6.14}
$$

由于

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left[ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} (t) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} (t) \right] = \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} \times \Delta \dot {\mathbf {V}} (t) - \Delta \mathbf {V} (t) \times \Delta \dot {\boldsymbol {\theta}} (t)
$$

所以

$$
\begin{array}{l} \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} (t) \times \boldsymbol {f} (t) = \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} (t) \times \Delta \dot {\boldsymbol {V}} (t) = \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} [ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} (t) \times \Delta \boldsymbol {V} (t) ] + \Delta \boldsymbol {V} (t) \times \Delta \dot {\boldsymbol {\theta}} (t) \\ = \frac {1}{2} \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} [ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} (t) \times \Delta \boldsymbol {V} (t) ] + \frac {1}{2} [ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} (t) \times \Delta \dot {\boldsymbol {V}} (t) \\ - \Delta \boldsymbol {V} (t) \times \Delta \dot {\boldsymbol {\theta}} (t) ] + \Delta \boldsymbol {V} (t) \times \Delta \dot {\boldsymbol {\theta}} (t) \\ = \frac {1}{2} \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} [ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} (t) \times \Delta \boldsymbol {V} (t) ] + \frac {1}{2} [ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} (t)) + [ \Delta \boldsymbol {V} (t) ] + [ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} (t)) ] \end{array}\tag{9.6.15}
$$

将式 (9.6.15) 代人式 (9.6.10) 。并考虑到式 (9. 6. 4) 和式 (9.6.12) 所列关系=当 $t = t _ { m }$ 时，$\Delta \pmb { V } ( t _ { m } ) = \Delta \pmb { V } _ { m } , \Delta \pmb { \theta } ( t _ { m } ) = \Delta \pmb { \theta } _ { m }$ 仇，即为 $\left[ t _ { m - 1 } , t _ { m } \right]$ 时间段内的速度增量和角增量 $t = t _ { m - 1 }$ 时，$\Delta \mathbf { V } ( t _ { m - 1 } ) = \mathbf { 0 } , \Delta \pmb { \theta } ( t _ { m - 1 } ) = \mathbf { 0 }$ 。所以有

$$
\Delta \mathbf {V} _ {s f m} = \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} + \frac {1}{2} \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} \times \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} + \frac {1}{2} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} [ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} (t) \times \boldsymbol {f} (t) + \Delta \mathbf {V} (t) \times \boldsymbol {\omega} (t) ] d t\tag{9.6.16}
$$

(9. 6. 16)右端的第二项

$$
\Delta \mathbf {V} _ {\mathrm{rot} m} = \frac {1}{2} \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} \times \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m}\tag{9.6.17}
$$

称为速度的旋转效应补偿项，之所以如此称谓是由于它由运载体的线运动方向在空间旋转引起，(9. 6. 16)右端的第三项

$$
\Delta \mathbf {V} _ {\mathrm{scul} m} = \frac {1}{2} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} [ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} (t) \times \boldsymbol {f} (t) + \Delta \mathbf {V} (t) \times \boldsymbol {\omega} (t) ] \mathrm{d} t\tag{9.6.18}
$$

称为速度的划桨效应补偿项，之所以如此命名解释如下，

假设飞机存在同频且同相的角振动和线振动，沿机体 轴的角振动和 轴的线振动分别为

$$
\theta_ {x} (t) = A _ {\theta} \cos \Omega t, \quad P _ {y} (t) = A _ {P} \cos \Omega t
$$

则

$$
\begin{array}{l} \omega_ {x} (t) = \dot {\theta} _ {x} (t) = - A _ {\theta} \Omega \sin \Omega t \\ V _ {y} (t) = \dot {P} _ {y} (t) = - A _ {P} \Omega \sin \Omega t \\ f _ {y} (t) = \dot {V} _ {y} (t) = - A _ {P} \Omega^ {2} \cos \Omega t \\ \Delta \theta_ {x} (t) = \theta_ {x} (t) - \theta_ {x} (t _ {m - 1}) = A _ {\theta} (\cos \Omega t - \cos \Omega t _ {m - 1}) \\ \Delta V _ {y} (t) = V _ {y} (t) - V _ {y} (t _ {m - 1}) = A _ {P} \Omega (\sin \Omega t _ {m - 1} - \sin \Omega t) \end{array}
$$

根据式 (9. 6. 18)

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {\mathrm{scul}} (t _ {m}) & = \frac {1}{2} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} \left(\left[ \begin{array}{c c c} 0 & 0 & 0 \\ 0 & 0 & - A _ {\theta} (\cos \Omega t - \cos \Omega t _ {m - 1}) \\ 0 & A _ {\theta} (\cos \Omega t - \cos \Omega t _ {m - 1}) & 0 \end{array} \right] \left[ \begin{array}{c} 0 \\ - A _ {P} \Omega^ {2} \cos \Omega t \\ 0 \end{array} \right] + \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} 0 & 0 & - A _ {P} \Omega (\sin \Omega t - \sin \Omega t _ {m - 1}) \\ 0 & 0 & 0 \\ A _ {P} \Omega (\sin \Omega t - \sin \Omega t _ {m - 1}) & 0 & 0 \end{array} \right] \left[ \begin{array}{c} - A _ {\theta} \Omega \sin \Omega t \\ 0 \\ 0 \end{array} \right]\right) d t \\ & = \frac {1}{2} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} \left[ \begin{array}{c} 0 \\ 0 \\ - A _ {\theta} A _ {P} \Omega^ {2} + A _ {\theta} A _ {P} \Omega^ {2} \cos \Omega (t - t _ {m - 1}) \end{array} \right] d t = \left[ \begin{array}{c} 0 \\ 0 \\ - \frac {1}{2} A _ {\theta} A _ {P} \Omega^ {2} T + \frac {1}{2} A _ {\theta} A _ {P} \Omega \sin \Omega T \end{array} \right] \end{array}
$$

上式表明 当飞机沿纵轴做线振动的同时又沿横轴做同频同相的角振动时，在飞机的立轴方向存在速度整流量 $- \frac { 1 } { 2 } A _ { \theta } A _ { P } \Omega ^ { 2 } T + \frac { 1 } { 2 } A _ { \theta } A _ { P } \Omega \sin \Omega T$ 。飞机的上述线振动和角振动形式与划艇运动类一方面桨绕艇的侧向轴做周期摇动，另一方面艇沿纵轴做间歇性前进。所以将式(9. 6. 18)为划桨效应补偿项。此处补偿的含意是 按式(9. 6. 9)做速度更新时，由加速度引起的速度增量必须同时考虑由式(9.6.17)确定的旋转效应补偿项和由式(9. 6. 18)确定的划桨效应补偿项

$$
\Delta \mathbf {V} _ {s f m} = \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} + \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {\text { rot } m} + \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {\text { scul } m}\tag{9.6.19}
$$

否则速度计算将存在划桨误差和旋转误差。

如果飞机不存在线振动和角振动，假设只存在常值线加速度和常值角速度，则根据(9. 6. 4) 和式 (9. 6. 12)

$$
\Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} (t) = \left(t - t _ {m - 1}\right) \boldsymbol {\omega}, \Delta \mathbf {V} (t) = \left(t - t _ {m - 1}\right) \boldsymbol {f}
$$

代人式 (9. 6. 18) ，得

$$
\Delta \mathbf {V} _ {\text { scul }} (t) = \frac {1}{2} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t} \left[ \left(t - t _ {m - 1}\right) \boldsymbol {\omega} \times \boldsymbol {f} + \left(t - t _ {m - 1}\right) \boldsymbol {f} \times \boldsymbol {\omega} \right] \mathrm{d} t = \mathbf {0}
$$

即飞机不做线振动和角振动时，速度计算中的划桨效应补偿量为零。

## 9.6.2 划桨效应补偿算法

由于陀螺和加速度计输出的是角增量信号和速度增量信号，所以无法直接按式(9.6.18)算划桨效应补偿项。运载体的角速度和加速度的变化一「分复杂，因为运载体的运动状态往往带有任意性和随机性，所以不可能用某一确定的函数精确描述之，但可以用一简单曲线分段拟合近似描述之。而拟合曲线是可以用陀螺和加速度计测量的角增量和速度增量来表示的，一且拟合曲线确定，式(9. 6. 18)便可作解析求解。为便于说明，此处用直线拟合运载体的角速度和比力，从而推导出用角增量和速度增量表示的划桨效应补偿项求解公式。

设在 $\left[ t _ { m - 1 } , t _ { m } \right]$ ]时间段内，对运载体的角速度和比力采用线性函数作拟合

$$
\begin{array}{l} \boldsymbol {\omega} (t) = \boldsymbol {a} + 2 \boldsymbol {b} (t - t _ {m - 1}) \\ \boldsymbol {f} (t) = \boldsymbol {A} + 2 \boldsymbol {B} (t - t _ {m - 1}) \end{array}
$$

则对于 $t _ { m - 1 }$ $t { \leqslant } t _ { m }$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} (t) = \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t} \boldsymbol {\omega} (\tau) \mathrm{d} \tau = \boldsymbol {a} (t - t _ {m - 1}) + \boldsymbol {b} (t - t _ {m - 1}) ^ {2} \\ \Delta \boldsymbol {V} (t) = \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t} \boldsymbol {f} (\tau) \mathrm{d} \tau = \boldsymbol {A} (t - t _ {m - 1}) + \boldsymbol {B} (t - t _ {m - 1}) ^ {2} \end{array}
$$

在速度更新周期 $T = t _ { m } - t _ { m - 1 }$ 内，对角增量和速度增量采样两次，令 $\Delta T { = } \frac { T } { 2 }$ ，由于

$$
\begin{array}{c} \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} (t _ {m - 1} + \Delta T) = \Delta T \boldsymbol {a} + \Delta T ^ {2} \boldsymbol {b} \\ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} (t _ {m - 1} + 2 \Delta T) = 2 \Delta T \boldsymbol {a} + 4 \Delta T ^ {2} \boldsymbol {b} \end{array}
$$

所以在 $[ t _ { m - 1 } , t _ { m - 1 } + \Delta T ]$ 内的角增量采样值为

$$
\Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (1) = \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} \left(t _ {m - 1} + \Delta T\right) = \Delta T \boldsymbol {a} + \Delta T ^ {2} \boldsymbol {b}\tag{9.6.20}
$$

在 $[ t _ { m - 1 } + \Delta T , t _ { m }$ ]内的角增量采样值为

$$
\Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (2) = \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} \left(t _ {m - 1} + 2 \Delta T\right) - \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} \left(t _ {m - 1} + \Delta T\right) = \Delta T \boldsymbol {a} + 3 \Delta T ^ {2} \boldsymbol {b}\tag{9.6.21}
$$

同理

$$
\Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) = \Delta T \mathbf {A} + \Delta T ^ {2} \mathbf {B}\tag{9.6.22}
$$

$$
\Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) = \Delta T \mathbf {A} + 3 \Delta T ^ {2} \mathbf {B}\tag{9.6.23}
$$

分别联列求解式 (9.6.20) 与式 (9. 6. 1)，式 (9.6.22) 与式 (9. 6. 23) ，得

$$
\boldsymbol {a} = \frac {1}{2 \Delta T} \left[ 3 \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (1) - \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (2) \right]\tag{9.6.24a}
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {b} = \frac {1}{2 \Delta T ^ {2}} \left[ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (2) - \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (1) \right]\tag{9.6.24b}
$$

$$
\mathbf {A} = \frac {1}{2 \Delta T} \left[ 3 \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) - \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) \right]\tag{9.6.24c}
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {B} = \frac {1}{2 \Delta T ^ {2}} \left[ \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) - \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) \right]\tag{9.6.24d}
$$

所以

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\Delta \pmb {\theta} (t) \times \pmb {f} (t) + \Delta \pmb {V} (t) \times \pmb {\omega} (t)} \\ & {= [ \pmb {a} (t - t _ {m - 1}) + \pmb {b} (t - t _ {m - 1}) ^ {2} ] \times [ \pmb {A} + 2 \pmb {B} (t - t _ {m - 1}) ]} \\ & {\quad + [ \pmb {A} (t - t _ {m - 1}) + \pmb {B} (t - t _ {m - 1}) ^ {2} ] \times [ \pmb {a} + 2 \pmb {b} (t - t _ {m - 1}) ]} \\ & {= (t - t _ {m - 1}) ^ {2} (\pmb {a} \times \pmb {B} + \pmb {A} \times \pmb {b})} \end{array}
$$

$$
\Delta \mathbf {V} _ {\text {scul} m} = \frac {1}{2} (\boldsymbol {a} \times \boldsymbol {B} + \boldsymbol {A} \times \boldsymbol {b}) \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} (t - t _ {m - 1}) ^ {2} \mathrm{d} t = \frac {T ^ {3}}{6} (\boldsymbol {a} \times \boldsymbol {B} + \boldsymbol {A} \times \boldsymbol {b})
$$

将式(9.6.24)代人上式，并考虑到 $T { = } 2 \Delta T$ ,得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {\mathrm{scul} m} & = \frac {8 \Delta T ^ {3}}{6} \left\{\frac {1}{2 \Delta T} \left[ 3 \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (1) - \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (2) \right] \times \frac {1}{2 \Delta T ^ {2}} \left[ \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) - \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) \right] + \frac {1}{2 \Delta T} \left[ 3 \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) - \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) \right] \times \frac {1}{2 \Delta T ^ {2}} \left[ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (2) - \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (1) \right] \right\} \end{array}
$$

$$
= \frac {2}{3} \left[ \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) \times \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (2) - \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) \times \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (1) \right]\tag{9.6.25}
$$

上式中. $\Delta \pmb { \theta } _ { m } ( 1 )$ 和 $\Delta \pmb { \theta } _ { m } \left( 2 \right)$ 是 $[ t _ { m - 1 } , t _ { m } ]$ 内角增量的两个等间隔采样值 $\Delta V _ { m } ( 1 )$ )和 $\Delta V _ { m } \left( 2 \right)$ 是$\left[ t _ { m - 1 } , t _ { m } \right]$ 内速度增量的两个等间隔采样值，所以称该式为划桨效应补偿项的双子样算法。双子样算法是在对角速度和比力作直线拟合的条件下得出的，如果拟合时分别采用抛物线和兰次抛物线，则分别可得划桨效应补偿项的主子样算法和四子祥算法。三子样和四子样算法的推导思路与双子样算法的推导思路完全相同，只不过繁杂冗长些罢了，此处只给出相应算法，而不作推导。

划桨效应补偿项的三子样算法

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {\text {sculm}} & = k _ {1} \left[ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (1) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (3) + \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) \times \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (3) \right] + k _ {2} \left[ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (1) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) + \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (2) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (3) \right] \\ & + \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (2) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (3) + \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) \times \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (2) + \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) \times \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (3) ] \end{array}\tag{9.6.26}
$$

式中 $, k _ { 1 } = \frac { 3 3 } { 8 0 } , k _ { 2 } = \frac { 5 7 } { 8 0 } , \Delta \pmb { \theta } _ { m } \left( 1 \right) , \Delta \pmb { \theta } _ { m } \left( 2 \right) , \Delta \pmb { \theta } _ { m } \left( 3 \right)$ 是 $\left[ t _ { m - 1 } , t _ { m } \right]$ 内角增量的三个等间隔采样值，$\Delta \boldsymbol { V } _ { m } ( 1 ) \ldots \Delta \boldsymbol { V } _ { m } ( 2 ) \ldots \Delta \boldsymbol { V } _ { m } ( 3 )$ 是 $\left[ t _ { m - 1 } , t _ { m } \right]$ 内速度增量的三个等间隔采样值。

划桨效应补偿项的四子样算法

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {\mathrm{scul} m} & = k _ {1} \left[ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (1) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) + \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (3) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (4) \right. \\ & \quad + \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) \times \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (2) + \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (3) \times \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (4) ] \\ & \quad + k _ {2} \left[ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (1) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (3) + \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (2) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (4) \right. \\ & \quad + \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) \times \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (3) + \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) \times \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (4) ] \\ & \quad + k _ {3} \left[ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (1) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (4) + \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) \times \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (4) \right] \\ & \quad + k _ {4} \left[ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (2) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (3) + \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) \times \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (3) \right] \end{array}\tag{9.6.27}
$$

式中 $, k _ { 1 } = \frac { 7 3 6 } { 9 4 5 } , k _ { 2 } = \frac { 3 3 4 } { 9 4 5 } , k _ { 3 } = \frac { 5 2 6 } { 9 4 5 } , k _ { 4 } = \frac { 6 5 4 } { 9 4 5 }$ '各增量的说明同上。

从上述诸式可归纳出划桨效应补偿项的一般算法:

$$
\Delta \mathbf {V} _ {\text { scul } m} = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {N - 1} \sum_ {j = i + 1} ^ {N} k _ {i j} \left[ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (i) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (j) + \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (i) \times \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (j) \right]\tag{9.6.28}
$$

式中 .N为角增量和速度增量的子样数。

## 9.6.3 tJ 桨效应补偿项的优化算法

(9. 6.26)和式(9.6.27)所给出的三子祥和四子样划桨效应补偿算法中，系数是在角速度和比力拟合成抛物线和三次抛物线的条件下获得的，而实际的角速度和比力与抛物线和三次抛物线相差甚远，所以这些系数并不能保证算法误差达到最小。与圆锥运动条件下推导旋转矢量优化算法的思路类似，此处以划桨运动为环境条件，推导出划桨效应补偿项的优化算法。

假设运载体做划桨运动，角速度和比力分别为

$$
\boldsymbol {\omega} (t) = i B \Omega \cos \Omega t\tag{9.6.29a}
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {f} (t) = \boldsymbol {j} C \sin \Omega t\tag{9.6.29b}
$$

式中.i 为沿机体坐标系相应轴的单位向量。

记ð $\Delta V _ { m } ( k )$ 〉和ð $\Delta \pmb { \theta } _ { m } ( k { + } l )$ 是速度更新周期 $\left[ t _ { m - 1 } , t _ { m _ { - } } \right]$ 内的第 个速度增量采样值和第 $k + l$ 个角增量采样值，则

$$
\Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (k) = \int_ {t _ {m - 1} + (k - 1) \Delta T} ^ {t _ {m - 1} + k \Delta T} \boldsymbol {f} (t) \mathrm{d} t = \boldsymbol {j} \frac {C}{\Omega} \left\{\cos \Omega \left[ t _ {m - 1} + (k - 1) \Delta T \right] - \cos \Omega \left(t _ {m - 1} + k \Delta T\right) \right\}
$$

为书写方便，记

$$
\tau_ {k - 1} = t _ {m - 1} + (k - 1) \Delta T, \quad \lambda = \Omega \Delta T, \quad \alpha = \sin \lambda , \quad \beta = 1 - \cos \lambda
$$

则

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (k) & = \mathbf {j} \frac {C}{\Omega} [ \cos \Omega \tau_ {k - 1} - \cos (\Omega \tau_ {k - 1} + \lambda) ] = \mathbf {j} \frac {C}{\Omega} (\alpha \sin \Omega \tau_ {k - 1} + \beta \cos \Omega \tau_ {k - 1}) \\ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (k + l) & = \mathbf {i} \int_ {t _ {m - 1} + (k + l - 1) \Delta T} ^ {t _ {m - 1} + (k + l) \Delta T} \boldsymbol {\omega} (t) d t \\ & = \mathbf {i} B \left\{\sin \Omega [ t _ {m - 1} + (k + l) \Delta T ] - \sin \Omega [ t _ {m - 1} + (k + l - 1) \Delta T ] \right\} \\ & = \mathbf {i} B \left\{\sin [ \Omega \tau_ {k - 1} + l \lambda + \lambda ] - \sin (\Omega \tau_ {k - 1} + l \lambda) \right\} \\ & = \mathbf {i} B [ \alpha \cos (\Omega \tau_ {k - 1} + l \lambda) - \beta \sin (\Omega \tau_ {k - 1} + l \lambda) ] \\ & = \mathbf {i} B [ \cos \Omega \tau_ {k - 1} (\alpha \cos l \lambda - \beta \sin l \lambda) - \sin \Omega \tau_ {k - 1} (\alpha \sin l \lambda + \beta \cos l \lambda) ] \end{array}
$$

所以

$$
\begin{array}{l} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (k) \times \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (k + l) \\ = - k \frac {B C}{\Omega} (\alpha \sin \Omega \tau_ {k - 1} + \beta \cos \Omega \tau_ {k - 1}) [ \cos \Omega \tau_ {k - 1} (\alpha \cos l \lambda - \beta \sin l \lambda) - \sin \Omega \tau_ {k - 1} (\alpha \sin l \lambda + \beta \cos l \lambda) ] \end{array}
$$

仅考虑上式中的直流分量

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \left[ \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (k) \times \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (k + l) \right] _ {\text {rect}} & = - k \frac {B C}{2 \Omega} \left[ \beta (\alpha \cos l \lambda - \beta \sin l \lambda) - \alpha (\alpha \sin l \lambda + \beta \cos l \lambda) \right] \\ & = k \frac {B C}{2 \Omega} \left(\alpha^ {2} + \beta^ {2}\right) \sin l \lambda \\ & = k \frac {B C}{2 \Omega} \left[ \sin^ {2} \lambda + (1 - \cos \lambda) ^ {2} \right] \sin l \lambda \\ & = k \frac {B C}{\Omega} (\sin l \lambda - \sin l \lambda \cos \lambda) \\ & = k \frac {B C}{\Omega} \left[ \sin l \lambda - \frac {1}{2} \sin (l + 1) \lambda - \frac {1}{2} \sin (l - 1) \lambda \right] \end{array} \tag {1}\tag{9.6.30}
$$

再考虑另一种情况

$$
\begin{array}{l} \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (k) = \int_ {t _ {m - 1} + (k - 1) \Delta T} ^ {t _ {m - 1} + k \Delta T} \boldsymbol {\omega} (t) \mathrm{d} t = i B [ \sin (\Omega \tau_ {k - 1} + \lambda) - \sin \Omega \tau_ {k - 1} ] = i B (\alpha \cos \Omega \tau_ {k - 1} - \beta \sin \Omega \tau_ {k - 1}) \\ \Delta V _ {m} (k + l) = \int_ {t _ {m - 1} + (k + l - 1) \Delta T} ^ {t _ {m - 1} + (k + l) \Delta T} f (t) \mathrm{d} t \\ = j \frac {C}{\Omega} [ \cos (\Omega \tau_ {k - 1} + l \lambda) - \cos (\Omega \tau_ {k - 1} + l \lambda + \lambda) ] \\ = j \frac {C}{\Omega} [ \alpha \sin (\Omega \tau_ {k - 1} + l \lambda) + \beta \cos (\Omega \tau_ {k - 1} + l \lambda) ] \\ = j \frac {C}{\Omega} [ \cos \Omega \tau_ {k - 1} (\alpha \sin l \lambda + \beta \cos l \lambda) + \sin \Omega \tau_ {k - 1} (\alpha \cos l \lambda - \beta \sin l \lambda) ] \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (k) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (k + l) & = k \frac {B C}{\Omega} (\alpha \cos \Omega \tau_ {k - 1} - \beta \sin \Omega \tau_ {k - 1}) \\ & \quad \times [ \cos \Omega \tau_ {k - 1} (\alpha \sin l \lambda + \beta \cos l \lambda) + \sin \Omega \tau_ {k - 1} (\alpha \cos l \lambda - \beta \sin l \lambda) ] \end{array}
$$

仅考虑上式中的直流分量

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left[ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (k) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (k + l) \right] _ {\text {rect}} = k \frac {B C}{2 \Omega} \left[ \alpha (\alpha \sin l \lambda + \beta \cos l \lambda) - \beta (\alpha \cos l \lambda - \beta \sin l \lambda) \right] \\ = k \frac {B C}{2 \Omega} (\alpha^ {2} + \beta^ {2}) \sin l \lambda \\ = k \frac {B C}{\Omega} \left[ \sin l \lambda - \frac {1}{2} \sin (l + 1) \lambda - \frac {1}{2} \sin (l - 1) \lambda \right] \end{array}\tag{9.6.31}
$$

比较式 (9.6.30) 和式 (9.6.3 1) .得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left[ \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (k) \times \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (k + l) \right] _ {\text {rect}} = \left[ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (k) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (k + l) \right] _ {\text {rect}} \\ = k \frac {B C}{\Omega} \left[ \sin l \lambda - \frac {1}{2} \sin (l + 1) \lambda - \frac {1}{2} \sin (l - 1) \lambda \right] \end{array}\tag{9.6.32}
$$

有了式(9.6.32)所述关系，可以方便地确定出划桨效应补偿项中的最佳系数。此处以三子样算法为例说明之。

由式(9. 6. 26). 桨效应补偿项的三子样算法为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \Delta \hat {\mathbf {V}} _ {\mathrm{scul} m} & = k _ {1} \left[ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (1) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (3) + \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) \times \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (3) \right] + k _ {2} \left[ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (1) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) + \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (2) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (3) + \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (2) \times \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (3) \right] \end{array}
$$

式中. $k _ { 1 }$ 和 $k _ { 2 }$ 是需要确定的最优系数。

根据式 (9. 6. 32).上式可写成

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \Delta \hat {\boldsymbol {V}} _ {\mathrm{sculm}} & = \boldsymbol {k} \frac {B C}{\Omega} \left[ k _ {1} \left(\sin 2 \lambda - \frac {1}{2} \sin 3 \lambda - \frac {1}{2} \sin \lambda\right) \cdot 2 + k _ {2} \left(\sin \lambda - \frac {1}{2} \sin 2 \lambda\right) \cdot 4 \right] \\ & = \boldsymbol {k} \frac {B C}{\Omega} \left[ \sin \lambda (4 k _ {2} - k _ {1}) + \sin 2 \lambda (2 k _ {1} - 2 k _ {2}) - k _ {1} \sin 3 \lambda \right] \end{array}
$$

而根据式(9. 6. 29)所示划桨运动，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} (t) = \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t} \boldsymbol {\omega} (\tau) \mathrm{d} \tau = i B (\sin \Omega t - \sin \Omega t _ {m - 1}) \\ \Delta \mathbf {V} (t) = \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t} \boldsymbol {f} (\tau) \mathrm{d} \tau = - j \frac {C}{\Omega} (\cos \Omega t - \cos \Omega t _ {m - 1}) \end{array}
$$

将上述两式和式(9.6.29)代人式(9.6.18) .得在划桨运动条件下划桨效应补偿项的精确计算值为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {\mathrm{sculm}} & = \frac {1}{2} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} [ i B (\sin \Omega t - \sin \Omega t _ {m - 1}) \times j C \sin \Omega t - j \frac {C}{\Omega} (\cos \Omega t - \cos \Omega t _ {m - 1}) \times i B \Omega \cos \Omega t ] \mathrm{d} t \\ & = k \frac {B C}{2} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} [ (\sin \Omega t - \sin \Omega t _ {m - 1}) \sin \Omega t + \cos \Omega t (\cos \Omega t - \cos \Omega t _ {m - 1}) ] \mathrm{d} t \\ & = k \frac {B C}{2} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} [ 1 - \cos \Omega (t - t _ {m - 1}) ] \mathrm{d} t = k \frac {B C}{2} (T - \frac {1}{\Omega} \sin \Omega T) \end{array}
$$

对于三子样算法 $T { = } 3 \Delta T$ 所以

$$
\Delta \mathbf {V} _ {\mathrm{scul} m} = \boldsymbol {k} \frac {B C}{2} \left(3 \Delta T - \frac {1}{\Omega} \sin 3 \Omega \Delta T\right) = \boldsymbol {k} \frac {B C}{2 \Omega} (3 \lambda - \sin 3 \lambda)
$$

因此三子样引起的算法误差为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \Delta \widetilde {\boldsymbol {V}} _ {\mathrm{scul} m} & = \Delta \dot {\boldsymbol {V}} _ {\mathrm{scul} m} - \Delta \boldsymbol {V} _ {\mathrm{scul} m} \\ & = \boldsymbol {k} \frac {B C}{\Omega} \left[ 2 k _ {1} \left(\sin 2 \lambda - \frac {1}{2} \sin 3 \lambda - \frac {1}{2} \sin \lambda\right) + 4 k _ {2} \left(\sin \lambda - \frac {1}{2} \sin 2 \lambda\right) - \left(\frac {3}{2} \lambda - \frac {1}{2} \sin 3 \lambda\right) \right] \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = k \frac {B C}{\Omega} \left[ - \frac {3}{2} \lambda + (4 k _ {2} - k _ {1}) \sin \lambda + (2 k _ {1} - 2 k _ {2}) \sin 2 \lambda + \left(\frac {1}{2} - k _ {1}\right) \sin 3 \lambda \right] \\ = k \frac {B C}{\Omega} \left\{\lambda \left[ - \frac {3}{2} + 4 k _ {2} - k _ {1} + (2 k _ {1} - 2 k _ {2}) \cdot 2 + \left(\frac {1}{2} - k _ {1}\right) \cdot 3 \right] \right. \\ \left. + \frac {\lambda^ {3}}{3 !} \left[ - (4 k _ {2} - k _ {1}) - (2 k _ {1} - 2 k _ {2}) \cdot 2 ^ {3} - \left(\frac {1}{2} - k _ {1}\right) \cdot 3 ^ {3} \right] \right. \\ \left. + \frac {\lambda^ {5}}{5 !} \left[ (4 k _ {2} - k _ {1}) + (2 k _ {1} - 2 k _ {2}) \cdot 2 ^ {5} + \left(\frac {1}{2} - k _ {1}\right) \cdot 3 ^ {5} \right] + \dots \right\} \\ = k \frac {B C}{\Omega} \left[ - \frac {\lambda^ {3}}{3 !} \left(1 2 k _ {1} + 1 2 k _ {2} - \frac {2 7}{2}\right) + \frac {\lambda^ {5}}{5 !} \left(- 1 8 0 k _ {1} - 6 0 k _ {2} + \frac {2 4 3}{2}\right) + \dots \right] \end{array}
$$

由于 $\lambda { = } \Omega \cdot \Delta T$ 运载体振动为机械振动，频率并不高 $\Delta T$ 为增量子样的采样周期，量级为毫秒级，所以一般情况下是满足 $\lambda { < } 1$ 的条件的，可见 的幕次越低对误差的影响越大，因此选择系数$k _ { 1 }$ 和 $k _ { 2 }$ 时应使三次幕及五次寨的系数为零，即有

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} 1 2 k _ {1} + 1 2 k _ {2} - \frac {2 7}{2} = 0 \\ - 1 8 0 k _ {1} - 6 0 k _ {2} + \frac {2 4 3}{2} = 0 \end{array} \right.
$$

联立求解上述方程组，得

$$
k _ {1} = \frac {9}{2 0}, k _ {2} = \frac {2 7}{4 0}
$$

此即为式(9. 6. 26)三子样算法中的优化系数，采用此系数将使三子样算法产生的划桨误差达到最小。

采用相同的分析方法，可得双子样和四子样算法的优化系数。现将双子祥、三子样、四子样算法的优化系一并列出如下

双子样算法 $k _ { 1 } = \frac { 2 } { 3 }$

兰子样算法 : $\displaystyle { \frac { \ d u } { \ d e ^ { 2 } } , k _ { 2 } = \frac { 9 } { 4 0 } , }$ ,

四子样算法 : $k _ { 1 } = \frac { 5 4 } { 1 0 5 } , k _ { 2 } = \frac { 4 6 } { 1 0 5 } , k _ { 3 } = \frac { 2 1 4 } { 3 1 5 } \circ$

## 9. 7 捷联惯导系统的位置算法

惯性器件均以增量形式输出测量信息，在这种情况下，速度和姿态只在各自的更新时间点上才有输出，所以位置解也只能根据离散时间点上的速度和角增量和速度增量求取。而运载体的运动是复杂空间角运动和线运动的复合，在采用增量形式求解差分方程确定位置解时，必须对角运动和线运动的糯合作补偿.本节介绍惯性器件的输出是角增量和速度增量情况下的位置解算算法，位置解算中的旋转效应及涡卷效应分析，以及相应的补偿算法和补偿算法的优化。

## 9.7.1 位置矩阵的确定

对于工作在非极区的捷联惯导，导航坐标系一般选用地理坐标系，相应的位置速率为

$$
\boldsymbol {\omega} _ {e n} ^ {n} = \left[ \begin{array}{c} - \frac {V _ {N}}{R _ {M} + h} \\ \frac {V _ {E}}{R _ {N} + h} \\ \frac {V _ {E}}{R _ {N} + h} \tan L \end{array} \right] = \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} 0 & - \frac {1}{R _ {M} + h} & 0 \\ \frac {1}{R _ {N} + h} & 0 & 0 \\ \frac {\tan L}{R _ {N} + h} & 0 & 0 \end{array} \right] \left[ \begin{array}{l} V _ {E} \\ V _ {N} \\ V _ {U} \end{array} \right] = \boldsymbol {F} (t) \boldsymbol {V} ^ {n} (t)\tag{9.7.1}
$$

式中

$$
\boldsymbol {F} (t) = \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} 0 & - \frac {1}{R _ {M} + h} & 0 \\ \frac {1}{R _ {N} + h} & 0 & 0 \\ \frac {\tan L}{R _ {N} + h} & 0 & 0 \end{array} \right]\tag{9.7.2}
$$

$V _ { E } , V _ { N } , V _ { U }$ 分别为运载体沿东、北、天方向的速度 $R _ { M }$ 和 $R _ { N }$ 分别为运载体所在点子午圈和gp圈的曲率半径.L 为运载体所在点的纬度.h 为运载体所在点的高度。

设位置更新周期为 $T _ { l } { = } t _ { l } { - } t _ { l - 1 } , n ( l )$ 和 $n ( l - 1 )$ 分别为 $t _ { l }$ 时刻和 $t _ { l - 1 }$ 时刻的导航坐标系。由于位置速率 ${ \bf \delta } _ { : { \pmb { \omega } } _ { e n } }$ 十分微小，而且由于 $T _ { \mathit { l } }$ 很小，在 $\sum { t _ { l - 1 } } , { t _ { l } } _ { - }$ 时间段内运载体的速度可近似看作不变，所以 $n ( l { - } 1 )$ 坐标系至 $n ( l )$ 坐标系的旋转矢量可写成

$$
\boldsymbol {\xi} _ {l} \approx \int_ {t _ {l - 1}} ^ {t _ {l}} \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {e n} ^ {n} (t) \mathrm{d} t \approx \hat {\boldsymbol {F}} _ {(l - 1, l) / 2} \Delta \boldsymbol {R} _ {l} ^ {n}\tag{9.7.3}
$$

其中，在计算 $\hat { F } _ { ( l - 1 , l ) / 2 }$ 时，所用纬度为

$$
\hat {L} _ {(l - 1, l) / 2} = \hat {L} _ {l - 1} + \frac {1}{2} (\hat {L} _ {l - 1} - \hat {L} _ {l - 2}) = \frac {3}{2} \hat {L} _ {l - 1} - \frac {1}{2} \hat {L} _ {l - 2}
$$

即为在 $t { = } \frac { 1 } { 2 } ( t _ { l - 1 } { + } t _ { l } )$ 司点上纬度的线性外推值

$$
\Delta \boldsymbol {R} _ {l} ^ {n} = \int_ {t _ {l - 1}} ^ {t _ {l}} \boldsymbol {V} ^ {n} (t) \mathrm{d} t
$$

设 ${ \cal T } _ { l } { = } M { \cal T } _ { m }$ ，即经过 次速度更新后作一次位置更新，则

$$
\Delta \boldsymbol {R} _ {l} ^ {n} = \sum_ {m = 1} ^ {M} \Delta \boldsymbol {R} _ {m} ^ {n}\tag{9.7.4}
$$

式中

$$
\boldsymbol {R} _ {m} ^ {n} = \int_ {t _ {l - 1} + (m - 1) T _ {m}} ^ {t _ {l - 1} + m T _ {m}} \boldsymbol {V} ^ {n} (t) \mathrm{d} t
$$

记 $t _ { m - 1 } = t _ { l - 1 } + ( m - 1 ) T _ { m } , t _ { m } = t _ { l - 1 } + m T _ { m }$ ，则

$$
\Delta \mathbf {R} _ {m} ^ {n} = \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} \mathbf {V} ^ {n} (t) \mathrm{d} t
$$

若已求得 $\pmb { \xi } _ { l }$ ，.则根据式 (9.2.42)

$$
\boldsymbol {C} _ {n (l)} ^ {n (l - 1)} = \boldsymbol {I} + \frac {\sin \xi_ {l}}{\xi_ {l}} (\boldsymbol {\xi} _ {l} \times) + \frac {1 - \cos \xi_ {l}}{\xi_ {l} ^ {2}} (\boldsymbol {\xi} _ {l} \times) ^ {2}\tag{9.7.5}
$$

由于 $\pmb { \xi } _ { l } = | \pmb { \xi } _ { l } |$ 很小，所以上式可取一阶近似

亦即

$$
\boldsymbol {C} _ {n (l)} ^ {n (l - 1)} = \boldsymbol {I} + (\boldsymbol {\xi} _ {l} \times)\tag{9.7.6a}
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {C} _ {n (l - 1)} ^ {n (l)} = \boldsymbol {I} - (\boldsymbol {\xi} _ {l} \times)\tag{9.7.6b}
$$

上述诸式中，(I $( \pmb { \xi } _ { l } \times )$ 是由 $\pmb { \xi } _ { l }$ ;，的各分量构成的向量叉乘斜对称矩阵，即

$$
(\boldsymbol {\xi} _ {l} \times) = \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} 0 & - \boldsymbol {\xi} _ {l z} & \boldsymbol {\xi} _ {l y} \\ \boldsymbol {\xi} _ {l z} & 0 & - \boldsymbol {\xi} _ {l x} \\ - \boldsymbol {\xi} _ {l y} & \boldsymbol {\xi} _ {l x} & 0 \end{array} \right]\tag{9.7.7}
$$

$t _ { l }$ 时刻的位置矩阵可按下式确定=

$$
\mathbf {C} _ {e} ^ {n (l)} = \mathbf {C} _ {n (l - 1)} ^ {n (l)} \mathbf {C} _ {e} ^ {n (l - 1)}\tag{9.7.8}
$$

式中 $C _ { e } ^ { n ( l - 1 ) }$ )为上一位置更新时间点上确定的位置矩阵。

根据式(8. 1. 10) ，经度和纬度与位置矩阵间存在如下关系

$$
\boldsymbol {C} _ {e} ^ {n} = \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} - \sin \lambda & \cos \lambda & 0 \\ - \sin L \cos \lambda & - \sin L \sin \lambda & \cos L \\ \cos L \cos \lambda & \cos L \sin \lambda & \sin L \end{array} \right]
$$

所以，如果已求得位置矩阵

$$
\boldsymbol {C} _ {e} ^ {n} = \left[ \begin{array}{l l l} P _ {1 1} & P _ {1 2} & P _ {1 3} \\ P _ {2 1} & P _ {2 2} & P _ {2 3} \\ P _ {3 1} & P _ {3 2} & P _ {3 3} \end{array} \right]
$$

则

$$
L = \arcsin P _ {3 3}\tag{9.7.9}
$$

$$
\lambda_ {\text {主}} = \arctan \frac {P _ {3 2}}{P _ {3 1}}\tag{9.7.10}
$$

的真值根据表 9.7.1 判别求取。

高度求取如下

$$
h _ {l} = h _ {l - 1} + \Delta R _ {z l} ^ {n}\tag{9.7.11}
$$

式中 ， $\Delta R _ { z l } ^ { n }$ 是 $\Delta { \cal R } _ { l } ^ { n }$ 沿导航坐标系 轴的分量。

上述分析说明，只要确定出t. $\Delta { \pmb R } _ { \mathit { m } } ^ { \textit { n } } ( \boldsymbol { m } { = } 1 , 2 , \cdots , M )$ )，就可以确定出运载体在 $t _ { l }$ 时刻的高度和位置矩阵，从位置矩阵又可确定出经度和纬度。

9.7.1 经度真值的求职

<table><tr><td> $P_{31}$  λ主</td><td>-</td><td>+</td></tr><tr><td>-</td><td> $\lambda=\lambda_{\text{主}}+180^{\circ}$ </td><td> $\lambda=\lambda_{\text{主}}-180^{\circ}$ </td></tr><tr><td>+</td><td colspan="2"> $\lambda=\lambda_{\text{主}}$ </td></tr></table>

## 9.7.2 位置解算中的旋转效应和涡卷效应分析

$\Delta { \pmb R } _ { m } ^ { n }$ R:;，可采用梯形积分近似确定

$$
\Delta \mathbf {R} _ {m} ^ {n} = \frac {T _ {m}}{2} (\mathbf {V} _ {m} ^ {n} + \mathbf {V} _ {m - 1} ^ {n})\tag{9.7.12}
$$

式中，v $, \pmb { V } _ { m } ^ { n }$ ，和 $\mathbf { V } _ { m - 1 } ^ { n }$ 分别是 $t _ { m }$ 和 $t _ { m - 1 }$ 时刻运载体的速度，由速度更新算法确定。但梯形积分引起的误差太大，只能在精度要求不高的场合使用，对于精度要求高的使用场合，应该采用高分辨率算法。

由于[ $\sum t _ { m - 1 } , t _ { m _ { - } }$ J时间段很短，重力加速度和有害加速度补偿项在该时间段内变化十分缓慢，

可近似看作常值，所以其积分值可近似看作时间的线性函数，根据式(9. 6. 9)

$$
\mathbf {V} ^ {n} (t) = \mathbf {V} _ {m - 1} ^ {n} + \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {s f} ^ {n} (t) + (\Delta \mathbf {V} _ {g m} ^ {n} + \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {\text {corm}} ^ {n}) \frac {t - t _ {m - 1}}{T _ {m}} \quad (t _ {m - 1} \leqslant t \leqslant t _ {m})\tag{9.7.13}
$$

式中

$$
\Delta \mathbf {V} _ {s f} ^ {n} (t) = \int_ {t _ {n r - 1}} ^ {t} \mathbf {C} _ {b (\tau)} ^ {n (l - 1)} \mathbf {f} ^ {b} (\tau) \mathrm{d} \tau\tag{9.7.14}
$$

对式 (9. 7. 13)两边在 $[ \boldsymbol { t } _ { m - 1 } , \boldsymbol { t } _ { m }$ J时间段内积分，得

$$
\Delta \mathbf {R} _ {m} ^ {n} = \left[ \mathbf {V} _ {m - 1} ^ {n} + \frac {1}{2} \left(\Delta \mathbf {V} _ {g m} ^ {n} + \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {\text {corm}} ^ {n}\right) \right] T _ {m} + \Delta \mathbf {R} _ {s f m} ^ {n}\tag{9.7.15}
$$

式中

$$
\Delta \boldsymbol {R} _ {s f m} ^ {n} = \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} \Delta \boldsymbol {V} _ {s f} ^ {n} (t) d t\tag{9.7.16}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {s f} ^ {n} (t) = \mathbf {C} _ {n (l - 1)} ^ {n (t)} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {s f} ^ {n (l - 1)} (t) = \mathbf {C} _ {n (m - 1)} ^ {n (t)} \mathbf {C} _ {n (l - 1)} ^ {n (m - 1)} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {s f} ^ {n (l - 1)} (t) \\ = (\mathbf {C} _ {n (m - 1)} ^ {n (t)} - \mathbf {I}) \mathbf {C} _ {n (l - 1)} ^ {n (m - 1)} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {s f} ^ {n (l - 1)} (t) + \mathbf {C} _ {n (l - 1)} ^ {n (m - 1)} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {s f} ^ {n (l - 1)} (t) \end{array}\tag{9.7.17}
$$

设在 $\sum { t _ { m - 1 } } , t _ { m _ { - } }$ 时间段内，导航坐标系的旋转矢量为 $\xi _ { { \scriptscriptstyle m } }$ ，加速度计的速度增量为 $\Delta V _ { m } ^ { b } , t _ { m }$ 时刻的姿态阵为 $C _ { b ( m ) } ^ { n ( l - 1 ) }$ U

$$
\Delta \mathbf {V} _ {s f m} ^ {n (l - 1)} = \mathbf {C} _ {b (m)} ^ {n (l - 1)} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} ^ {b}
$$

而速度更新周期 ${ T _ { m } } = { t _ { m } } - { t _ { m - 1 } }$ 很短，在该时刻段内导航坐标系的旋转可近似看作匀速的，加速度也可近似看作不变，所以对 $t _ { m - 1 } { \leqslant } t { \leqslant } t _ { m }$

$$
\boldsymbol {\xi} (t) = \frac {t - t _ {m - 1}}{T _ {m}} \boldsymbol {\xi} _ {m}\tag{9.7.18}
$$

$$
\Delta \mathbf {V} _ {s f} ^ {n (l - 1)} (t) = \frac {t - t _ {m - 1}}{T _ {m}} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {s f m} ^ {n (l - 1)}\tag{9.7.19}
$$

由式 (9.7.18)

$$
\boldsymbol {C} _ {n (m - 1)} ^ {n (t)} - \boldsymbol {I} = \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} 0 & \xi_ {m z} & - \xi_ {m y} \\ - \xi_ {m z} & 0 & \xi_ {m x} \\ \xi_ {m y} & - \xi_ {m x} & 0 \end{array} \right] \cdot \frac {t - t _ {n - 1}}{T _ {m}} = \frac {t - t _ {n - 1}}{T _ {m}} [ \boldsymbol {C} _ {n (m - 1)} ^ {n (m)} - \boldsymbol {I} ]\tag{9.7.20}
$$

将式 (9.7.18) 、式 (9.7.19) 和式 (9.7.20) 代人式 (9.7.17)

$$
\begin{array}{l} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {s f} ^ {n} (t) = [ \mathbf {C} _ {n (m - 1)} ^ {n (m)} - \mathbf {I} ] \mathbf {C} _ {n (l - 1)} ^ {n (m - 1)} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {s f m} ^ {n (l - 1)} \frac {(t - t _ {m - 1}) ^ {2}}{T _ {m} ^ {2}} + \mathbf {C} _ {n (l - 1)} ^ {n (m - 1)} \mathbf {C} _ {b (m - 1)} ^ {n (l - 1)} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {s f} ^ {b (m - 1)} (t) \\ = [ \mathbf {C} _ {n (l - 1)} ^ {n (m)} - \mathbf {C} _ {n (l - 1)} ^ {n (m - 1)} ] \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {s f m} ^ {n (l - 1)} \frac {(t - t _ {m - 1}) ^ {2}}{T _ {m} ^ {2}} + \mathbf {C} _ {n (l - 1)} ^ {n (m - 1)} \mathbf {C} _ {b (m - 1)} ^ {n (l - 1)}   \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {s f} ^ {b (m - 1)} (t) \end{array}\tag{9.7.21}
$$

根据式 (9. 6. 16)

$$
\Delta \mathbf {V} _ {s f} ^ {b (m - 1)} (t) = \Delta \mathbf {V} ^ {b} (t) + \frac {1}{2} \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} ^ {b} (t) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} ^ {b} (t) + \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {\mathrm{scul}} ^ {b} (t)\tag{9.7.22}
$$

式中，划桨补偿量为

$$
\Delta \mathbf {V} _ {\text { scul }} ^ {b} (t) = \frac {1}{2} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t} \left[ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} ^ {b} (\tau) \times \boldsymbol {f} ^ {b} (\tau) + \Delta \mathbf {V} ^ {b} (\tau) \times \boldsymbol {\omega} ^ {b} (\tau) \right] d \tau\tag{9.7.23}
$$

$\Delta \pmb { \theta } ^ { b } \left( \tau \right)$ 和 $\Delta { \bf V } ^ { b } \left( \tau \right)$ 分别为 $\left[ t _ { m - 1 } , \tau \right]$ 时间段内陀螺的角增量输出和加速度计的速度增量输出。根据式 (9. 7. 6b)

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \boldsymbol {C} _ {n (l - 1)} ^ {n (m)} - \boldsymbol {C} _ {n (l - 1)} ^ {n (m - 1)} & = \boldsymbol {I} - (\boldsymbol {\xi} _ {l - 1, m} \times) - [ \boldsymbol {I} - (\boldsymbol {\xi} _ {l - 1, m - 1} \times) ] \\ & = [ (\boldsymbol {\xi} _ {l - 1, m - 1} - \boldsymbol {\xi} _ {l - 1, m}) \times ] \end{array}\tag{9.7.24}
$$

将式 (9.7.24)代人式 (9.7.2 1)后积分，得

$$
\Delta \boldsymbol {R} _ {s f m} ^ {n} = \frac {T _ {m}}{3} \left[ \left(\boldsymbol {\xi} _ {l - 1, m - 1} - \boldsymbol {\xi} _ {l - 1, m}\right) \times \right] \Delta \boldsymbol {V} _ {s f m} ^ {n (l - 1)} + \boldsymbol {C} _ {n (l - 1)} ^ {n (m - 1)} \boldsymbol {C} _ {b (m - 1)} ^ {n (l - 1)} \Delta \boldsymbol {R} _ {s f m} ^ {b (m - 1)}
$$

式中 $\pmb { \xi } _ { l - 1 , m - 1 }$ 和 ${ \pmb { \xi } } _ { l - 1 , m }$ 为导航坐标系分别在 $[ t _ { l - 1 } , t _ { m - 1 } ]$ 和 $[ t _ { l - 1 } , t _ { m } ]$ 时间段内的旋转矢量，按式 (9.7.3)

$$
\boldsymbol {\xi} _ {l - 1, m - 1} \approx \hat {\boldsymbol {F}} _ {(l - 1, m - 1) / 2} \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {m - 1} \Delta \boldsymbol {R} _ {j}
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {\xi} _ {l - 1, m} \approx \hat {\boldsymbol {F}} _ {(l - 1, m) / 2} \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {m} \Delta \boldsymbol {R} _ {j}
$$

计算中所用的纬度为

$$
\hat {L} _ {(l - 1, m - 1) / 2} = \hat {L} _ {l - 1} + \frac {t _ {m - 1} - t _ {l - 1}}{2 T _ {l}} (\hat {L} _ {l - 1} - \hat {L} _ {l - 2})
$$

$$
\hat {L} _ {(l - 1, m) / 2} = \hat {L} _ {l - 1} + \frac {t _ {m} - t _ {l - 1}}{2 T _ {l}} (\hat {L} _ {l - 1} - \hat {L} _ {l - 2})
$$

由式 (9 7.22)

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \Delta \mathbf {R} _ {s f m} ^ {b (m - 1)} & = \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {s f} ^ {b (m - 1)} (t) \mathrm{d} t \\ & = \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} \left[ \Delta \mathbf {V} ^ {b} (t) + \frac {1}{2} \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} ^ {b} (t) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} ^ {b} (t) + \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {\text {scul}} ^ {b} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t \end{array}\tag{9.7.25}
$$

记

$$
\boldsymbol {\gamma} _ {1} = \frac {1}{2} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} ^ {b} (t) \times \Delta \boldsymbol {V} ^ {b} (t) d t
$$

对上述积分采用分步积分法求取，并记

$$
\begin{array}{l} \boldsymbol {\gamma} _ {2} = \frac {1}{2} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} ^ {b} (t) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} ^ {b} (t) \mathrm{d} t = \frac {1}{2} \mathbf {S} _ {\Delta \theta m} ^ {b} \times \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} ^ {b} - \frac {1}{2} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} \mathbf {S} _ {\Delta \theta} ^ {b} (t) \times \mathbf {f} ^ {b} (t) \mathrm{d} t \\ \boldsymbol {\gamma} _ {3} = \frac {1}{2} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} ^ {b} (t) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} ^ {b} (t) \mathrm{d} t = \frac {1}{2} \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} ^ {b} \times \mathbf {S} _ {\Delta v m} ^ {b} + \frac {1}{2} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} \mathbf {S} _ {\Delta v} ^ {b} (t) \times \boldsymbol {\omega} ^ {b} (t) \mathrm{d} t \end{array}
$$

则

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {1}{2} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} ^ {b} (t) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} ^ {b} (t) \mathrm{d} t = \frac {1}{3} (\boldsymbol {\gamma} _ {1} + \boldsymbol {\gamma} _ {2} + \boldsymbol {\gamma} _ {3}) \\ = \frac {1}{6} (\mathbf {S} _ {\Delta \emptyset m} ^ {b} \times \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} ^ {b} + \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} ^ {b} \times \mathbf {S} _ {\Delta v m} ^ {b}) + \frac {1}{6} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} [ \mathbf {S} _ {\Delta v} ^ {b} (t) \times \boldsymbol {\omega} ^ {b} (t) \\ - \mathbf {S} _ {\Delta \emptyset} ^ {b} (t) \times \mathbf {f} ^ {b} (t) + \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} ^ {b} (t) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} ^ {b} (t) ] \mathrm{d} t \end{array}\tag{9.7.26}
$$

上式代人式 (9. 7. 25)

$$
\Delta \boldsymbol {R} _ {s f m} ^ {b (m - 1)} = \boldsymbol {S} _ {\Delta v m} ^ {b} + \Delta \boldsymbol {R} _ {\mathrm{rot} m} ^ {b} + \Delta \boldsymbol {R} _ {\mathrm{scrl} m} ^ {b}\tag{9.7.27}
$$

式中

$$
\boldsymbol {S} _ {\Delta v m} ^ {b} = \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} \Delta \boldsymbol {V} ^ {b} (t) \mathrm{d} t = \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t} \boldsymbol {f} ^ {b} (\tau) \mathrm{d} \tau \mathrm{d} t\tag{9.7.28}
$$

为比力的二次积分增量。

$$
\Delta \boldsymbol {R} _ {\text { rot } m} ^ {b} = \frac {1}{6} (\boldsymbol {S} _ {\Delta \theta m} ^ {b} \times \Delta \boldsymbol {V} _ {m} ^ {b} + \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} ^ {b} \times \boldsymbol {S} _ {\Delta v m} ^ {b})\tag{9.7.29}
$$

称为位置计算中的旋转效应补偿量。

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \Delta \boldsymbol {R} _ {\text {scrlm}} ^ {b} = & \frac {1}{6} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} \left[ \boldsymbol {S} _ {\Delta v} ^ {b} (t) \times \boldsymbol {\omega} ^ {b} (t) - \boldsymbol {S} _ {\Delta \theta} ^ {b} (t) \times \boldsymbol {f} ^ {b} (t) \right. \\ & + \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} ^ {b} (t) \times \Delta \boldsymbol {V} ^ {b} (t) + 6 \Delta \boldsymbol {V} _ {\text {scul}} (t) ] \mathrm{d} t \end{array}\tag{9.7.30}
$$

称为位置计算中的涡卷效应补偿量。

(9.7.30)说明，影响涡卷效应的因素有划桨效应和运载体角运动和线运动之间的精合效应.

假设运载体的角速度和加速度均为常值，即 $\vert \pmb { \omega } ( t ) = \pmb { \omega } , \pmb { f } ( t ) = \pmb { f }$ .则对 $t _ { m - 1 } { \leqslant } t { \leqslant } t _ { m }$

$$
\Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} (t) = \left(t - t _ {m - 1}\right) \boldsymbol {\omega}, \quad \Delta \mathbf {V} (t) = \left(t - t _ {m - 1}\right) \boldsymbol {f}
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {S} _ {\Delta \theta} (t) = \frac {1}{2} (t - t _ {m - 1}) ^ {2} \boldsymbol {\omega}, \quad \boldsymbol {S} _ {\Delta v} (t) = \frac {1}{2} (t - t _ {m - 1}) ^ {2} \boldsymbol {f}
$$

根据式 (9.7.23)

$$
\Delta \mathbf {V} _ {\text { scul }} (t) = \frac {1}{2} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t} \left[ (\tau - t _ {m - 1}) \boldsymbol {\omega} \times \boldsymbol {f} + (\tau - t _ {m - 1}) \boldsymbol {f} \times \boldsymbol {\omega} \right] \mathrm{d} \tau = \mathbf {0}
$$

上述诸式代人式 (9.7.30)

$$
\Delta \boldsymbol {R} _ {\mathrm{scrl} m} (t) = \frac {1}{6} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} \left[ \frac {1}{2} \left(t - t _ {m - 1}\right) ^ {2} (\boldsymbol {f} \times \boldsymbol {\omega} - \boldsymbol {\omega} \times \boldsymbol {f}) + \left(t - t _ {m - 1}\right) ^ {2} \boldsymbol {\omega} \times \boldsymbol {f} + \mathbf {0} \right] \mathrm{d} t = \mathbf {0}
$$

上述分析说明，当运载体的角速度和加速度均为常值时，涡卷效应补偿量为零。但应注意旋转效应补偿量不为零。

如果运载体的角速度和加速度虽然随时间而变，但始终保持共线，则涡卷效应补偿量和旋转效应补偿量均为零，读者可自行分析之。

## 9.7.3 旋转效应和涡卷效应补偿算法

由式(9.7.29)和式(9.7.30)给出的旋转效应和涡卷效应补偿计算公式中具有积分形式，而相应量的时间函数是不知道的，所以公式并不实用。与速度解算类似，应该将公式转换成只含角增量和速度增量的表达式才适用于工程。为此，可将运载体在[ $\cdot t _ { m - 1 } \cdot t _ { m }$ 时间段内的角速度和比力拟合成直线、抛物线或三次抛物线等有规律函数，这样做的好处是=一方面拟合曲线可以用角增量和速度增量来表示，另一方面可以根据拟合曲线求取相应积分。此处以角速度和比力都采用直线拟合为例，说明旋转效应和涡卷效应实用补偿算法的具体推导方法。对于角速度和比力采用高次曲线拟合的情况，算法的推导方法与此完全相同，只不过繁杂冗长些罢了。

假设在速度更新周期 $[ t _ { m - 1 } , t _ { m }$ 内采用直线拟合角速度和比力，即对于 $t _ { m - 1 } { \leqslant } t { \leqslant } t _ { m }$

$$
\boldsymbol {\omega} (t) = \boldsymbol {a} + \boldsymbol {b} (t - t _ {m - 1})\tag{9.7.31}
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {f} (t) = \boldsymbol {A} + \boldsymbol {B} (t - t _ {m - 1})\tag{9.7.32}
$$

此处，为书写简便，角速度和比力均省去了表示在机体坐标系 内投影的上标，下同。

根据式 (9.7.31)和式 (9. 7. 32).

$$
\Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} (t) = \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t} \boldsymbol {\omega} (\tau) \mathrm{d} \tau = \boldsymbol {a} (t - t _ {m - 1}) + \frac {\boldsymbol {b}}{2} (t - t _ {m - 1}) ^ {2}\tag{9.7.33a}
$$

$$
\Delta \mathbf {V} (t) = \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t} \boldsymbol {f} (\tau) \mathrm{d} \tau = \mathbf {A} (t - t _ {m - 1}) + \frac {\mathbf {B}}{2} (t - t _ {m - 1}) ^ {2}\tag{9.7.33b}
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {S} _ {\Delta \theta} (t) = \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {\tau} \boldsymbol {\omega} (\mu) \mathrm{d} \mu \mathrm{d} \tau = \frac {\boldsymbol {a}}{2} (t - t _ {m - 1}) ^ {2} + \frac {\boldsymbol {b}}{6} (t - t _ {m - 1}) ^ {3}\tag{9.7.33c}
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {S} _ {\Delta v} (t) = \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {\tau} \boldsymbol {f} (\mu) \mathrm{d} \mu \mathrm{d} \tau = \frac {\boldsymbol {A}}{2} (t - t _ {m - 1}) ^ {2} + \frac {\boldsymbol {B}}{6} (t - t _ {m - 1}) ^ {3}\tag{9.7.33d}
$$

由式(ω9.7.3 1)、式 (9.7.32) 和式 (9.7.33) ，得

$$
\Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} = \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} \boldsymbol {\omega} (t) \mathrm{d} t = T _ {m} \boldsymbol {a} + \frac {T _ {m} ^ {2}}{2} \boldsymbol {b}\tag{9.7.34a}
$$

$$
\Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} = \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} \boldsymbol {f} (t) \mathrm{d} t = T _ {m} \mathbf {A} + \frac {T _ {m} ^ {2}}{2} \mathbf {B}\tag{9.7.34b}
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {S} _ {\Delta \theta m} = \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {\tau} \boldsymbol {\omega} (\mu) \mathrm{d} \mu \mathrm{d} \tau = \frac {T _ {m} ^ {2}}{2} \boldsymbol {a} + \frac {T _ {m} ^ {3}}{6} \boldsymbol {b}\tag{9.7.34c}
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {S} _ {\Delta v m} = \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m}} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {\tau} \boldsymbol {f} (\mu) \mathrm{d} \mu \mathrm{d} \tau = \frac {T _ {m} ^ {2}}{2} \boldsymbol {A} + \frac {T _ {m} ^ {3}}{6} \boldsymbol {B}\tag{9.7.34d}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {\text {scul}} (t) = \frac {1}{2} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t} [ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} (\tau) \times \boldsymbol {f} (\tau) + \Delta \mathbf {V} (\tau) \times \boldsymbol {\omega} (\tau) ] \mathrm{d} \tau \\ = \frac {1}{2} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t} \left\{\left[ \boldsymbol {a} (\tau - t _ {m - 1}) + \frac {\boldsymbol {b}}{2} (\tau - t _ {m - 1}) ^ {2} \right] \times [ \boldsymbol {A} + \boldsymbol {B} (\tau - t _ {m - 1}) ] + \left[ \boldsymbol {A} (\tau - t _ {m - 1}) + \frac {\boldsymbol {B}}{2} (\tau - t _ {m - 1}) ^ {2} \right] \times [ \boldsymbol {a} + \boldsymbol {b} (\tau - t _ {m - 1}) ] \right\} \mathrm{d} \tau \\ = \frac {1}{2} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t} \left[ (\tau - t _ {m - 1}) \boldsymbol {a} \times \boldsymbol {A} + (\tau - t _ {m - 1}) ^ {2} \boldsymbol {a} \times \boldsymbol {B} + \frac {1}{2} (\tau - t _ {m - 1}) ^ {2} \boldsymbol {b} \times \boldsymbol {A} + \frac {1}{2} (\tau - t _ {m - 1}) ^ {3} \boldsymbol {b} \times \boldsymbol {B} + (\tau - t _ {m - 1}) \boldsymbol {A} \times \boldsymbol {a} + (\tau - t _ {m - 1}) ^ {2} \boldsymbol {A} \times \boldsymbol {b} + \frac {1}{2} (\tau - t _ {m - 1}) ^ {2} \boldsymbol {B} \times \boldsymbol {a} + \frac {1}{2} (\tau - t _ {m - 1}) ^ {3} \boldsymbol {B} \times \boldsymbol {b} \right] \mathrm{d} \tau \\ = \frac {1}{1 2} (t - t _ {m - 1}) ^ {3} (\boldsymbol {A} \times \boldsymbol {b} + \boldsymbol {a} \times \boldsymbol {B}) \end{array}\tag{9.7.35}
$$

若将 $\sum { t _ { m - 1 } } , { t _ { m } } _ { - }$ ]时间段作两等分，则在每个等分段内的角增量和速度增量为

$$
\Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (1) = \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m - 1} + \frac {T _ {m}}{2}} [ \boldsymbol {a} + \boldsymbol {b} (t - t _ {m - 1}) ] \mathrm{d} t = \frac {T _ {m}}{2} \boldsymbol {a} + \frac {T _ {m} ^ {2}}{8} \boldsymbol {b}\tag{9.7.36a}
$$

$$
\Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (2) = \int_ {t _ {m - 1} + \frac {T _ {m}}{2}} ^ {t _ {m - 1} + T _ {m}} [ \boldsymbol {a} + \boldsymbol {b} (t - t _ {m - 1}) ] \mathrm{d} t = \frac {T _ {m}}{2} \boldsymbol {a} + \frac {3 T _ {m} ^ {2}}{8} \boldsymbol {b}\tag{9.7.36b}
$$

$$
\Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) = \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t _ {m - 1} + \frac {T _ {m}}{2}} [ \mathbf {A} + \mathbf {B} (t - t _ {m - 1}) ] \mathrm{d} t = \frac {T _ {m}}{2} \mathbf {A} + \frac {T _ {m} ^ {2}}{8} \mathbf {B}\tag{9.7.37a}
$$

$$
\Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) = \int_ {t _ {m - 1} + \frac {T _ {m}}{2}} ^ {t _ {m - 1} + T _ {m}} [ \mathbf {A} + \mathbf {B} (t - t _ {m - 1}) ] \mathrm{d} t = \frac {T _ {m}}{2} \mathbf {A} + \frac {3 T _ {m} ^ {2}}{8} \mathbf {B}\tag{9.7.37b}
$$

求解式 (9.7.36) ，得

$$
\boldsymbol {a} = \frac {1}{T _ {m}} \left[ 3 \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (1) - \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (2) \right]\tag{9.7.38a}
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {b} = \frac {4}{T _ {m} ^ {2}} \left[ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (2) - \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (1) \right]\tag{9.7.38b}
$$

求解式 (9.7.37) ，得

$$
\boldsymbol {A} = \frac {1}{T _ {m}} \left[ 3 \Delta \boldsymbol {V} _ {m} (1) - \Delta \boldsymbol {V} _ {m} (2) \right]\tag{9.7.38c}
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {B} = \frac {4}{T _ {m} ^ {2}} \left[ \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) - \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) \right]\tag{9.7.38d}
$$

将式(ω9.7.34) 代人式 (9.7.29) ，得

$$
\begin{array} { r l } \Delta \boldsymbol { R } _ { \text {rotm} } & = \frac { 1 } { 6 } ( \boldsymbol { S } _ { \Delta \theta m } \times \Delta \boldsymbol { V } _ { m } + \Delta \boldsymbol { \theta } _ { m } \times \boldsymbol { S } _ { \Delta v m } ) \\ & = \frac { 1 } { 6 } \left[ \left( \frac { T _ { m } ^ { 2 } } { 2 } \boldsymbol { a } + \frac { T _ { m } ^ { 3 } } { 6 } \boldsymbol { b } \right) \times \left( T _ { m } \boldsymbol { A } + \frac { T _ { m } ^ { 2 } } { 2 } \boldsymbol { B } \right) + \left( T _ { m } \boldsymbol { a } + \frac { T _ { m } ^ { 2 } } { 2 } \boldsymbol { b } \right) \times \left( \frac { T _ { m } ^ { 2 } } { 2 } \boldsymbol { A } + \frac { T _ { m } ^ { 3 } } { 6 } \boldsymbol { B } \right) \right] \\ & = \frac { T _ { m } ^ { 3 } } { 3 6 } \left[ ( 3 \boldsymbol { a } + T _ { m } \boldsymbol { b } ) \times \left( \boldsymbol { A } + \frac { T _ { m } } { 2 } \boldsymbol { B } \right) + \left( \boldsymbol { a } + \frac { T _ { m } } { 2 } \boldsymbol { b } \right) \times ( 3 \boldsymbol { A } + T _ { m } \boldsymbol { B } ) \right] \\ & = \frac { T _ { m } ^ { 3 } } { 3 6 } \left\{ \frac { 3 } { T _ { m } } [ 3 \Delta \boldsymbol { \theta } _ { m } ( 1 ) - \Delta \boldsymbol { \theta } _ { m } ( 2 ) ] + T _ { m } \cdot \frac { 4 } { T _ { m } ^ { 2 } } [ \Delta \boldsymbol { \theta } _ { m } ( 2 ) - \Delta \boldsymbol { \theta } _ { m } ( 1 ) ] \right\} \\ & \quad \times \left\{ \frac { 1 } { T _ { m } } [ 3 \Delta \boldsymbol { V } _ { m } ( 1 ) - \Delta \boldsymbol { V } _ { m } ( 2 ) ] + \frac { T _ { m } } { 2 } \cdot
