(9.7.39)和式(9.7.40)分别是旋转效应补偿量和涡卷效应补偿量的双子样算法，它们是在角速度和比力采用直线拟合的条件下获得的。如果角速度和比力采用二次抛物线拟舍，则可得旋转效应补偿量和涡卷效应补偿量的三子样算法。同样，如果用三次抛物线拟合时，则可得四子样算法，以此类推。算法的推导思路与双子样算法完全相同。此处给出涡卷效应补偿量的三子祥算法和四子样算法，以及旋转效应补偿量的三子样算法和四子祥算法。

涡卷效应补偿量的三子样算法:

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \Delta \boldsymbol {R} _ {\mathrm{scr} b m} & = T _ {m} \left\{\Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (1) \times \left[ \frac {1 7}{1 4 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) + \frac {1 6}{3 5} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) - \frac {5 1}{5 6 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (3) \right] \right. \\ & + \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (2) \times \left[ - \frac {2 2 7}{5 6 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) + \frac {6 9}{5 6 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) + \frac {2}{3 5} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (3) \right] \\ & + \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (3) \times \left[ - \frac {9}{7 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) - \frac {7 3}{5 6 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) - \frac {1}{2 8 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (3) \right] \end{array}\tag{9.7.41}
$$

涡卷效应补偿量的四子样算法

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \Delta \boldsymbol {R} _ {\mathrm{scr} b m} & = T _ {m} \left\{\Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (1) \times \left[ \frac {7 9 7}{5 6 7 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) + \frac {1 1 0 3}{1 8 9 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) + \frac {4 7}{6 3 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (3) - \frac {4 7}{8 1 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (4) \right] \right. \\ & \quad + \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (2) \times \left[ - \frac {3 0 7}{6 3 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) + \frac {4 3}{3 7 8} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) + \frac {6 2 9}{1 8 9 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (3) - \frac {1 3}{2 7 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (4) \right] \\ & \quad + \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (3) \times \left[ - \frac {3 7}{3 7 8 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) - \frac {7 9}{2 7 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) + \frac {1 7 3}{1 8 9 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (3) + \frac {6 1}{1 8 9 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (4) \right] \\ & \quad + \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (4) \times \left[ - \frac {1 0 9 1}{5 6 7 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) - \frac {5 9}{6 3 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) - \frac {1 8 7}{1 8 9 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (3) - \frac {1}{5 6 7 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (4) \right] \end{array}\tag{9.7.42}
$$

旋转效应补偿量的三子样算法

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \Delta \boldsymbol {R} _ {\text {rot} m} & = T _ {m} \left\{\Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (1) \times \left[ \frac {7}{2 4} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) + \frac {1 1}{4 8} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) + \frac {1}{6} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (3) \right] \right. \\ & \quad + \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (2) \times \left[ \frac {1 1}{4 8} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) + \frac {1}{6} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) + \frac {5}{4 8} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (3) \right] \\ & \quad + \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (3) \times \left[ \frac {1}{6} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) + \frac {5}{4 8} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) + \frac {1}{2 4} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (3) \right] \end{array}\tag{9.7.43}
$$

旋转效应补偿量的四子样算法

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \Delta \boldsymbol {R} _ {\text {rotm}} & = T _ {m} \left\{\Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (1) \times \left[ \frac {8 3}{2 7 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) + \frac {6 7}{2 7 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) + \frac {6 1}{2 7 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (3) + \frac {1}{6} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (4) \right] \right. \\ & \quad + \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (2) \times \left[ \frac {6 7}{2 7 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) + \frac {1 7}{9 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) + \frac {1}{6} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (3) + \frac {2 9}{2 7 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (4) \right] \\ & \quad + \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (3) \times \left[ \frac {6 1}{2 7 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) + \frac {1}{6} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) + \frac {1 3}{9 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (3) + \frac {2 3}{2 7 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (4) \right] \\ & \quad + \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (4) \times \left[ \frac {1}{6} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) + \frac {2 9}{2 7 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) + \frac {2 3}{2 7 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (3) + \frac {7}{2 7 0} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (4) \right] \end{array}\tag{9.7.44}
$$

## 9.7.4 涡卷效应补偿算法的优化

9.7.2 节中曾分析过当运载体的角速度和比力都为常值时，涡卷效应补偿量为零。此时，若采用双子样算法计算涡卷效应补偿量，则

$$
\begin{array}{l} \Delta \boldsymbol {R} _ {\mathrm{scrl} m} = T _ {m} \left[ \frac {T _ {m}}{2} \boldsymbol {\omega} \times \left(\frac {1 1}{9 0} \cdot \frac {T _ {m}}{2} \boldsymbol {f} + \frac {1}{1 0} \cdot \frac {T _ {m}}{2} \boldsymbol {f}\right) + \frac {T _ {m}}{2} \boldsymbol {\omega} \times \left(- \frac {7}{3 0} \cdot \frac {T _ {m}}{2} \boldsymbol {f} + \frac {1}{9 0} \cdot \frac {T _ {m}}{2} \boldsymbol {f}\right) \right] \\ = \frac {T _ {m} ^ {3}}{4} \boldsymbol {\omega} \times \boldsymbol {f} \left(\frac {1 1}{9 0} + \frac {1}{1 0} - \frac {7}{3 0} + \frac {1}{9 0}\right) = \mathbf {0} \end{array}
$$

而运载体的角速度和比力保持不变可理解成角运动和线运动的频率为零，即仅存在直流分量。可见在零频率运动状态下，即使采用子样数较低的双子样算法也不会产生算法误差。由此可推断涡卷效应补偿量的算法误差与运载体的运动状态有关，运载体机动运动越激烈，即频率越高，线运动和角运动之间的精合越强烈，则算法误差也就越大。划桨运动反映了运载体线运动和角运动之间的强烈搞合，同时也体现了运载体的高频运动。所以应该以划桨运动为条件推导出涡卷效应补偿算法中的优化系数。与圆锥误差的优化补偿算法类似，在划桨运动这种极端运动状态下推导出的优化算法，在其他机动运动状态下算法也是最优的。

假设运载体沿 $\mathcal { X } _ { b }$ 轴存在角振动，沿 $y _ { b }$ 轴存在线振动，相应的角速度和比力分别为

$$
\pmb {\omega} (t) = i B \Omega \cos \Omega t\tag{9.7.45a}
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {f} (t) = \boldsymbol {j} C \sin (\Omega t + \varphi)\tag{9.7.45b}
$$

由上两式，对于 $t _ { m - 1 } { \leqslant } t { \leqslant } t _ { m }$

$$
\Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} (t) = \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t} \boldsymbol {\omega} (\tau) \mathrm{d} \tau = i B (\sin \Omega t - \sin \Omega t _ {m - 1})\tag{9.7.46a}
$$

$$
\Delta \mathbf {V} (t) = \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t} \boldsymbol {f} (\tau) \mathrm{d} \tau = - \boldsymbol {j} \frac {C}{\Omega} [ \cos (\Omega t + \varphi) - \cos (\Omega t _ {m - 1} + \varphi) ]\tag{9.7.46b}
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {S} _ {\Delta \theta} (t) = \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {\mu} \boldsymbol {\omega} (\tau) \mathrm{d} \tau \mathrm{d} \mu = - i \frac {B}{\Omega} \left[ \cos \Omega t - \cos \Omega t _ {m - 1} + \Omega (t - t _ {m - 1}) \sin \Omega t _ {m - 1} \right]\tag{9.7.46c}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \boldsymbol {S} _ {\Delta v} (t) & = \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {\mu} \boldsymbol {f} (\tau) \mathrm{d} \tau \mathrm{d} \mu \\ & = - \boldsymbol {j} \frac {C}{\Omega^ {2}} [ \sin (\Omega t + \varphi) - \sin (\Omega t _ {m - 1} + \varphi) - \Omega (t - t _ {m - 1}) \cos (\Omega t _ {m - 1} + \varphi) ] \\ \Delta V _ {\text {scul}} (t) & = \frac {1}{2} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t} [ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} (\tau) \times \boldsymbol {f} (\tau) + \Delta V (\tau) \times \boldsymbol {\omega} (\tau) ] \mathrm{d} \tau \\ & = \boldsymbol {k} \cdot \frac {B C}{2} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t} \left\{(\sin \Omega \tau - \sin \Omega t _ {m - 1}) \sin (\Omega \tau + \varphi) + [ \cos (\Omega \tau + \varphi) - \cos (\Omega t _ {m - 1} + \varphi) ] \cos \Omega \tau \right\} \mathrm{d} \tau \\ & = \boldsymbol {k} \frac {B C}{2} \int_ {t _ {m - 1}} ^ {t} \left\{\frac {1}{2} [ \cos \varphi - \cos (2 \Omega \tau + \varphi) ] - \sin \Omega t _ {m - 1} \sin (\Omega \tau + \varphi) + \frac {1}{2} [ \cos (2 \Omega \tau + \varphi) + \cos \varphi ] - \cos (\Omega t _ {m - 1} + \varphi) \cos \Omega \tau \right\} \mathrm{d} \tau \end{array}\tag{9.7.46d}
$$

.7.4

将式(9.7.45)和式(9.7.46)代人式(9.7.30) .经整理并略去交流分量，则划桨运动条件下涡卷效应补偿量直流分量的真实值为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \left[ \Delta \boldsymbol {R} _ {\mathrm{scrlm}} \right] _ {\text {rect}} = & \boldsymbol {k} \left\{\frac {B C}{4 \Omega^ {2}} \left[ (\Omega T _ {m}) ^ {2} + 2 \cos \Omega T _ {m} - 2 \right] \cos \varphi \right. \\ & \left. + \frac {B C}{2 \Omega^ {2}} \left(\frac {2}{3} \Omega T _ {m} + \frac {1}{3} \Omega T _ {m} \cos \Omega T _ {m} - \sin \Omega T _ {m}\right) \sin \varphi \right\} \end{array}\tag{9.7.47}
$$

对上式作泰勒级数展开，并记 $\lambda { = } \varOmega T _ { m }$ ，则

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \left[ \Delta \boldsymbol {R} _ {\mathrm{scrl} m} \right] _ {\text {rect}} & = k \frac {B C}{4 \Omega^ {2}} \left\{\left[ \lambda^ {2} + 2 \left(1 - \frac {\lambda^ {2}}{2 !} + \frac {\lambda^ {4}}{4 !} - \frac {\lambda^ {6}}{6 !} + \dots\right) - 2 \right] \cos \varphi \right. \\ & \quad + 2 \left[ \frac {2}{3} \lambda + \frac {\lambda}{3} \left(1 - \frac {\lambda^ {2}}{2 !} + \frac {\lambda^ {4}}{4 !} - \frac {\lambda^ {6}}{6 !} + \dots\right) - \left(\lambda - \frac {\lambda^ {3}}{3 !} + \frac {\lambda^ {5}}{5 !} - \dots\right) \right] \sin \varphi \Bigg \} \end{array}
$$

$$
= k \frac {B C T _ {m}}{\Omega} \left[ \left(\frac {\lambda^ {3}}{4 8} - \frac {\lambda^ {5}}{1 4 4 0} + \dots\right) \cos \varphi + \left(\frac {\lambda^ {4}}{3 6 0} - \frac {\lambda^ {6}}{7 5 6 0} + \dots\right) \sin \varphi \right]\tag{9.7.48}
$$

而在实际计算过程中，涡卷效应补偿量是根据角增量和速度增量确定的，在式(9.7.45)所假设的划桨运动条件下，对子样数为 的情况

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (\nu) & = \int_ {t _ {m - 1} + \frac {\nu - 1}{N} T _ {m}} ^ {t _ {m - 1} + \frac {\nu}{N} T _ {m}} \boldsymbol {f} (t) \mathrm{d} t = \boldsymbol {j} \frac {C}{\Omega} \left\{\cos \left[ \Omega \left(t _ {m - 1} + \frac {\nu - 1}{N} T _ {m}\right) + \varphi \right] \right. \\ & \quad \left. - \cos \left[ \Omega \left(t _ {m - 1} + \frac {\nu}{N} T _ {m}\right) + \varphi \right] \right\}, \quad \nu = 1, 2, 3, \dots , N \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (\mu) = \boldsymbol {i} \int_ {t _ {m - 1} + \frac {\mu - 1}{N} T _ {m}} ^ {t _ {m - 1} + \frac {\mu}{N} T _ {m}} \boldsymbol {\omega} (t) \mathrm{d} t = \boldsymbol {i} B \left[ \sin \Omega \left(t _ {m - 1} + \frac {\mu}{N} T _ {m}\right) \right. \\ \left. - \sin \Omega \left(t _ {m - 1} + \frac {\mu - 1}{N} T _ {m}\right) \right], \quad \mu = 1, 2, 3, \dots , N \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (\mu) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (\nu) = \boldsymbol {k} \frac {B C}{\Omega} \left[ \sin \Omega \left(t _ {m - 1} + \frac {\mu}{N} T _ {m}\right) - \sin \Omega \left(t _ {m - 1} + \frac {\mu - 1}{N} T _ {m}\right) \right] \\ \quad \times \left\{\cos \left[ \Omega \left(t _ {m - 1} + \frac {\nu - 1}{N} T _ {m}\right) + \varphi \right] - \cos \left[ \Omega \left(t _ {m - 1} + \frac {\nu}{N} T _ {m}\right) + \varphi \right] \right\} \\ = \boldsymbol {k} \frac {4 B C}{\Omega} \left(\sin \frac {\Omega T _ {m}}{2 N}\right) ^ {2} \cos \Omega \left(t _ {m - 1} + \frac {2 \mu - 1}{2 N} T _ {m}\right) \times \sin \left[ \Omega \left(t _ {m - 1} + \frac {2 \nu - 1}{2 N} T _ {m}\right) + \varphi \right] \\ = \boldsymbol {k} \frac {B C}{\Omega} \left(1 - \cos \frac {\Omega T _ {m}}{N}\right) \left[ \sin \left(2 \Omega t _ {m - 1} + \frac {\mu + \nu - 1}{N} \Omega T _ {m} + \varphi\right) + \sin \left(\frac {\nu - \mu}{N} \Omega T _ {m} + \varphi\right) \right] \end{array}
$$

略去交流分量，并记 $\lambda { = } \Omega T _ { m }$ ，则直流分量为

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left[ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (\mu) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (\nu) \right] _ {\text {rect}} = k \frac {B C}{\Omega} \left(1 - \cos \frac {\lambda}{N}\right) \sin \left(\frac {\nu - \mu}{N} \lambda + \varphi\right) \\ = k \frac {B C}{\Omega} \left(1 - \cos \frac {\lambda}{N}\right) \left(\sin \frac {\nu - \mu}{N} \lambda \cos \varphi + \cos \frac {\nu - \mu}{N} \lambda \sin \varphi\right), \quad \nu , \mu = 1, 2, 3, \dots , N \end{array}\tag{9.7.49}
$$

对于双子样算法 N=2 根据式(9 7.49)

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left[ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (1) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) \right] _ {\text {rect}} = k \frac {B C}{\Omega} \left(1 - \cos \frac {\lambda}{2}\right) \sin \varphi \\ \left[ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (1) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) \right] _ {\text {rect}} = k \frac {B C}{\Omega} \left(1 - \cos \frac {\lambda}{2}\right) \left(\sin \frac {\lambda}{2} \cos \varphi + \cos \frac {\lambda}{2} \sin \varphi\right) \\ \left[ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (2) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) \right] _ {\text {rect}} = k \frac {B C}{\Omega} \left(1 - \cos \frac {\lambda}{2}\right) \left(- \sin \frac {\lambda}{2} \cos \varphi + \cos \frac {\lambda}{2} \sin \varphi\right) \\ \left[ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (2) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) \right] _ {\text {rect}} = k \frac {B C}{\Omega} \left(1 - \cos \frac {\lambda}{2}\right) \sin \varphi \end{array}
$$

将上述诸式代人式(9.7.40) ，并将相应系数换成待求系数，注意到 $\overline { { \Delta } } \theta _ { m } ( 1 ) \times \Delta \mathbf { V } _ { m } ( 1 ) \daleth _ { \mathrm { r e c t } } =$ $\left[ \Delta \theta _ { m } ( 2 ) \times \Delta V _ { m } ( 2 ) \right] _ { \mathrm { r e c t } }$ ，对该两项的利用系数可取成相同，则

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \left[ \Delta \hat {\boldsymbol {R}} _ {\mathrm{scrl} m} \right] _ {\text {rect}} & = T _ {m} \left\{k _ {1} \left[ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (1) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) + \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (2) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) \right] _ {\text {rect}} \right. \\ & \quad + k _ {2} \left[ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (1) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (2) \right] _ {\text {rect}} + k _ {3} \left[ \Delta \boldsymbol {\theta} _ {m} (2) \times \Delta \mathbf {V} _ {m} (1) \right] _ {\text {rect}} \} \\ & = k \frac {B C T _ {m}}{\Omega} \left(1 - \cos \frac {\lambda}{2}\right) \left[ 2 k _ {1} \sin \varphi + k _ {2} \left(\sin \frac {\lambda}{2} \cos \varphi + \cos \frac {\lambda}{2} \sin \varphi\right) \right. \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l}+ k _ {3} \left(\cos \frac {\lambda}{2} \sin \varphi - \sin \frac {\lambda}{2} \cos \varphi\right)\\= k \frac {B C T _ {m}}{\Omega} \left(1 - \cos \frac {\lambda}{2}\right) \left\{\left[ 2 k _ {1} + (k _ {2} + k _ {3}) \cos \frac {\lambda}{2} \right] \sin \varphi + (k _ {2} - k _ {3}) \sin \frac {\lambda}{2} \cos \varphi \right\}\\= k \frac {B C T _ {m}}{\Omega} \left\{\left[ 2 k _ {1} - \frac {1}{2} (k _ {2} + k _ {3}) + (k _ {2} + k _ {3} - 2 k _ {1}) \cos \frac {\lambda}{2} - \frac {1}{2} (k _ {2} + k _ {3}) \cos \lambda \right] \sin \varphi + (k _ {2} - k _ {3}) \left(\sin \frac {\lambda}{2} - \frac {1}{2} \sin \lambda\right) \cos \varphi \right\}\\= k \frac {B C T _ {m}}{\Omega} \left\{ \right.\left[ 2 k _ {1} - \frac {1}{2} (k _ {2} + k _ {3}) + (k _ {2} + k _ {3} - 2 k _ {1}) \left(1 - \frac {\frac {\lambda^ {2}}{4}}{2 !} + \frac {\frac {\lambda^ {4}}{1 6}}{4 !} - \frac {\frac {\lambda^ {6}}{6 4}}{6 !} + \dots\right) - \frac {1}{2} (k _ {2} + k _ {3}) \left(1 - \frac {\lambda^ {2}}{2 !} + \frac {\lambda^ {4}}{4 !} - \frac {\lambda^ {6}}{6 !} + \dots\right)\right] \sin \varphi + (k _ {2} - k _ {3}) \left[ \frac {\lambda^ {3}}{2 !} + \frac {\lambda^ {5}}{3 !} - \frac {\lambda^ {7}}{5 !} + \dots - \frac {1}{2} (\lambda - \frac {\lambda^ {3}}{3 !} + \frac {\lambda^ {5}}{5 !} - \frac {\lambda^ {7}}{7 !} + \dots) \right] \cos \varphi\\= k \frac {B C T _ {m}}{\Omega} \left\{ \right.\left[ \right. \lambda^ {2} (\frac {k _ {1}}{4} + \frac {k _ {2}}{8} + \frac {k _ {3}}{8}) + \lambda^ {4} (- \frac {k _ {1}}{1 9 2} - \frac {7 k _ {2}}{3 8 4} - \frac {7 k _ {3}}{3 8 4}) + \lambda^ {6} (\frac {k _ {1}}{2 3 0 4 0} + \frac {6 3 k _ {2}}{4 6 0 8 0} + \frac {6 3 k _ {3}}{4 6 0 8 0}) + \dots ] \sin \varphi + [ \lambda^ {3} (\frac {k _ {2} - k _ {3}}{1 6} - \lambda^ {5} (\frac {k _ {2} - k _ {3}}{2 5 6} + \lambda^ {7} (\frac {6 3 (k _ {2} - k _ {3})}{6 4 5 1 2 0} + \dots) ] \cos \varphi ]\end{array}\tag{9.7.50}
$$

由式 (9.7.48) 和式 (9.7.50) ，引起的算法误差为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \left[ \Delta \tilde {\boldsymbol {R}} _ {\mathrm{scrl} m} \right] _ {\text {rect}} & = \left[ \Delta \dot {\boldsymbol {R}} _ {\mathrm{scrl} m} \right] _ {\text {rect}} - \left[ \Delta \boldsymbol {R} _ {\mathrm{scrl} m} \right] _ {\text {rect}} \\ & = \boldsymbol {k} \frac {B C T _ {m}}{\Omega} \left\{\left[ \lambda^ {2} \left(\frac {k _ {1}}{4} + \frac {k _ {2}}{8} + \frac {k _ {3}}{8}\right) + \lambda^ {4} \left(- \frac {k _ {1}}{1 9 2} - \frac {7 k _ {2}}{3 8 4} - \frac {7 k _ {3}}{3 8 4} - \frac {1}{3 6 0}\right) \right. \right. \\ & \quad \left. + \lambda^ {6} \left(\frac {k _ {1}}{2 3 0 4 0} + \frac {6 3 k _ {2}}{4 6 0 8 0} + \frac {6 3 k _ {3}}{4 6 0 8 0} + \frac {1}{7 5 6 0}\right) + \dots \right] \sin \varphi \\ & \quad \left. + \left[ \lambda^ {3} \left(\frac {k _ {2} - k _ {3}}{1 6} - \frac {1}{4 8}\right) - \lambda^ {5} \left(\frac {k _ {2} - k _ {3}}{2 5 6} - \frac {1}{1 4 4 0}\right) + \dots \right] \cos \varphi \right\} \end{array}\tag{9.7.51}
$$

由于飞机的划桨频率较低，速度更新周期具有毫秒量级，所以 $\lambda = \Omega T _ { m } { < } 1$ 。要使算法误差达到最小，应该使 的低署次项等于零，因此应使下述诸式成立

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} \frac {k _ {1}}{4} + \frac {k _ {2}}{8} + \frac {k _ {3}}{8} = 0 \\ - \frac {k _ {1}}{1 9 2} - \frac {7 k _ {2}}{3 8 4} - \frac {7 k _ {3}}{3 8 4} - \frac {1}{3 6 0} \\ \frac {k _ {2} - k _ {3}}{1 6} - \frac {1}{4 8} = 0 \end{array} \right.
$$

即

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} 2 k _ {1} + k _ {2} + k _ {3} = 0 \\ 2 k _ {1} + 7 k _ {2} + 7 k _ {3} = - \frac {1 6}{1 5} \\ k _ {2} - k _ {3} = \frac {1}{3} \end{array} \right.
$$

从上式可解得算法的优化系数

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} k _ {1} = \frac {4}{4 5} \\ k _ {2} = \frac {7}{9 0} \\ k _ {3} = - \frac {2 3}{9 0} \end{array} \right.
$$

上述诸系数代人式(9.7.51) ，算法误差为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \left[ \Delta \widetilde {\boldsymbol {R}} _ {\mathrm{scrl} m} \right] _ {\text {rect}} & \approx k \frac {B C T _ {m}}{\Omega} \left[ \lambda^ {6} \left(\frac {k _ {1}}{2 3 0 4 0} + \frac {6 3 k _ {2}}{4 6 0 8 0} + \frac {6 3 k _ {3}}{4 6 0 8 0} + \frac {1}{7 5 6 0}\right) \sin \varphi - \lambda^ {5} \left(\frac {k _ {2} - k _ {3}}{2 5 6} - \frac {1}{1 4 4 0}\right) \cos \varphi \right] \\ & = k \frac {B C T _ {m}}{\Omega} \left(- \frac {7}{1 1 5 2 0} \lambda^ {5} \cos \varphi + \frac {9 7}{9 0 7 2 0 0} \lambda^ {6} \sin \varphi\right) \end{array}
$$

相应的算法漂移为

$$
\left[ \Delta \dot {\tilde {R}} _ {\mathrm{scrl} m} \right] _ {\text {rect}} = \frac {1}{T _ {m}} \left[ \Delta \tilde {R} _ {\mathrm{scrl} m} \right] _ {\text {rect}} = k B C T _ {m} \left(- \frac {7}{1 1 5 2 0} \lambda^ {4} \cos \varphi + \frac {9 7}{9 0 7 2 0 0} \lambda^ {5} \sin \varphi\right)
$$

## 9.8 捷联惯导系统的误差模型

捷联惯导系统与平台惯导系统的主要区别在于对平台的构造方式上，前者采用数学方式，后者采用物理方式，但在本质上两类系统是相同的。对于工作在非极区的捷联惯导系统，为了简化计算，导航坐标系一般选取地理坐标系，这样，捷联惯导完全等效于指北方位系统。但是由于陀螺在平台系统中起控制器件作用，而在捷联系统中起测量器件作用，所以陀螺漂移及刻度系数误差对系统的影响方式并不相同。在平台惯导系统中，陀螺漂移引起的平台漂移率与陀螺漂移的方向相同，\~J度系数误差通过对平台的指令角速度引人系统;而在捷联惯导系统中，陀螺漂移引起的数学平台漂移率与陀螺漂移的方向相反，刻度系数误差引起对运载体角速度的测量误差，经姿态更新计算引人系统.

## 9.8.1 速度误差和位置误差方程

根据比力方程式(7.4.7) ，当不考虑任何误差时，速度的理想值由下式确定

$$
\dot {\mathbf {V}} ^ {n} = \mathbf {C} _ {b} ^ {n} \mathbf {f} ^ {b} - (2 \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i e} ^ {n} + \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {e n} ^ {n}) \times \mathbf {V} ^ {n} + \mathbf {g} ^ {n}\tag{9.8.1}
$$

而实际系统中总存在各种误差，所以实际的速度计算值应由下述方程确定

$$
\dot {\boldsymbol {V}} ^ {c} = \hat {\boldsymbol {C}} _ {b} ^ {n} \tilde {\boldsymbol {f}} ^ {b} - (2 \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i e} ^ {c} + \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {e n} ^ {c}) \times \boldsymbol {V} ^ {c} + \boldsymbol {g} ^ {c}\tag{9.8.2}
$$

式中

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & \mathbf {V} ^ {c} = \mathbf {V} ^ {n} + \delta \mathbf {V} ^ {n}, \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i e} ^ {c} = \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i e} ^ {n} + \delta \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i e} ^ {n}, \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {e n} ^ {c} = \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {e n} ^ {n} + \delta \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {e n} ^ {n}, \mathbf {g} ^ {c} = \mathbf {g} ^ {n} + \delta \mathbf {g} \\ & \hat {\mathbf {C}} _ {b} ^ {n} = \mathbf {C} _ {n} ^ {n ^ {\prime}} \mathbf {C} _ {b} ^ {n} = (\mathbf {I} - \boldsymbol {\varphi} ^ {n} \times) \mathbf {C} _ {b} ^ {n}, \tilde {\mathbf {f}} ^ {b} = (\mathbf {I} + [ \delta K _ {A} ]) (\mathbf {I} + [ \delta A ]) \mathbf {f} ^ {b} + \nabla^ {b} \\ & \boldsymbol {\varphi} ^ {n} \times = \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} 0 & - \phi_ {U} & \phi_ {N} \\ \phi_ {U} & 0 & - \phi_ {E} \\ - \phi_ {N} & \phi_ {E} & 0 \end{array} \right], [ \delta K _ {A} ] = \operatorname{diag} [ \delta K _ {A x} \quad \delta K _ {A y} \quad \delta K _ {A z} ] \\ & [ \delta A ] = \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} 0 & \delta A _ {z} & - \delta A _ {y} \\ - \delta A _ {z} & 0 & \delta A _ {x} \\ \delta A _ {y} & - \delta A _ {x} & 0 \end{array} \right] \end{array}
$$

式中， $\mathrm { ~ , ~ } \phi _ { E } \mathrm { \hdots } \phi _ { N } \mathrm { \hdots } \phi _ { U }$ 如为姿态误差角 $\delta K _ { A i }$ 和 $\delta A _ { i } ( i = x , y , z )$ 分别为加速度计的刻度系数误差和安装误差角。

用式 (9. 8. 2)减去式 (9.8，1)，忽 $\delta { g }$ 泌的影响，并略去二阶小量，得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \delta \dot {\boldsymbol {V}} ^ {n} = & - \phi^ {n} \times \boldsymbol {f} ^ {n} + \boldsymbol {C} _ {b} ^ {n} ([ \delta K _ {A} ] + [ \delta A ]) \boldsymbol {f} ^ {b} + \delta \boldsymbol {V} ^ {n} \times (2 \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i e} ^ {n} + \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {e n} ^ {n}) \\ & + \boldsymbol {V} ^ {n} \times (2 \delta \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i e} ^ {n} + \delta \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {e n} ^ {n}) + \boldsymbol {\nabla} ^ {n} \end{array}\tag{9.8.3}
$$

当取地理坐标系为导航坐标系时，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i e} ^ {n} = \left[ \begin{array}{c} 0 \\ \omega_ {i e} \cos L \\ \omega_ {i e} \sin L \end{array} \right], \quad \delta \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i e} ^ {n} = \left[ \begin{array}{c} 0 \\ - \delta L \omega_ {i e} \sin L \\ \delta L \omega_ {i e} \cos L \end{array} \right], \quad \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {e n} ^ {n} = \left[ \begin{array}{c} - \frac {V _ {N}}{R _ {M} + h} \\ \frac {V _ {E}}{R _ {N} + h} \\ \frac {V _ {E}}{R _ {N} + h} \tan L \end{array} \right] \\ \delta \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {e n} ^ {n} = \left[ \begin{array}{c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c c} - \frac {\delta V _ {N}}{R _ {M} + h} + \delta h   \frac {V _ {N}}{(R _ {M} + h) ^ {2}} & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & \\ \frac {\delta V _ {E}}{R _ {N} + h} - \delta h   \frac {V _ {E}}{(R _ {N} + h) ^ {2}} & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & & \\ \frac {\delta V _ {E}}{R _ {N} + h} \tan L + \delta L   \frac {V _ {E}}{R _ {N} + h} \sec^ {2} L - \delta h   \frac {V _ {E} \tan L}{(R _ {N} + h) ^ {2}} \end{array} \right] \end{array}
$$

记

$$
\boldsymbol {C} _ {b} ^ {n} = \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} T _ {1 1} & T _ {1 2} & T _ {1 3} \\ T _ {2 1} & T _ {2 2} & T _ {2 3} \\ T _ {3 1} & T _ {3 2} & T _ {3 3} \end{array} \right]
$$

上述诸式代人式(9.8.3) ，可得以分量形式表示的速度误差方程

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \left[ \begin{array}{c} \delta \dot {V} _ {E} \\ \delta \dot {V} _ {N} \\ \delta \dot {V} _ {U} \end{array} \right] & = \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} 0 & \phi_ {U} & - \phi_ {N} \\ - \phi_ {U} & 0 & \phi_ {E} \\ \phi_ {N} & - \phi_ {E} & 0 \end{array} \right] \left[ \begin{array}{c} f _ {E} \\ f _ {N} \\ f _ {U} \end{array} \right] + \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} T _ {1 1} & T _ {1 2} & T _ {1 3} \\ T _ {2 1} & T _ {2 2} & T _ {2 3} \\ T _ {3 1} & T _ {3 2} & T _ {3 3} \end{array} \right] \\ & \times \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} \delta K _ {A x} & \delta A _ {z} & - \delta A _ {y} \\ - \delta A _ {z} & \delta K _ {A y} & \delta A _ {x} \\ \delta A _ {y} & - \delta A _ {x} & \delta K _ {A z} \end{array} \right] \left[ \begin{array}{c} f _ {x} ^ {b} \\ f _ {y} ^ {b} \\ f _ {z} ^ {b} \end{array} \right] + \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} 0 & - \delta V _ {U} & \delta V _ {N} \\ \delta V _ {U} & 0 & - \delta V _ {E} \\ - \delta V _ {N} & \delta V _ {E} & 0 \end{array} \right] \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array} { r l } & { } \times \left[ \begin{array} { c } - \frac { V _ { N } } { R _ { M } + h } \\ 2 \omega _ { i e } \cos L + \frac { V _ { E } } { R _ { N } + h } \\ 2 \omega _ { i e } \sin L + \frac { V _ { E } } { R _ { N } + h } \tan L \end{array} \right] + \left[ \begin{array} { c c c } 0 & - V _ { U } & V _ { N } \\ V _ { U } & 0 & - V _ { E } \\ - V _ { N } & V _ { E } & 0 \end{array} \right] \\ & { } \times \left[ \begin{array} { c } - \frac { \delta V _ { N } } { R _ { M } + h } + \delta h \frac { V _ { N } } { ( R _ { M } + h ) ^ { 2 } } \\ - 2 \delta L \omega _ { i e } \sin L + \frac { \delta V _ { E } } { R _ { N } + h } - \delta h \frac { V _ { E } } { ( R _ { N } + h ) ^ { 2 } } \\ 2 \delta L \omega _ { i e } \cos L + \frac { \delta V _ { E } } { R _ { N } + h } \tan L + \delta L \frac { V _ { E } } { R _ { N } + h } \sec ^ { 2 } L - \delta h \frac { V _ { E } \tan L } { ( R _ { N } + h ) ^ { 2 } } \end{array} \right] + \left[ \begin{array} { c } \nabla _ { E } \\ \nabla _ { N } \\ \nabla _ { U } \end{array} \right] \\ & { } \delta \dot { V } _ { E } = \phi _ { U } f _ { N } - \phi _ { N } f _ { U } + T _ { 1 1 } ( \delta K _ { A x } f _ { x } ^ { b } + \delta A _ { z } f _ { y } ^ { b } - \delta A _ { y } f _ { z } ^ { b } ) \\ & { } + T _ { 1 2 } ( \delta K _ { A y } f _ { y } ^ { b } - \delta A _ { z } f _ { x } ^ { b } + \delta A _ { x } f _ { z } ^ { b } ) + T _ { 1 3 } ( \delta K _ { A z } f _ { z } ^ { b } + \delta A _ { y } f _ { x } ^ { b } - \delta A _ { x } f _ { y } ^ { b } ) \\ & { } + \delta V _ { E } \frac { V _ { N } \tan L - V _ { U } } { R _ { N } + h } + \delta V _ { N } ( 2 \omega _ { i e } \sin L + \frac { V _ { E } } { R _ { N } + h } \tan L ) \\ & { } - \delta V _ { U } ( 2 \omega _ { i e } \cos L + \frac { V _ { E } } { R _ { N } + h } ) + \delta L [ 2 \omega _ { i e } ( V _ { U } \sin L + V _ { N } \cos L ) \\ & { } + \frac { V _ { E } V _ { N } } { R _ { N } + h} \sec ^ { 2 } L ] + \delta h \frac { V _ { E } V _ { U } - V _ { E } V _ { N } \tan L } { ( R _ { N } + h ) ^ { 2 } } + \nabla _ { E } \\ & { } \delta \dot { V } _ { N } = - \phi _ { U } f _ { E } + \phi _ { E } f _ { U } + T _ { 2 1 } ( \delta K _ { A x } f _ { x } ^ { b } + \delta A _ { z } f _ { y } ^ { b } - \delta A _ { y } f _ { z } ^ { b } ) + T _ { 2 2 } ( \delta K _ { A y } f _ { y } ^ { b } - \delta A _ { z } f _ { x } ^ { b } + \delta A _ { x } f _ { z } ^ { b } ) \\ & { } + T _ { 2 3 } ( \delta K _ { A z } f _ { z } ^ { b } + \delta A _ { y } f _ { x } ^ { b } - \delta A _ { x } f _ { y } ^ { b} ) - \delta V _ { E} \cdot 2 ( \omega _ { i e} \sin L + \frac { V _ { E }}{ R _ { N } + h} \tan L ) \\ & { } - \delta V _ { N } \frac { V _ { U } } { R _ { M } + h } - \delta V _ { U } \frac { V _ { N } } { R _ { M } + h } - \delta L ( 2 V _ { E} \omega _ { i e} \cos L + \frac { V _ { E} ^ { 2 }}{ R _ { N } + h} \sec ^ { 2 } L ) \\ & { } + \delta h [ \frac { V _ { N } V _ { U }}{ ( R _ { M } + h ) ^ { 2 }} + \frac { V _ { E} ^ { 2} \tan L}{ ( R _ { N } + h ) ^ { 2 }} ] + \nabla _ { N } \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * . \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ & \\ / 9. 8. 4 a)   = - \end{array}
$$

式中

$$
\begin{array}{l} \nabla_ {E} = T _ {1 1} \nabla_ {x} ^ {b} + T _ {1 2} \nabla_ {y} ^ {b} + T _ {1 3} \nabla_ {z} ^ {b} \\ \nabla_ {N} = T _ {2 1} \nabla_ {x} ^ {b} + T _ {2 2} \nabla_ {y} ^ {b} + T _ {2 3} \nabla_ {z} ^ {b} \\ \nabla_ {U} = T _ {3 1} \nabla_ {x} ^ {b} + T _ {3 2} \nabla_ {y} ^ {b} + T _ {3 3} \nabla_ {z} ^ {b} \end{array}
$$

定位误差方程为

$$
\delta \dot {L} = \frac {\delta V _ {N}}{R _ {M} + h} - \delta h \frac {V _ {N}}{(R _ {M} + h) ^ {2}}\tag{9.8.5a}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \delta \dot {\lambda} = \frac {\delta V _ {E}}{R _ {N} + h} \sec L + \delta L \frac {V _ {E}}{R _ {N} + h} \tan L \sec L - \delta h \frac {V _ {E} \sec L}{(R _ {N} + h) ^ {2}} \\ \delta \dot {h} = \delta V _ {U} \end{array}\tag{9.8.5b}
$$

(9. 8. 5c)

## 9.8.2 姿态误差方程

根据式(9.2.47) .姿态四元数满足如下微分方程

$$
\dot {\boldsymbol {Q}} = \frac {1}{2} \boldsymbol {Q} \otimes \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {n b} ^ {b}
$$

式中，姿态速率 $\mathbf { \omega } _ { \omega _ { n b } } ^ { b }$ .视为零标量四元数。

如果求取的姿态速率不含任何误差，即

$$
\pmb {\omega} _ {n b} ^ {b} = \pmb {\omega} _ {i b} ^ {b} - \pmb {\omega} _ {i n} ^ {b}
$$

则无误差的理想姿态四元数由下式确定

$$
\dot {\boldsymbol {Q}} = \frac {1}{2} \boldsymbol {Q} \otimes \left(\boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i b} ^ {b} - \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i n} ^ {b}\right)\tag{9.8.6}
$$

但实际系统中，姿态速率由陀螺的输出角速度 $\widetilde { \pmb { \omega } } _ { i b } ^ { b }$ 和对数学平台的指令角速度 $\mathbf { \dot { \omega } } _ { \dot { m } } ^ { b }$ 确定

$$
\hat {\pmb {\omega}} _ {n b} ^ {b} = \widetilde {\pmb {\omega}} _ {i b} ^ {b} - \hat {\pmb {\omega}} _ {i n} ^ {b}
$$

其中，指令角速度 $\mathbf { \hat { \Pi } } _ { \pmb { { \omega } } _ { m } ^ { b } }$ 根据系统解算出的导航解确定，带有一定的误差。所以实际解算的四元数由下式确定

$$
\dot {\hat {Q}} = \frac {1}{2} \hat {Q} \otimes (\tilde {\omega} _ {i b} ^ {b} - \hat {\omega} _ {i n} ^ {b})\tag{9.8.7}
$$

设与 相对应的姿态阵为 $\pmb { C } _ { b } ^ { n ^ { \prime } }$ ，根据式(9.2.35) (9. 2. 36)所述姿态阵与姿态四元数之间的等价关系，与 $\pmb { C } _ { b } ^ { n ^ { \prime } } = \pmb { C } _ { n } ^ { n ^ { \prime } } \pmb { C } _ { b } ^ { n }$ 相对应的四元数为

$$
\dot {Q} = \delta Q ^ {*} \otimes Q
$$

\~p

$$
\delta \boldsymbol {Q} = \boldsymbol {Q} \otimes \hat {\boldsymbol {Q}} ^ {*}\tag{9.8.8}
$$

式中，也为 引起的误差四元数。

对式(9.8. 8)两边对时间求导数，用式(9.8.6) 和式 (9.8.7)代人之，并考虑到式 (9.2.21)关系，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \delta \dot {\boldsymbol {Q}} = \dot {\boldsymbol {Q}} \otimes \dot {\boldsymbol {Q}} ^ {*} + \boldsymbol {Q} \otimes \dot {\boldsymbol {Q}} ^ {*} = \frac {1}{2} \boldsymbol {Q} \otimes (\boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i b} ^ {b} - \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i n} ^ {b}) \otimes \dot {\boldsymbol {Q}} ^ {*} + \frac {1}{2} \boldsymbol {Q} \otimes (- \tilde {\boldsymbol {\omega}} _ {i b} ^ {b} + \dot {\boldsymbol {\omega}} _ {i n} ^ {b}) \otimes \dot {\boldsymbol {Q}} ^ {*} \\ = - \frac {1}{2} \boldsymbol {Q} \otimes (\tilde {\boldsymbol {\omega}} _ {i b} ^ {b} - \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i b} ^ {b}) \otimes \dot {\boldsymbol {Q}} ^ {*} - \frac {1}{2} \boldsymbol {Q} \otimes \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i n} ^ {b} \otimes \dot {\boldsymbol {Q}} ^ {*} + \frac {1}{2} \boldsymbol {Q} \otimes \tilde {\boldsymbol {\omega}} _ {i n} ^ {b} \otimes \dot {\boldsymbol {Q}} ^ {*} \\ = - \frac {1}{2} \boldsymbol {Q} \otimes \delta \tilde {\boldsymbol {\omega}} _ {i b} ^ {b} \otimes \boldsymbol {Q} ^ {*} \otimes \boldsymbol {Q} \otimes \dot {\boldsymbol {Q}} ^ {*} - \frac {1}{2} \boldsymbol {Q} \otimes \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i n} ^ {b} \otimes \boldsymbol {Q} ^ {*} \otimes \boldsymbol {Q} \otimes \dot {\boldsymbol {Q}} ^ {*} \\ + \frac {1}{2} \boldsymbol {Q} \otimes \dot {\boldsymbol {Q}} ^ {*} \otimes \dot {\boldsymbol {Q}} \otimes \hat {\boldsymbol {\omega}} _ {i n} ^ {b} \otimes \dot {\boldsymbol {Q}} ^ {*} \end{array}
$$

式中

$$
\delta \widetilde {\pmb {\omega}} _ {i b} ^ {b} = \widetilde {\pmb {\omega}} _ {i b} ^ {b} - \pmb {\omega} _ {i b} ^ {b}
$$

根据式 (9. 2. 35)

$$
\delta \tilde {\boldsymbol {\omega}} _ {i b} ^ {n} = \boldsymbol {Q} \otimes \delta \tilde {\boldsymbol {\omega}} _ {i b} ^ {b} \otimes \boldsymbol {Q} ^ {*}, \quad \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i n} ^ {n} = \boldsymbol {Q} \otimes \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i n} ^ {b} \otimes \boldsymbol {Q} ^ {*}, \quad \hat {\boldsymbol {\omega}} _ {i n} ^ {n} = \hat {\boldsymbol {Q}} \otimes \hat {\boldsymbol {\omega}} _ {i n} ^ {b} \otimes \hat {\boldsymbol {Q}} ^ {*}
$$

所以

$$
\delta \dot {\boldsymbol {Q}} = - \frac {1}{2} \delta \tilde {\boldsymbol {\omega}} _ {i b} ^ {n} \otimes \boldsymbol {Q} \otimes \hat {\boldsymbol {Q}} ^ {*} - \frac {1}{2} \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i n} ^ {n} \otimes \boldsymbol {Q} \otimes \hat {\boldsymbol {Q}} ^ {*} + \frac {1}{2} \boldsymbol {Q} \otimes \hat {\boldsymbol {Q}} ^ {*} \otimes \hat {\boldsymbol {\omega}} _ {i n} ^ {n}
$$

将式 (9.8. 8) 代人上式，得

$$
\delta \dot {Q} = - \frac {1}{2} \delta \tilde {\omega} _ {i b} ^ {n} \otimes \delta Q - \frac {1}{2} \omega_ {m} ^ {n} \otimes \delta Q + \frac {1}{2} \delta Q \otimes (\omega_ {m} ^ {n} + \delta \omega_ {m} ^ {n})\tag{9.8.9}
$$

将 $\mathfrak { d } \varrho$ 写成三角形式

$$
\delta Q = \cos \frac {\phi}{2} + \frac {\phi}{\phi} \sin \frac {\phi}{2}
$$

式中， ${ \boldsymbol { \phi } } = \left| \phi \right| , \phi$ >:是由 $| Q \rangle$ 确定的导航坐标系 $n ^ { ' }$ ，相对由 $Q$ 确定的导航坐标系 的偏差角矢量，即姿态误差角矢量。由于 $\cdot \phi$ 是小角，所以 $\delta Q  \overline { { \mathrm { H } } }  \mathrm { J }$ 写成

$$
\delta Q = 1 + \frac {\phi}{2}\tag{9.8.10}
$$

上式两边对 求导后得

$$
\delta \dot {Q} = \frac {\dot {\phi}}{2}\tag{9.8.11}
$$

将式 (9.8.10) 和式 (9.8.11) 代人式 (9.8. 9) ，略去二阶小量后

$$
\dot {\boldsymbol {\phi}} = - \delta \tilde {\boldsymbol {\omega}} _ {i b} ^ {n} + \delta \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i n} ^ {n} + \boldsymbol {\phi} \times \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i n} ^ {n}\tag{9.8.12}
$$

式中

$$
\begin{array}{l} \delta \widetilde {\boldsymbol {\omega}} _ {i b} ^ {n} = \boldsymbol {C} _ {b} ^ {n} ([ \delta K _ {G} ] + [ \delta G ]) \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i b} ^ {b} + \boldsymbol {\varepsilon} ^ {n} \\ [ \delta K _ {G} ] = \operatorname{diag} [ \delta K _ {G x} \quad \delta K _ {G y} \quad \delta K _ {G z} ] \\ [ \delta G ] = \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} 0 & \delta G _ {z} & - \delta G _ {y} \\ - \delta G _ {z} & 0 & \delta G _ {x} \\ \delta G _ {y} & - \delta G _ {x} & 0 \end{array} \right] \end{array}
$$

$\delta K _ { G i } \ 、 \delta G _ { i } ( i = x , y , z )$ 分别为陀螺的刻度系数误差和安装误差角。所以式(9. 8. 12)可写成

$$
\dot {\boldsymbol {\phi}} = \boldsymbol {\phi} \times \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i n} ^ {n} + \delta \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i n} ^ {n} - \boldsymbol {C} _ {b} ^ {n} ([ \delta K _ {G} ] + [ \delta G ]) \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i b} ^ {b} - \boldsymbol {\varepsilon} ^ {n}\tag{9.8.13}
$$

上式即为捷联惯导的姿态误差方程的矢量形式。

比较式(9. 8. 13)和式(8.2.19) ，可看出捷联惯导的姿态误差角受指令角速度和陀螺漂移的影响，这一点和平台式惯导类似。除此之外，捷联惯导的姿态误差角还受运载体的角速度影响，并且陀螺漂移引起姿态误差角向与陀螺漂移相反的方向增长，这一点和平台惯导不同。

当取地理坐标系为导航坐标系时，式(9. 8. 13)可写成

$$
\left[ \begin{array}{c} \dot {\phi} _ {E} \\ \dot {\phi} _ {N} \\ \dot {\phi} _ {U} \end{array} \right] = \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} 0 & - \varphi_ {U} & \phi_ {N} \\ \phi_ {U} & 0 & - \phi_ {E} \\ - \phi_ {N} & \phi_ {E} & 0 \end{array} \right] \left[ \begin{array}{c} - \frac {V _ {N}}{R _ {M} + h} \\ \omega_ {i e} \cos L + \frac {V _ {E}}{R _ {N} + h} \\ \omega_ {i e} \sin L + \frac {V _ {E}}{R _ {N} + h} \tan L \end{array} \right]
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} + \left[ \begin{array}{c} - \frac {\delta V _ {N}}{R _ {M} + h} + \delta h \frac {V _ {N}}{(R _ {M} + h) ^ {2}} \\ - \delta L \omega_ {i e} \sin L + \frac {\delta V _ {E}}{R _ {N} + h} - \delta h \frac {V _ {E}}{(R _ {N} + h) ^ {2}} \\ \delta L \omega_ {i e} \cos L + \frac {\delta V _ {E}}{R _ {N} + h} \tan L + \delta L \frac {V _ {E}}{R _ {N} + h} \sec^ {2} L - \delta h \frac {V _ {E} \tan L}{(R _ {N} + h) ^ {2}} \end{array} \right] \\ - \left[ \begin{array}{l l l} T _ {1 1} & T _ {1 2} & T _ {1 3} \\ T _ {2 1} & T _ {2 2} & T _ {2 3} \\ T _ {3 1} & T _ {3 2} & T _ {3 3} \end{array} \right] \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} \delta K _ {G x} & \delta G _ {z} & - \delta G _ {y} \\ - \delta G _ {z} & \delta K _ {G y} & \delta G _ {x} \\ \delta G _ {y} & - \delta G _ {x} & \delta K _ {G z} \end{array} \right] \left[ \begin{array}{c} \omega_ {i b x} ^ {b} \\ \omega_ {i b y} ^ {b} \\ \omega_ {i b z} ^ {b} \end{array} \right] - \left[ \begin{array}{c} \varepsilon_ {E} \\ \varepsilon_ {N} \\ \varepsilon_ {U} \end{array} \right] \end{array}
$$

展开后得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \dot {\phi} _ {E} = \phi_ {N} \left(\omega_ {i e} \sin L + \frac {V _ {E}}{R _ {N} + h} \tan L\right) - \phi_ {U} \left(\omega_ {i e} \cos L + \frac {V _ {E}}{R _ {N} + h}\right) - \frac {\delta V _ {N}}{R _ {M} + h} + \delta h \frac {V _ {N}}{\left(R _ {M} + h\right) ^ {2}} \\ - T _ {1 1} \left(\delta K _ {G x} \omega_ {i b x} ^ {b} + \delta G _ {z} \omega_ {i b y} ^ {b} - \delta G _ {y} \omega_ {i b z} ^ {b}\right) - T _ {1 2} \left(\delta K _ {G y} \omega_ {i b y} ^ {b} - \delta G _ {z} \omega_ {i b x} ^ {b} + \delta G _ {x} \omega_ {i b z} ^ {b}\right) \\ - T _ {1 3} \left(\delta K _ {G z} \omega_ {i b z} ^ {b} + \delta G _ {y} \omega_ {i b x} ^ {b} - \delta G _ {x} \omega_ {i b y} ^ {b}\right) - \varepsilon_ {E} \\ \dot {\phi} _ {N} = - \phi_ {E} \left(\omega_ {i e} \sin L + \frac {V _ {E}}{R _ {N} + h} \tan L\right) - \phi_ {U} \frac {V _ {N}}{R _ {M} + h} - \delta L \omega_ {i e} \sin L + \frac {\delta V _ {E}}{R _ {N} + h} - \delta h \frac {V _ {E}}{\left(R _ {N} + h\right) ^ {2}} \\ - T _ {2 1} \left(\delta K _ {G x} \omega_ {i b x} ^ {b} + \delta G _ {z} \omega_ {i b y} ^ {b} - \delta G _ {y} \omega_ {i b z} ^ {b}\right) - T _ {2 2} \left(\delta K _ {G y} \omega_ {i b y} ^ {b} - \delta G _ {z} \omega_ {i b x} ^ {b} + \delta G _ {x} \omega_ {i b z} ^ {b}\right) \\ - T _ {2 3} \left(\delta K _ {G z} \omega_ {i b z} ^ {b} + \delta G _ {y} \omega_ {i b x} ^ {b} - \delta G _ {x} \omega_ {i b y} ^ {b}\right) - \varepsilon_ {N} \\ \dot {\phi} _ {U} = \phi_ {E} \left(\omega_ {i e} \cos L + \frac {V _ {E}}{R _ {N} + h}\right) + \phi_ {N} \frac {V _ {N}}{R _ {M} + h} + \delta L \left(\omega_ {i e} \cos L + \frac {V _ {E}}{R _ {N} + h} \sec^ {2} L\right) + \frac {\delta V _ {E}}{R _ {N} + h} \tan L \\ - \delta h \frac {V _ {E} \tan L}{\left(R _ {N} + h\right) ^ {2}} - T _ {3 1} (\delta K _ {G x} \omega_ {i b x} ^ {b} + \delta G _ {z} \omega_ {i b y} ^ {b} - \delta G _ {y} \omega_ {i b z} ^ {b}) - T _ {3 2} (\delta K _ {G y} \omega_ {i b y} ^ {b} - \delta G _ {z} \omega_ {i b x} ^ {b} + \delta G _ {x} \omega_ {i b z} ^ {b}) \\ - T _ {3 3} (\delta K _ {G z} \omega_ {i b z} ^ {b} + \delta G _ {y} \omega_ {i b x} ^ {b} - \delta G _ {x} \omega_ {i b y} ^ {b}) - \varepsilon_ {U} \\ (9. 8. 1 4 a) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 b) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 c) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 d) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 e) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 f) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 g) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 h) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 i) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 j) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 k) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 l) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 m) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 n) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 o) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 p) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 q) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 r) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 s) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 t) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 u) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 v) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 w) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 x) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 y) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 z) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 w) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 x) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 y) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 z) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 w) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 x) \\ (9. 8. 1 4 y) \\ (9. 8. \end{array}
$$

式中

$$
\begin{array}{l} \boldsymbol {\varepsilon} _ {E} = T _ {1 1} \boldsymbol {\varepsilon} _ {x} ^ {b} + T _ {1 2} \boldsymbol {\varepsilon} _ {y} ^ {b} + T _ {1 3} \boldsymbol {\varepsilon} _ {z} ^ {b} \\ \boldsymbol {\varepsilon} _ {N} = T _ {2 1} \boldsymbol {\varepsilon} _ {x} ^ {b} + T _ {2 2} \boldsymbol {\varepsilon} _ {y} ^ {b} + T _ {2 3} \boldsymbol {\varepsilon} _ {z} ^ {b} \\ \boldsymbol {\varepsilon} _ {U} = T _ {3 1} \boldsymbol {\varepsilon} _ {x} ^ {b} + T _ {3 2} \boldsymbol {\varepsilon} _ {y} ^ {b} + T _ {3 3} \boldsymbol {\varepsilon} _ {z} ^ {b} \end{array}
$$

## 9. 9 捷联惯导系统抗干扰自对准

平台式惯导系统的初始对准利用水平加速度计的输出作为修正信息，经过适当加权和滤波后加至陀螺，通过陀螺控制平台旋转消除失准角。捷联惯导没有实体平台，运载体的晃动干扰直接加给陀螺和加速度计。计算机对陀螺和加速度计的测量输出值作处理，解算出姿态阵和速度误差，并从速度误差中估计出失准角.待失准角估计达到稳态后，用失准角估计值对姿态阵作校正，初始对准也就完成了。与平台式惯导的初始对准类似，捷联式惯导的自对准也分相才准和精对准两个过程。

对于基座做微幅晃动的情况，如飞机停在停机坪上受风扰动引起的晃动，粗对准过程中，根据陀螺和加速度计的输出直接解算出姿态阵。这样处理实际上忽略了晃动干扰的影响，将陀螺和加速度计的输出近似看作对地球旋转角速度和重力加速度的测量值。所以粗对准误差主要取决于晃动干扰的剧烈程度。对于基座做较大幅度摇摆运动的情况，如舰船在系泊状态下受风浪作用引起船体摇摆晃荡，摇摆角速度远大于地球自转角速度，无法直接根据陀螺和加速度计的输出计算姿态阵。由于地球做自转运动，对准所在地的重力加速度随地球旋转形成圆锥轨迹，圆锥顶点位于地心，使重力加速度相对惯性空间的指向发生改变。若在两个不同时刻计算出重力加速度在惯性空间内的数学矢量，根据双矢量定姿原理就可确定出北向。基于重力信息的抗摇摆自对准算法正是根据这一思路提出的。精对准在粗对准完成后进行。此过程中，系统一方面根据粗对准确定的姿态阵和陀螺的实时输出解算出姿态阵，另一方面根据加速度计的输出解算出速度输出，由于运载体无移动，所以此速度输出实际上是速度误差，它包含有姿态误差角信息，从中可估计出姿态误差角，待估计达到稳态后，用此估计值对姿态阵作修正，并以此修正后的姿态阵作为导航解算的初始姿态阵，初始对准即告结束。可见捷联惯导对准也使用了地球旋转角速度和重力加速度作为参考基准量，这与平台式惯导自对准本质上是相同的.

捷联惯导精对准方案有多种，此处分别介绍采用参数辨识法实现的开环精对准方案和卡尔曼滤波精对准方案。所介绍方案概念直观，易于理解，并具有很好的工程实用性。

## 9.9.1 粗对准

## 1.抗晃动粗对准

为机体坐标系，规定 $x _ { b } \ 、 y _ { b } 、 z _ { b }$ 分别指向飞机的右、前、上，导航坐标系 取地理坐标系$_ { g }$ ，规定 $n _ { x } ( g _ { x } ) \lrcorner n _ { y } ( g _ { y } ) \lrcorner n _ { z } ( g _ { z } )$ 分别指向东、北、天。则重力加速度和地球自转角速度在 系和系内的分量有如下关系=

$$
\pmb {g} ^ {b} = \pmb {C} _ {n} ^ {b} \pmb {g} ^ {n}, \qquad \pmb {\omega} _ {i e} ^ {b} = \pmb {C} _ {n} ^ {b} \pmb {\omega} _ {i e} ^ {n}
$$

各式两边同时作转置

$$
(\mathbf {g} ^ {b}) ^ {\mathrm{T}} = (\mathbf {g} ^ {n}) ^ {\mathrm{T}} \mathbf {C} _ {b} ^ {n}, \qquad (\pmb {\omega} _ {i e} ^ {b}) ^ {\mathrm{T}} = (\pmb {\omega} _ {i e} ^ {n}) ^ {\mathrm{T}} \mathbf {C} _ {b} ^ {n}
$$

即

$$
\left[ \begin{array}{c} (\boldsymbol {g} ^ {b}) ^ {\mathrm{T}} \\ (\boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i e} ^ {b}) ^ {\mathrm{T}} \end{array} \right] = \left[ \begin{array}{c} (\boldsymbol {g} ^ {n}) ^ {\mathrm{T}} \\ (\boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i e} ^ {n}) ^ {\mathrm{T}} \end{array} \right] \cdot \boldsymbol {C} _ {b} ^ {n}\tag{9.9.1}
$$

自主式初始对准过程中，飞机停在地面上，忽略飞机的晃动影响和陀螺及加速度计的测量误差，并根据地速为零时的比力方程式(7.4.7) ，有

$$
\widetilde {\pmb {\omega}} _ {i b} ^ {b} \approx \pmb {\omega} _ {i e} ^ {b}, \qquad \widetilde {\pmb {f}} ^ {b} \approx - \pmb {g} ^ {b}
$$

式中， $\mathbf { \Delta } _ { \mathbf { \alpha } } ^ { \widetilde { \mathbf { \alpha } } } \mathbf { \tilde { \Xi } } ^ { b }$ 和 $\tilde { f } ^ { b }$ 分别为陀螺和加速度计的输出。

上述关系代人式 (9. 9. 1)，得

$$
\left[ \begin{array}{c} - (\tilde {\boldsymbol {f}} ^ {b}) ^ {\mathrm{T}} \\ (\tilde {\boldsymbol {\omega}} _ {i b} ^ {b}) ^ {\mathrm{T}} \end{array} \right] = \left[ \begin{array}{c} (\boldsymbol {g} ^ {n}) ^ {\mathrm{T}} \\ (\boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i e} ^ {n}) ^ {\mathrm{T}} \end{array} \right] \cdot \boldsymbol {C} _ {b} ^ {n}\tag{9.9.2}
$$

设对准时刻飞机所在地纬度 准确己知，则

$$
\boldsymbol {g} ^ {n} = \left[ \begin{array}{c} 0 \\ 0 \\ - g \end{array} \right], \quad \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i e} ^ {n} = \left[ \begin{array}{c} 0 \\ \omega_ {i e} \cos L \\ \omega_ {i e} \sin L \end{array} \right]
$$

记

$$
\boldsymbol {C} _ {b} ^ {n} = \left[ \begin{array}{l l l} T _ {1 1} & T _ {1 2} & T _ {1 3} \\ T _ {2 1} & T _ {2 2} & T _ {2 3} \\ T _ {3 1} & T _ {3 2} & T _ {3 3} \end{array} \right], \quad \tilde {\boldsymbol {f}} ^ {b} = \left[ \begin{array}{l} \tilde {f} _ {x} ^ {b} \\ \tilde {f} _ {y} ^ {b} \\ \tilde {f} _ {z} ^ {b} \end{array} \right], \quad \tilde {\boldsymbol {\omega}} _ {i b} ^ {b} = \left[ \begin{array}{l} \tilde {\omega} _ {i b x} ^ {b} \\ \tilde {\omega} _ {i b y} ^ {b} \\ \tilde {\omega} _ {i b z} ^ {b} \end{array} \right]
$$

则式 (9. 9. 2) 可写成

$$
\left[ \begin{array}{c c c} - \widetilde {f} _ {x} ^ {b} & - \widetilde {f} _ {y} ^ {b} & - \widetilde {f} _ {z} ^ {b} \\ \widetilde {\omega} _ {i b x} ^ {b} & \widetilde {\omega} _ {i b y} ^ {b} & \widetilde {\omega} _ {i b z} ^ {b} \end{array} \right] \approx \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} 0 & 0 & - g \\ 0 & \omega_ {i e} \cos L & \omega_ {i e} \sin L \end{array} \right] \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} T _ {1 1} & T _ {1 2} & T _ {1 3} \\ T _ {2 1} & T _ {2 2} & T _ {2 3} \\ T _ {3 1} & T _ {3 2} & T _ {3 3} \end{array} \right]
$$

展开上式，可解得

$$
T _ {3 1} \approx \frac {\tilde {f} _ {x} ^ {b}}{g}\tag{9.9.3a}
$$

$$
T _ {3 2} \approx \frac {\tilde {f} _ {y} ^ {b}}{g}\tag{9.9.3b}
$$

$$
T _ {3 3} \approx \frac {\tilde {f} _ {z} ^ {b}}{g}\tag{9. 9. 3c}
$$

$$
T _ {2 1} \approx \frac {1}{\omega_ {i e} \cos L} (\tilde {\omega} _ {i b x} ^ {b} - T _ {3 1} \omega_ {i e} \sin L)\tag{9. 9. 3d}
$$

$$
T _ {2 2} \approx \frac {1}{\omega_ {i e} \cos L} (\tilde {\omega} _ {i b y} ^ {b} - T _ {3 2} \omega_ {i e} \sin L)\tag{9.9.3e}
$$

$$
T _ {2 3} \approx \frac {1}{\omega_ {i e} \cos L} (\tilde {\omega} _ {i b z} ^ {b} - T _ {3 3} \omega_ {i e} \sin L)\tag{9. 9. 3f}
$$

由于姿态阵为单位正交阵，逆和转置相等

$$
\left[ \begin{array}{c c c} T _ {1 1} & T _ {1 2} & T _ {1 3} \\ T _ {2 1} & T _ {2 2} & T _ {2 3} \\ T _ {3 1} & T _ {3 2} & T _ {3 3} \end{array} \right] ^ {- 1} = \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} T _ {1 1} & T _ {2 1} & T _ {3 1} \\ T _ {1 2} & T _ {2 2} & T _ {3 2} \\ T _ {1 3} & T _ {2 3} & T _ {3 3} \end{array} \right]
$$

对上式左端求逆，第一列诸元有如下关系

$$
T _ {1 1} = T _ {2 2} T _ {3 3} - T _ {2 3} T _ {3 2}\tag{9.9.4a}
$$

$$
T _ {1 2} = T _ {2 3} T _ {3 1} - T _ {2 1} T _ {3 3}\tag{9.9.4b}
$$

$$
T _ {1 3} = T _ {2 1} T _ {3 2} - T _ {2 2} T _ {3 1}\tag{9.9.4c}
$$

在实际系统中，惯性器件输出的是角增量和速度增量。设在 ${ T _ { m } } = { t _ { m } } - { t _ { m - 1 } }$ 时间段内陀螺输出的角增量为 $\Delta \theta _ { x } \left( m \right) , \Delta \theta _ { y } \left( m \right) , \Delta \theta _ { z } \left( m \right)$ 加速度计输出的速度增量为 $\Delta V _ { x } \left( m \right) , \Delta V _ { y } \left( m \right)$ $\Delta V _ { z } ( m )$ ，则式 (9. 9. 3) 可改写成

$$
T _ {3 1} \approx \frac {\Delta V _ {x} (m)}{g T _ {m}}\tag{9. 9. 5a}
$$

$$
T _ {3 2} \approx \frac {\Delta V _ {y} (m)}{g T _ {m}}\tag{9.9.5b}
$$

$$
T _ {3 3} \approx \frac {\Delta V _ {z} (m)}{g T _ {m}}\tag{9. 9. 5c}
$$

$$
T _ {2 1} \approx \frac {1}{T _ {m} \omega_ {i e} \cos L} \left[ \Delta \theta_ {x} (m) - T _ {m} T _ {3 1} \omega_ {i e} \sin L \right]\tag{9. 9. 5d}
$$

$$
T _ {2 2} \approx \frac {1}{T _ {m} \omega_ {i e} \cos L} \left[ \Delta \theta_ {y} (m) - T _ {m} T _ {3 2} \omega_ {i e} \sin L \right]\tag{9. 9. 5e}
$$

$$
T _ {2 3} \approx \frac {1}{T _ {m} \omega_ {i e} \cos L} \left[ \Delta \theta_ {z} (m) - T _ {m} T _ {3 3} \omega_ {i e} \sin L \right]\tag{9. 9. 5f}
$$

粗对准的精度并不高，原因是忽略了晃动及惯性器件的测量误差影响，失准角一般在数角分至数十角分范围内，视晃动剧烈程度而定。

## 2. 抗摇摇粗对准(秦永元等，2005)

对于诸如舰船在风浪作用下做较大幅度摇摆运动的对准环境，由于摇摆引起的干扰角速度远大于地球自转角速度，陀螺输出中的信噪比十分低，且干扰角速度具有很宽的频带，已无法从陀螺输出中将地球自转角速度这一有用信息提取出来。然而地球自转角速度是一个已知的恒定值，只要时间测准，重力加速度在惯性空间内旋转过的角度可精确计算出，重力加速度在惯性空间内的方向改变包含了地球北向信息，抗摇摆粗对准方法正是利用了这一基本原理。

## 1)粗对准中的坐标系规定

(1)地心地球坐标系 $e :$ 原点位于地心 $\mathfrak { Z } _ { e }$ 轴沿地球自转方向 $x _ { e }$ 轴位于赤道平面内，从地心指向舰船所在点的子午线 $y _ { e }$ 轴与 $x _ { e } , x _ { e }$ 轴构成右手坐标系。

(2) 地心惯性坐标系， $i :$ ，在粗对准起始时刻 $t _ { 0 }$ 将 $o ^ { - } \mathcal { X } _ { e } \mathcal { Y } _ { e } \mathcal { Z } _ { e }$ 惯性凝固后形成的坐标系。

(3) 导航坐标系 $n \colon$ \~p地理坐标系，原点位于舰船重心 $\mathcal { X } _ { n }$ 轴指向东 $y _ { n }$ 轴指向北 ${ \mathcal { Z } } _ { n }$ 轴指向天.

(4) 舰体坐标系 $b \colon$ 原点位于舰船重 $\therefore \dot { \Sigma } \cdot \mathcal { X } _ { b } \setminus \mathcal { Y } _ { b } \setminus \mathcal { Z } _ { b }$ 轴分别沿舰体横轴指向右、沿纵轴指向前、沿立轴指向上。

(5) 基座惯性坐标系 $i _ { b _ { 0 } }$ :在 $t _ { 0 }$ 时刻将舰体坐标系 经惯性凝固后的坐标系。

## 2) 粗对准算法原理

设对准点的纬度为 $L$ ，则姿态矩阵可由下式确定

$$
\mathbf {C} _ {b} ^ {n} = \mathbf {C} _ {e} ^ {n} \mathbf {C} _ {i} ^ {e} \mathbf {C} _ {i _ {b _ {0}}} ^ {i} \mathbf {C} _ {b ^ {b _ {0}}} ^ {i}\tag{9. 9. 6}
$$

式中

$$
\boldsymbol {C} _ {e} ^ {n} = \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} 0 & 1 & 0 \\ - \sin L & 0 & \cos L \\ \cos L & 0 & \sin L \end{array} \right]\tag{9.9.7a}
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {C} _ {i} ^ {e} = \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} \cos \omega_ {i e} (t - t _ {0}) & \sin \omega_ {i e} (t - t _ {0}) & 0 \\ - \sin \omega_ {i e} (t - t _ {0}) & \cos \omega_ {i e} (t - t _ {0}) & 0 \\ 0 & 0 & 1 \end{array} \right]\tag{9. 9. 7b}
$$

$\pmb { C } _ { b 0 } ^ { i _ { b _ { 0 } } } \ ( t _ { 0 } ) { = } \pmb { I } , \pmb { C } _ { b 0 } ^ { i _ { b _ { 0 } } } \ ( t )$ 根据陀螺输出通过旋转矢量求解获得。所以 $\mathbf { { C } } _ { b } ^ { n }$ ，的确定关键在于 $\pmb { C } _ { i _ { b _ { 0 } } } ^ { i }$ 。的确定。

在摇摆基座上，加速度计的输出在 $i _ { b _ { 0 } }$ 。坐标系内的投影为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \boldsymbol {C} _ {b ^ {0}} ^ {i _ {b}} \widetilde {\boldsymbol {f}} ^ {b} & = \boldsymbol {C} _ {b ^ {0}} ^ {i _ {b}} \left[ (\boldsymbol {I} + \delta \boldsymbol {K} _ {A}) (\boldsymbol {I} + \delta \boldsymbol {A}) (- \boldsymbol {g} ^ {b} + \boldsymbol {a} _ {L A} ^ {b} + \boldsymbol {a} _ {D} ^ {b}) + \nabla^ {b} \right] \\ & = - \boldsymbol {C} _ {b ^ {0}} ^ {i _ {b}} \boldsymbol {g} ^ {b} + \boldsymbol {C} _ {b ^ {0}} ^ {i _ {b}} \boldsymbol {a} _ {L A} ^ {b} + \delta \boldsymbol {a} ^ {i _ {b _ {0}}} = - \boldsymbol {C} _ {b ^ {0}} ^ {i _ {b}} \boldsymbol {g} ^ {b} + \boldsymbol {a} _ {L A} ^ {i _ {b _ {0}}} + \delta \boldsymbol {a} ^ {i _ {b _ {0}}} \end{array}\tag{9.9.8}
$$

式中

$$
\boldsymbol {\nabla} ^ {b} = \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} \nabla_ {x} & \nabla_ {y} & \nabla_ {z} \end{array} \right] ^ {\mathrm{T}}, \quad \delta \boldsymbol {K} _ {A} = \operatorname{diag} \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} \delta K _ {A x} & \delta K _ {A y} & \delta K _ {A z} \end{array} \right]
$$

$$
\delta \boldsymbol {A} = \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} 0 & \delta A _ {z} & - \delta A _ {y} \\ - \delta A _ {z} & 0 & \delta A _ {x} \\ \delta A _ {y} & - \delta A _ {x} & 0 \end{array} \right]
$$

$\pmb { a } _ { L A } ^ { b }$ 为捷联惯导质心相对舰体浮心存在杆臂 所产生的杆臂干扰加速度， $\pmb { a } _ { D } ^ { b }$ 为舰体垂荡、纵荡、

横荡及振动产生的干扰角速度，

$$
\delta \boldsymbol {a} ^ {i _ {b _ {0}}} = \boldsymbol {C} _ {b ^ {b _ {0}}} ^ {i} \left[ \boldsymbol {a} _ {D} ^ {b} + (\delta \boldsymbol {K} _ {A} + \delta \boldsymbol {A}) (- \boldsymbol {g} ^ {b} + \boldsymbol {a} _ {L A} ^ {b} + \boldsymbol {a} _ {D} ^ {b}) + \boldsymbol {\nabla} ^ {b} \right]
$$

对式 (9. 9. 8) $[ t _ { 0 } , t _ { k }$ ] 内积分并记:

$$
\widetilde {\boldsymbol {V}} _ {b _ {0}} ^ {i} = \int_ {t _ {0}} ^ {t _ {k}} \boldsymbol {C} _ {b ^ {b _ {0}}} ^ {i} \widetilde {\boldsymbol {f}} ^ {b} \mathrm{d} t\tag{9.9.9}
$$

则

$$
\widetilde {\boldsymbol {V}} ^ {i _ {b _ {0}}} = - \boldsymbol {C} _ {i ^ {b _ {0}}} ^ {i} \int_ {t _ {0}} ^ {t _ {k}} \boldsymbol {g} ^ {i} \mathrm{d} t + \boldsymbol {V} _ {L A} ^ {i _ {b _ {0}}} + \delta \boldsymbol {V} ^ {i _ {b _ {0}}}\tag{9.9.10}
$$

式中

$$
\mathbf {V} _ {L A} ^ {i _ {b _ {0}}} = - \mathbf {C} _ {b ^ {b _ {0}}} ^ {i} (\pmb {\omega} _ {i e} ^ {b} \times \mathbf {r} ^ {b})\tag{9.9.11}
$$

$$
\delta \boldsymbol {V} ^ {i _ {b _ {0}}} = \int_ {t _ {0}} ^ {t _ {k}} \delta \boldsymbol {a} ^ {i _ {b _ {0}}} d t
$$

记

$$
\boldsymbol {V} ^ {i _ {b _ {0}}} = - \boldsymbol {C} _ {t ^ {b _ {0}}} ^ {i} \int_ {t _ {0}} ^ {t _ {k}} \boldsymbol {g} ^ {i} \mathrm{d} t = \boldsymbol {C} _ {t ^ {b _ {0}}} ^ {i} \boldsymbol {V} ^ {i} (t _ {k})\tag{9.9.12}
$$

$$
\overline {{\pmb {V}}} ^ {i _ {b _ {0}}} = \widetilde {\pmb {V}} ^ {i _ {b _ {0}}} - \pmb {V} _ {L A} ^ {i _ {b _ {0}}}\tag{9.9.13}
$$

式中

$$
\mathbf {V} ^ {i} \left(t _ {k}\right) = \int_ {t _ {0}} ^ {t _ {k}} \left(- \mathbf {g} ^ {i}\right) \mathrm{d} t\tag{9.9.14}
$$

则式(9.9.10)可近似写成 $\mathbf { } V ^ { i } b _ { 0 } \approx \widetilde { V } ^ { i } b _ { 0 } - V _ { L A } ^ { i }$ ,即

$$
\pmb {C} _ {i ^ {b _ {0}}} ^ {i} \pmb {V} ^ {i} \approx \overline {{\pmb {V}}} ^ {i} _ {b _ {0}}\tag{9.9.15}
$$

由于

$$
\mathbf {g} ^ {i} = \mathbf {C} _ {e} ^ {i} \mathbf {C} _ {n} ^ {e} \mathbf {g} ^ {n} = \left[ \begin{array}{c} - g \cos L \cos \omega_ {i e} (t - t _ {0}) \\ - g \cos L \sin \omega_ {i e} (t - t _ {0}) \\ - g \sin L \end{array} \right]\tag{9.9.16}
$$

所以

$$
\mathbf {V} ^ {i} \left(t _ {k}\right) = \left[ \begin{array}{c} \frac {g \cos L}{\omega_ {i e}} \sin \omega_ {i e} \Delta t _ {k} \\ \frac {g \cos L}{\omega_ {i e}} \left(1 - \cos \omega_ {i e} \Delta t _ {k}\right) \\ \Delta t _ {k} g \sin L \end{array} \right]\tag{9.9.17}
$$

式中 ， $\Delta t _ { k } = t _ { k } - t _ { 0 }$

根据式 (9. 9. 15) ，在 $t _ { k _ { 1 } }$ 和 $t _ { k _ { 2 } }$ 时刻t $( t _ { 0 } { < } t _ { k _ { 1 } } { < } t _ { k _ { 2 } } )$ ，有.

$$
\overline {{\boldsymbol {V}}} ^ {i _ {b _ {0}}} \left(t _ {k _ {1}}\right) = \boldsymbol {C} _ {i ^ {b _ {0}}} ^ {i} \boldsymbol {V} ^ {i} \left(t _ {k _ {1}}\right)\tag{9.9.18a}
$$

$$
\overline {{{\boldsymbol {V}}}} ^ {i _ {b _ {0}}} \left(t _ {k _ {2}}\right) = \boldsymbol {C} _ {i ^ {b _ {0}}} ^ {i} \boldsymbol {V} ^ {i} \left(t _ {k _ {2}}\right)\tag{9.9.18b}
$$

由式(9.9.18),可得

$$
\hat {\boldsymbol {C}} _ {i _ {b _ {0}}} ^ {i} = \left[ \begin{array}{c} \boldsymbol {V} ^ {i \text {T}} (t _ {k _ {1}}) \\ \boldsymbol {V} ^ {i \text {T}} (t _ {k _ {2}}) \\ [ \boldsymbol {V} ^ {i} (t _ {k _ {1}}) \times \boldsymbol {V} ^ {i} (t _ {k _ {2}}) ] ^ {\mathrm{T}} \end{array} \right] ^ {- 1} \left[ \begin{array}{c} \overline {{\boldsymbol {V}}} ^ {i _ {b _ {0}} \text {T}} (t _ {k _ {1}}) \\ \overline {{\boldsymbol {V}}} ^ {i _ {b _ {0}} \text {T}} (t _ {k _ {2}}) \\ [ \overline {{\boldsymbol {V}}} ^ {i _ {b _ {0}}} (t _ {k _ {1}}) \times \overline {{\boldsymbol {V}}} ^ {i _ {b _ {0}}} (t _ {k _ {2}}) ] ^ {\mathrm{T}} \end{array} \right]\tag{9.9.19}
$$

式中， $V ^ { i } ( t _ { k _ { i } } ) ( j = 1 , 2 )$ 接式 (9.9.17) 计算， $\overline { { { \bf V } } } ^ { i _ { b _ { 0 } } } ( t _ { k _ { i } } ) ( j = 1 , 2 )$ 按式 (9.9.9) 、式 .11)(9. 9. 13) 计算。

获得 $\cdot \mathbf { C } _ { i _ { b _ { \| } } } ^ { i }$ 后，代人式(9. 9. 6) ，即可计算出粗对准确定的姿态阵。

## 3) 仿真环境假设

(1)假设在风浪影响下，舰体的航向角 $\psi ,$ 俯仰角。、横滚角 作周期变化:

$$
\psi = 3 0 ^ {\circ} + 5 ^ {\circ} \cos \left(\frac {2 \pi}{7} t + \frac {\pi}{3}\right)\tag{9.9.20a}
$$

$$
\theta = 7 ^ {\circ} \cos \left(\frac {2 \pi}{5} t + \frac {\pi}{4}\right)\tag{9.9.20b}
$$

$$
\gamma = 1 0 ^ {\circ} \cos \left(\frac {2 \pi}{6} t + \frac {\pi}{7}\right)\tag{9. 9. 20c}
$$

(2) 存在横荡、纵荡和垂荡引起的线速度:

$$
V _ {D _ {i}} = A _ {D _ {i}} \omega_ {D _ {i}} \cos \left(\omega_ {D _ {i}} t + \varphi_ {D _ {i}}\right), \quad i = x, y, z\tag{9.9.21}
$$

式中， $, A _ { D _ { x } } = 0 . \ 0 2 \mathrm { m } , A _ { D _ { y } } = 0 . \ 0 3 \mathrm { m } , A _ { D _ { z } } = 0 . \ 3 \mathrm { m } ; \omega _ { D _ { i } } = { \frac { 2 \pi } { T _ { D _ { i } } } } , T _ { D _ { x } } = 7 \mathrm { s } , T _ { D _ { y } } = 6 \mathrm { s } , T _ { D _ { z } } = 8 \mathrm { s } ; \varphi _ { D _ { i } } = { \frac { 2 \pi } { T _ { D _ { i } } } } , T _ { D _ { y } } = { \frac { 2 \pi } { T _ { D _ { i } } } } , T _ { D _ { y } } = { \frac { 2 \pi } { T _ { D _ { i } } } } , T _ { D _ { z } } = 9 .$ 为$[ 0 , 2 \pi ]$ 上服从均匀分布的随机相位

(3) 存在高频振动引起的线速度:

$$
V _ {D H _ {i}} = \frac {A _ {D H _ {i}}}{2 \pi f _ {D H _ {i}}} \cos \left(2 \pi f _ {D H _ {i}} + \varphi_ {D H _ {i}}\right), \quad i = x, y, z\tag{9.9.22}
$$

式中， $, A _ { D H _ { x } } = 4 . \ 2 g , A _ { D H _ { y } } = 3 . \ 8 g , A _ { D H _ { z } } = 4 g \{ f _ { D H _ { x } } = 3 0 0 \forall z , f _ { D H _ { y } } = 2 5 0 \forall z , f _ { D H _ { z } } = 4 0 0 \forall z \} \oplus \bar { y } _ { D H _ { y } } = 3 . 0 3 6 \forall z , f _ { D H _ { z } } = 3 . 0 3 $ 为$[ 0 , 2 \pi ]$ 服从均匀分布的随机相位

(4) 假设陀螺的随机常值漂移为 $\varepsilon _ { b i } = 0 . 0 1 ^ { \circ } / h ( i = x , y , z )$ ，随机游走系数 $\sigma _ { \varepsilon _ { w i } } = 0 . \ 0 0 1$ Jh$( i = x , y , z )$ ，加速度计的随机常值偏置为 $\nabla _ { b i } = 1 \times 1 0 ^ { - 4 } g ( i = x , y , z )$ 0

## 的仿真结果和分析

姿态矩阵的理想值 $\mathbf { C } _ { b } ^ { n }$ 由仿真环境假设的￠ $\psi , \theta , \gamma$ 确定，以粗对准结束时刻的姿态误差角 $\phi ^ { n }$ 作为衡量粗对准精度的指标。共进行 50 个样本的粗对准仿真，每次粗对准仿真时间为 120s，且取$t _ { k _ { 1 } } = 5 0 \mathrm { s } , t _ { k _ { 2 } } = 1 2 0 \mathrm { s }$ 真结果如图 9. 9.1 .3 示。由图 看出

![](images/10e443265f97c705697a2a16f31c9a7cab8036a696256e52a74202431c869b6b.jpg)  
9. 9. 1 东向水平失准角

![](images/f092f6cda7327ec480845ee64f749ca837b03655013c347749549e8b7eaa34a2.jpg)  
.9. 北向水平失准角

![](images/f8711946bfb69e6151782c9ea082c85bb2a26dcbc39e2955b22fa833cc703a85.jpg)  
9. 9. 3 天向方位失准角

50 个样本的东向、北向、天向姿态误差角的均值分别为 $2 . 0 1 ^ { \prime } , - 1 . 3 8 ^ { \prime }$ 和 $- 0 . 2 0 ^ { \circ }$ ，东向、北向、天向姿态误差角的标准差分别为 0.26 , O. 21'1. 因此上述粗对准算法能有效计算出舰船姿态阵的粗略值，姿态误差角可视为小角，在此基础上可对舰船姿态阵作精确估计，即对捷联惯导的精对准

## 5) 结论

(1)较大幅度摇摆基座上的捷联惯导粗对准方法与微幅晃动基座上的粗对准方法有着明显的不同，后者根据陀螺和加速度计对地球旋转角速度

和重力加速度的直接测量值计算出姿态阵的粗略值，而前者根据式(9.9.6)求取姿态阵的粗略值，其最大特点是基于重力加速度信息在惯性坐标系内解算有关变换矩阵

(2) (9. 9. 6)将姿态矩阵的求取分散成四个矩阵的求取: $C _ { b 0 } ^ { i }$ 反映了摇摆基座的姿态变化，由于初值为单位阵，所以可根据陀螺输出，通过旋转矢量法求解; $C _ { i } ^ { e }$ 反映了地球的旋转信息，只要准确计时，就能准确计算该矩阵 $\mathbf { ; C } _ { e } ^ { n }$ 利用了对准点的地理信息; $\bullet ^ { c _ { i _ { b _ { 0 } } } ^ { i } }$ 是常阵，它根据重力加速度在地心惯性坐标系 和舰体惯性坐标系 $i _ { b _ { 0 } }$ 中的积分通过式(9.9. 9) 由于计算重力加速度在 $i _ { b _ { 0 } }$ 。坐标系内的积分值时未对垂荡、纵荡和横荡干扰速度作补偿，所以解算 $\boldsymbol { C } _ { i _ { b _ { 0 } } } ^ { i }$ 时会产生较大误差。因此本文所提对准算法只适用于粗对准。而精对准解算可在粗对准基础上采用最小乘法或卡尔曼滤波法完成。

## 9.9.2 参数辨识法精对准(秦永元，1990)

粗对准确定的姿态阵是紧接着进行的姿态更新的初值 姿态更新常采用旋转矢量法，在各更新时间点上所确 的姿态阵实际上建立起了数学平台。由 粗对准确定的姿态阵误差较大，所以与数学平台相对应的实际建立的导航坐标系 $n ^ { ' }$ 与要求建立的理想导航坐标系 存在偏差角。此偏差角即为对准失准角。精对准所要做的工作就是估计出失准角，待失准角估计值达到稳态后，用此失准角估计值对最新得到的姿态阵作 次性修正，整个初始对准过程也就结束了

对失准角作实时估计的方法有多种，其中采用参数辨识法实现的估计是行之有效的方法。

## 1.姿态误差角分析

实际建立的导航坐标系 $n ^ { ' }$ 偏离理想、导航坐标系 的失准角为 $\ l { 1 } \phi _ { E } \ l , \phi _ { N } \lsetminus \phi _ { U }$ 过粗对准它们角，所以。 $\pmb { \phi } { = } \big [ \phi _ { E } \big . \quad \phi _ { N } \quad \phi _ { U } \big ]$ JT可看做 系至 $n ^ { ' }$ 系的等效旋转矢量。根据式(9 .4 ，对于如 $\hphantom { { ' } } \setminus \phi _ { N } \setminus \phi _ { U }$ 如均为小角的情况，略去高阶小量后

$$
\boldsymbol {C} _ {n ^ {\prime}} ^ {n} = \boldsymbol {I} + (\boldsymbol {\phi} \times)\tag{9.9.23}
$$

式中

$$
(\boldsymbol {\phi} \times) = \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} 0 & - \phi_ {U} & \phi_ {N} \\ \phi_ {U} & 0 & - \phi_ {E} \\ - \phi_ {N} & \phi_ {E} & 0 \end{array} \right]\tag{9.9.24}
$$

所以

$$
\boldsymbol {C} _ {b} ^ {n} = \boldsymbol {C} _ {n ^ {\prime}} ^ {n} \boldsymbol {C} _ {b} ^ {n ^ {\prime}} = [ \boldsymbol {I} + (\boldsymbol {\phi} \times) ] \boldsymbol {C} _ {b} ^ {n ^ {\prime}}\tag{9.9.25}
$$

(9. 9. 25)说明，计算出姿态阵 $C _ { b } ^ { n ^ { \prime } }$ 后，若能准确确定出失准角 $\phi _ { E } \setminus \phi _ { N } \setminus \phi _ { U }$ ，则可用来对 $C _ { b } ^ { n ^ { \prime } }$ 作修正从而获得精确的姿态阵。因此精对准实质上就是精确确定出失准角如 $\phi _ { E } \setminus \phi _ { N } \setminus \phi _ { U }$ o

自对准中，对准地纬度准确已知，运载体元移动，所以 ${ \pmb { \omega } } _ { i n } = { \pmb { \omega } } _ { i e } , \delta { \pmb { \omega } } _ { i n } = 0$ ，根据式 (9. 8. 13) .此情况下，捷联惯导的姿态误差方程为

$$
\dot {\boldsymbol {\phi}} = \boldsymbol {\phi} \times \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i e} ^ {n} - \boldsymbol {C} _ {b} ^ {n} (\left[ \delta K _ {G} \right] + \left[ \delta G \right]) \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i b} ^ {b} - \boldsymbol {C} _ {b} ^ {n} \boldsymbol {\varepsilon} ^ {b}\tag{9. 9. 26}
$$

式中

$$
\left[ \delta K _ {G} \right] = \operatorname{diag} \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} \delta K _ {G x} & \delta K _ {G y} & \delta K _ {G z} \end{array} \right], \quad \left[ \delta G \right] = \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} 0 & \delta G _ {z} & - \delta G _ {y} \\ - \delta G _ {z} & 0 & \delta G _ {x} \\ \delta G _ {y} & - \delta G _ {x} & 0 \end{array} \right]
$$

$\delta K _ { G x } \backprime \nabla K _ { G y } \backprime \nabla K _ { G z }$ 为陀螺的刻度系数误差.ð $\partial G _ { x } \backprime \partial G _ { y } \backprime \partial G _ { z }$ .为陀螺的安装误差角 . $\cdot \pmb { \varepsilon } ^ { b }$ 为陀螺漂移，对准过程中近似看作随机常值向量。

当运载体晃动幅度不大时 $\mathbf { \mathcal { C } } _ { b } ^ { n }$ 可近似看作常阵，所以式(9.9.26)右端最后一项 $C _ { b } ^ { n } \pmb { \mathcal { E } } ^ { b }$ 可近似看作常值向量，并称之为陀螺在导航坐标系内的等效常值漂移，记为

$$
\boldsymbol {\varepsilon} ^ {n} = \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} \varepsilon_ {E} & \varepsilon_ {N} & \varepsilon_ {U} \end{array} \right] ^ {\mathrm{T}}
$$

又由于晃动角速度 ${ \bf \dot { \omega } } _ { \bf \dot { \omega } }$ 远大于地球自转角速度，即有 ${ \pmb { \omega } } _ { i b } \approx { \pmb { \omega } } _ { \mathrm { d } }$ d' 所以式 (9.9.26) 右端第二项可写成

$$
\boldsymbol {C} _ {b} ^ {n} \left(\left[ \delta K _ {G} \right] + [ \delta G ]\right) \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {i b} ^ {b} \approx \boldsymbol {C} _ {b} ^ {n} \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} \delta K _ {G x} & \delta G _ {z} & - \delta G _ {y} \\ - \delta G _ {z} & \delta K _ {G y} & \delta G _ {x} \\ \delta G _ {y} & - \delta G _ {x} & \delta K _ {G z} \end{array} \right] \left[ \begin{array}{l} \omega_ {d x} \\ \omega_ {d y} \\ \omega_ {d z} \end{array} \right] = \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} \delta_ {1 1} & \delta_ {1 2} & \delta_ {1 3} \\ \delta_ {2 1} & \delta_ {2 2} & \delta_ {2 3} \\ \delta_ {3 1} & \delta_ {3 2} & \delta_ {3 3} \end{array} \right] \left[ \begin{array}{l} \omega_ {\mathrm{d} x} \\ \omega_ {\mathrm{d} y} \\ \omega_ {\mathrm{d} z} \end{array} \right]
$$

记

$$
\begin{array}{l} w _ {E} = \delta_ {1 1} \omega_ {\mathrm{d} x} + \delta_ {1 2} \omega_ {\mathrm{d} y} + \delta_ {1 3} \omega_ {\mathrm{d} z} \\ w _ {N} = \delta_ {2 1} \omega_ {\mathrm{d} x} + \delta_ {2 2} \omega_ {\mathrm{d} y} + \delta_ {2 3} \omega_ {\mathrm{d} z} \\ w _ {U} = \delta_ {3 1} \omega_ {\mathrm{d} x} + \delta_ {3 2} \omega_ {\mathrm{d} y} + \delta_ {3 3} \omega_ {\mathrm{d} z} \end{array}
$$

并将

$$
\boldsymbol {w} ^ {n} (t) = \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} \omega_ {E} & \omega_ {N} & \omega_ {U} \end{array} \right] ^ {\mathrm{T}}
$$

称之为陀螺的等效时变漂移.

所以式 (9. 9. 26) 可写成

$$
\dot {\pmb {\phi}} = - \pmb {\omega} _ {i e} ^ {n} \times \pmb {\phi} - \pmb {\varepsilon} ^ {n} - \pmb {w} ^ {n}
$$

上式写成分量形式

$$
\left[ \begin{array}{c} \dot {\phi} _ {E} \\ \dot {\phi} _ {N} \\ \dot {\phi} _ {U} \end{array} \right] = \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} 0 & \omega_ {i e} \sin L & - \omega_ {i e} \cos L \\ - \omega_ {i e} \sin L & 0 & 0 \\ \omega_ {i e} \cos L & 0 & 0 \end{array} \right] \left[ \begin{array}{c} \phi_ {E} \\ \phi_ {N} \\ \phi_ {U} \end{array} \right] - \left[ \begin{array}{c} \varepsilon_ {E} \\ \varepsilon_ {N} \\ \varepsilon_ {U} \end{array} \right] - \left[ \begin{array}{c} w _ {E} \\ w _ {N} \\ w _ {U} \end{array} \right]\tag{9.9.27}
$$

为便于求解上式，记

$$
\boldsymbol {A} = \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} 0 & \omega_ {i e} \sin L & - \omega_ {i e} \cos L \\ - \omega_ {i e} \sin L & 0 & 0 \\ \omega_ {i e} \cos L & 0 & 0 \end{array} \right], \quad \boldsymbol {x} = \left[ \begin{array}{l} \phi_ {E} - \phi_ {E 0} \\ \phi_ {N} - \phi_ {N 0} \\ \phi_ {U} - \phi_ {U 0} \end{array} \right]
$$

式中 $\begin{array} { r } { \pmb { \phi } _ { 0 } = [ \pmb { \phi } _ { E 0 } \ \quad \pmb { \phi } _ { N 0 } \ \quad \pmb { \phi } _ { U 0 } ] ^ { \mathrm { T } } } \end{array}$ 为姿态误差角初值。则式(9.9.27)可写成

$$
\dot {\boldsymbol {x}} (t) = \boldsymbol {A} \boldsymbol {x} (t) + \boldsymbol {A} \boldsymbol {\phi} _ {0} - \boldsymbol {\varepsilon} ^ {n} - \boldsymbol {w} ^ {n}
$$

记

$$
\boldsymbol {u} = \boldsymbol {A} \boldsymbol {\phi} _ {0} - \boldsymbol {\varepsilon} ^ {n}\tag{9.9.28}
$$

即

$$
u _ {E} = \phi_ {\mathrm{N0}} \omega_ {i e} \sin L - \phi_ {\mathrm{U0}} \omega_ {i e} \cos L - \varepsilon_ {E}\tag{9. 9. 29a}
$$

$$
u _ {N} = - \phi_ {E 0} \omega_ {i e} \sin L - \varepsilon_ {N}\tag{9. 9. 29b}
$$

$$
u _ {U} = \phi_ {E 0} \omega_ {i e} \cos L - \varepsilon_ {U}\tag{9.9.29c}
$$

则式 (9. 9. 28) 可写成

$$
\dot {\boldsymbol {x}} (t) = \boldsymbol {A} \boldsymbol {x} (t) + \boldsymbol {u} - \boldsymbol {w} (t)\tag{9.9.30}
$$

这是具有零初始状态和常激励和变激励的线性定常系统，可用拉氏变换求解。

对式 (9.9.30) 作拉氏变换

$$
\boldsymbol {X} (s) = (s \boldsymbol {I} - \boldsymbol {A}) ^ {- 1} \left[ \frac {\boldsymbol {U}}{s} - \boldsymbol {W} (s) \right]\tag{9.9.31}
$$

记 $( s I - A ) ^ { - 1 } = C$ 通过运算可解得

$$
C _ {1 1} = \frac {s}{s ^ {2} + \omega_ {i e} ^ {2}}, \quad C _ {1 2} = \frac {\omega_ {i e} \sin L}{s ^ {2} + \omega_ {i e} ^ {2}}, \quad C _ {1 3} = - \frac {\omega_ {i e} \cos L}{s ^ {2} + \omega_ {i e} ^ {2}}
$$

$$
C _ {2 1} = - \frac {\omega_ {i e} \sin L}{s ^ {2} + \omega_ {i e} ^ {2}}, \quad C _ {2 2} = \frac {s ^ {2} + \omega_ {i e} ^ {2} \cos^ {2} L}{s \left(s ^ {2} + \omega_ {i e} ^ {2}\right)}, \quad C _ {2 3} = \frac {\omega_ {i e} ^ {2} \sin L \cos L}{s \left(s ^ {2} + \omega_ {i e} ^ {2}\right)}
$$

$$
C _ {3 1} = \frac {\omega_ {i e} \cos L}{s ^ {2} + \omega_ {i e} ^ {2}}, \quad C _ {3 2} = \frac {\omega_ {i e} ^ {2} \sin L \cos L}{s \left(s ^ {2} + \omega_ {i e} ^ {2}\right)}, \quad C _ {3 3} = \frac {s ^ {2} + \omega_ {i e} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} L}{s \left(s ^ {2} + \omega_ {i e} ^ {2}\right)}
$$

作拉氏反变换，记 $\pmb { { \cal B } } = \mathcal { L } ^ { - 1 } \left\{ C \right\}$ ，得

$$
\begin{array}{l l l} B _ {1 1} = \cos \omega_ {i e} t, & B _ {1 2} = \sin L \sin \omega_ {i e} t, & B _ {1 3} = - \cos L \sin \omega_ {i e} t \\ B _ {2 1} = - \sin L \sin \omega_ {i e} t, & B _ {2 2} = \cos^ {2} L + \sin^ {2} L \cos \omega_ {i e} t, & B _ {2 3} = \sin L \cos L (1 - \cos \omega_ {i e} t) \\ B _ {3 1} = \cos L \sin \omega_ {i e} t, & B _ {3 2} = \sin L \cos L (1 - \cos \omega_ {i e} t), & B _ {3 3} = \sin^ {2} L + \cos^ {2} L \cos \omega_ {i e} t \end{array}
$$

由于对准时间一般不超过 20 $\operatorname* { m i n } , \omega _ { i e } t$ 约为 $5 ^ { \circ }$ ，可作如下近似

$$
\cos \omega_ {i e} t \approx 1, \sin \omega_ {i e} t \approx \omega_ {i e} t
$$

所以

$$
\boldsymbol {B} (t) = \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} 1 & t \omega_ {i e} \sin L & - t \omega_ {i e} \cos L \\ - t \omega_ {i e} \sin L & 1 & 0 \\ t \omega_ {i e} \cos L & 0 & 1 \end{array} \right]
$$

(9.9.31)的拉氏反变换为

$$
\boldsymbol {x} (t) = \boldsymbol {B} (t) * \boldsymbol {u} - \boldsymbol {B} (t) * \boldsymbol {w} (t)
$$

式中

$$
\boldsymbol {B} (t) * \boldsymbol {w} (t) = \left[ \begin{array}{c} w _ {E} * 1 + \omega_ {i e} \sin L w _ {N} * t - \omega_ {i e} \cos L w _ {U} * t \\ w _ {N} * 1 - \omega_ {i e} \sin L w _ {E} * t \\ w _ {U} * 1 + \omega_ {i e} \cos L w _ {E} * t \end{array} \right]
$$

通过求解上述卷积可以证明，当机体接近水平，陀螺安装误差为 $1 0 ^ { \prime \prime }$ ，刻度系数误差为 $5 0 \times 1 0 ^ { - 6 }$ 时，陀螺的等效时变漂移引起姿态误差的常值偏置量不超过 $0 . 0 4 ^ { \prime \prime }$ ，漂移量不超过 $5 \times 1 0 ^ { - 6 }$ Ojh ,所以其影响完全可以忽略。

作上述近似后，式(9.9.31)的拉氏反变换为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \boldsymbol {x} (t) = \boldsymbol {B} (t) * \boldsymbol {u} & = \int_ {0} ^ {t} \boldsymbol {B} (\tau) \boldsymbol {u} \mathrm{d} \tau = \left[ \begin{array}{c} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \left(u _ {E} + \tau u _ {N} \omega_ {i e} \sin L - \tau u _ {U} \omega_ {i e} \cos L\right) \mathrm{d} \tau \\ \int_ {0} ^ {t} \left(- \tau u _ {E} \omega_ {i e} \sin L + u _ {N}\right) \mathrm{d} \tau \\ \int_ {0} ^ {t} \left(\tau u _ {E} \omega_ {i e} \cos L + u _ {U}\right) \mathrm{d} \tau \end{array} \right] \\ & = \left[ \begin{array}{c} u _ {E} t + \frac {t ^ {2}}{2} u _ {N} \omega_ {i e} \sin L - \frac {t ^ {2}}{2} u _ {U} \omega_ {i e} \cos L \\ u _ {N} t - \frac {t ^ {2}}{2} u _ {E} \omega_ {i e} \sin L \\ u _ {U} t + \frac {t ^ {2}}{2} u _ {E} \omega_ {i e} \cos L \end{array} \right] \end{array}
$$

NP

$$
\phi_ {E} = \phi_ {E 0} + u _ {E} t + \frac {t ^ {2}}{2} \omega_ {i e} (u _ {N} \sin L - u _ {U} \cos L)\tag{9.9.32a}
$$

$$
\phi_ {N} = \phi_ {N 0} + u _ {N} t - \frac {t ^ {2}}{2} \omega_ {i e} u _ {E} \sin L\tag{9. 9. 32b}
$$

$$
\phi_ {U} = \phi_ {U 0} + u _ {U} t + \frac {t ^ {2}}{2} \omega_ {i e} u _ {E} \cos L\tag{9.9.32c}
$$

## 2. 姿态误差角的参数辨识法求解

晃动基座条件下，加速度计感测两种加速度成分，一种是重力加速度，另一种是晃动引起的干扰加速度。由加速度计组合输出的比力向量经姿态更新确定的姿态阵变换后，可得加速度计的等效水平比力分量输出，由于存在姿态误差角，这些等效水平比力分量中藕合进了重力加速度，即等效水平比力分量含有姿态误差角信息。为了消除由晃动引起的比力中的交变干扰，应选择等效水平比力分量的积分即速度误差作为观测量。

设加速度计的安装误差角为 $\phantom { } 8 A _ { x } \phantom { } _ { \textrm { b } } \delta A _ { y } \phantom { } \cdot \delta A _ { z }$ ，刻度系数误差为 $\delta K _ { A x } \textrm { , } \delta K _ { A y } \textrm { , } \delta K _ { A z }$ 眩，偏置量为$\boldsymbol { \nabla } _ { \boldsymbol { x } } ^ { b } \cdot \boldsymbol { \nabla } _ { \boldsymbol { y } } ^ { b } \cdot \boldsymbol { \nabla } _ { \boldsymbol { z } } ^ { b }$ ，则加速度计的输出为

$$
\tilde {\boldsymbol {f}} ^ {b} = \left(\boldsymbol {I} + \left[ \delta K _ {A} \right]\right) \left(\boldsymbol {I} + \left[ \delta A \right]\right) \left(- \boldsymbol {g} ^ {b} + \boldsymbol {f} _ {d} ^ {b}\right) + \nabla^ {b}
$$

略去二阶小量后

$$
\tilde {\boldsymbol {f}} ^ {b} = - \boldsymbol {g} ^ {b} + \boldsymbol {f} _ {d} ^ {b} + \left(\left[ \delta K _ {A} \right] + \left[ \delta A \right]\right) \left(- \boldsymbol {g} ^ {b} + \boldsymbol {f} _ {d} ^ {b}\right) + \nabla^ {b}
$$

式中

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \left[ \delta K _ {A} \right] & = \operatorname{diag} \left[ \delta K _ {A x} \quad \delta K _ {A y} \quad \delta K _ {A z} \right] \\ \left[ \delta A \right] & = \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} 0 & \delta A _ {z} & - \delta A _ {y} \\ - \delta A _ {z} & 0 & \delta A _ {x} \\ \delta A _ {y} & - \delta A _ {x} & 0 \end{array} \right] \\ \boldsymbol {\nabla} ^ {b} & = \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} \nabla_ {x} ^ {b} & \nabla_ {y} ^ {b} & \nabla_ {z} ^ {b} \end{array} \right] ^ {\mathrm{T}} \end{array}
$$

根据姿态更新确定的姿态阵，可得加速度计在导航坐标系内的等效比力输出

$$
\boldsymbol {f} ^ {n ^ {\prime}} = \boldsymbol {C} _ {b} ^ {n ^ {\prime}} \tilde {\boldsymbol {f}} ^ {b} = \boldsymbol {C} _ {n} ^ {n ^ {\prime}} \left[ - \boldsymbol {g} ^ {n} + \boldsymbol {f} _ {d} ^ {n} + \nabla^ {n} + \boldsymbol {C} _ {b} ^ {n} \left(\left[ \delta K _ {A} \right] + [ \delta A ]\right) \left(- \boldsymbol {g} ^ {b} + \boldsymbol {f} _ {d} ^ {b}\right) \right]
$$

$$
= \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} 1 & \phi_ {U} & - \phi_ {N} \\ - \phi_ {U} & 1 & \phi_ {E} \\ \phi_ {N} & - \phi_ {E} & 1 \end{array} \right] \left[ \begin{array}{c} f _ {d E} + \nabla_ {E} + \delta f _ {E} \\ f _ {d N} + \nabla_ {N} + \delta f _ {N} \\ f _ {d U} + \nabla_ {U} + \delta f _ {U} + g \end{array} \right]
$$

略去二阶小量，得

$$
f _ {E} ^ {n ^ {\prime}} = - g \phi_ {N} + f _ {d E} + \nabla_ {E} + \delta f _ {E}\tag{9. 9. 33a}
$$

$$
f _ {N} ^ {n ^ {\prime}} = g \phi_ {E} + f _ {d N} + \nabla_ {N} + \delta f _ {N}\tag{9.9.33b}
$$

$$
f _ {U} ^ {n ^ {\prime}} = g + f _ {d U} + \nabla_ {U} + \delta f _ {U}\tag{9.9.33c}
$$

式中

$$
\left[ \begin{array}{c c c} \delta f _ {E} & \delta f _ {N} & \delta f _ {U} \end{array} \right] ^ {\mathrm{T}} = \boldsymbol {C} _ {b} ^ {n} (\left[ \begin{array}{c} \delta K _ {A} \end{array} \right] + \left[ \begin{array}{c} \delta A \end{array} \right]) \boldsymbol {f} _ {d} ^ {b}
$$

为对干扰加速度的测量误差

$$
\left[ \begin{array}{c c c} \nabla_ {E} & \nabla_ {N} & \nabla_ {U} \end{array} \right] ^ {\mathrm{T}} = \boldsymbol {C} _ {b} ^ {n} \{\boldsymbol {\nabla} ^ {b} + (\left[ \delta K _ {A} \right] + [ \delta A ]) (- \boldsymbol {g} ^ {b}) \}
$$

为加速度计在导航坐标系内的等效偏置。

为方便起见，定义

$$
\left[ \begin{array}{c c c} f _ {D E} & f _ {D N} & f _ {D U} \end{array} \right] ^ {\mathrm{T}} = \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} f _ {d E} & f _ {d N} & f _ {d U} \end{array} \right] ^ {\mathrm{T}} + \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} \delta f _ {E} & \delta f _ {N} & \delta f _ {U} \end{array} \right] ^ {\mathrm{T}}
$$

为加速度计输出中的等效干扰加速度，显然它与 $f _ { d }$ 同频率且做简谐波动。

这样，式(9. 9. 33)中的等效水平比力分量可写成

$$
f _ {E} ^ {n ^ {\prime}} = - g \phi_ {N} + f _ {D E} + \nabla_ {E}, \quad f _ {N} ^ {n ^ {\prime}} = g \phi_ {E} + f _ {D N} + \nabla_ {N}
$$

将式(9. 9. 32)代人上式，并在 $[ 0 , t ]$ ]内积分，得在[O t]时间段内积累的速度误差为

$$
\Delta V _ {E} (t) = \left(\nabla_ {E} - g \phi_ {N 0}\right) t - \frac {t ^ {2}}{2} g u _ {N} + \frac {t ^ {3}}{6} g \omega_ {i e} u _ {E} \sin L + V _ {D E}\tag{9.9.34a}
$$

$$
\Delta V _ {N} (t) = \left(\nabla_ {N} + g \phi_ {E 0}\right) t + \frac {t ^ {2}}{2} g u _ {E} + \frac {t ^ {3}}{6} g \omega_ {i e} \left(u _ {N} \sin L - u _ {U} \cos L\right) + V _ {D N}\tag{9.9.34b}
$$

从上述两式和式(9. 9. 29)可以看出 ①在 $[ 0 , t ]$ 内积累的速度误差中，水平姿态误差初值构成时间的一次方项，包含陀螺等效北向漂移信息的 $u _ { N }$ 及包含方位误差初值信息的 $u _ { E }$ 构成时间的二次方项，而包含陀螺等效方位漂移信息 $u _ { U }$ 构成时间的三次方项。由于三次曲线在短时间内只呈现线性特性，所以水平姿态误差初值最先辨识出，而辨识等效北向漂移和方位误差初值所需时间比辨识水平姿态误差初值的时间长，辨识等效方位漂移时间更长。这说明缩短对准时间与提高对准和测漂精度的要求是矛盾的，对准精度必须要有一定的时间长度来保证. $\textcircled{2} V _ { D E }$ 和$V _ { D N }$ 是对做简谐波动的等效于扰加速度的积分，没有随时间增长的趋势，而速度误差以时间三次函数积累，所以信噪比随对准时间的增加而加速增大，这对提高辨识精度十分有益。

将式 (9.9.34)改写成

$$
\Delta V _ {E} (k) = a _ {1 E} \left(k T _ {s}\right) + a _ {2 E} \left(k T _ {s}\right) ^ {2} + a _ {3 E} \left(k T _ {s}\right) ^ {3} + V _ {D E}\tag{9.9.35a}
$$

$$
\Delta V _ {N} (k) = a _ {1 N} \left(k T _ {s}\right) + a _ {2 N} \left(k T _ {s}\right) ^ {2} + a _ {3 N} \left(k T _ {s}\right) ^ {3} + V _ {D N}\tag{9.9.35b}
$$

式中， $T _ { s }$ ，为采样周期

$$
a _ {1 E} = \nabla_ {E} - g \phi_ {N 0}\tag{9.9.36a}
$$

$$
a _ {2 E} = - \frac {1}{2} g u _ {N}\tag{9. 9. 36b}
$$

$$
a _ {3 E} = \frac {1}{6} g \omega_ {i e} u _ {E} \sin L\tag{9.9.36c}
$$

$$
a _ {1 N} = \nabla_ {N} + g \phi_ {E 0}\tag{9.9.36d}
$$

$$
a _ {2 N} = \frac {1}{2} g u _ {E}\tag{9.9.36e}
$$

$$
a _ {3 N} = \frac {1}{6} g \omega_ {i e} (u _ {N} \sin L - u _ {U} \cos L)\tag{9. 9. 36f}
$$

它们都可看作常参数，式(9.9.35)为这些常参数的量测方程，根据量测值 $\Delta V _ { E } ( k )$ 和 $\Delta V _ { N } ( k )$ 可辨识出这些常参数。

定义

$$
\boldsymbol {\Theta} _ {E} = \left[ \begin{array}{l} a _ {1 E} \\ a _ {2 E} \\ a _ {3 E} \end{array} \right], \quad \boldsymbol {\Theta} _ {N} = \left[ \begin{array}{l} a _ {1 N} \\ a _ {2 N} \\ a _ {3 N} \end{array} \right]
$$

则面 $\pmb { \Theta } _ { E }$ ' $\mathbf { \delta } \mathcal { \mathbf { Q } } _ { N }$ 的系统方程和量测方程为(秦永元等 1998; Hung J C et al. , 1975):

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} \boldsymbol {\Theta} _ {E} (k + 1) = \boldsymbol {\Theta} _ {E} (k) \\ \Delta V _ {E} (k) = \boldsymbol {H} (k) \boldsymbol {\Theta} _ {E} (k) + V _ {D E} (k) \end{array} \right.\tag{9.9.37}
$$

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} \boldsymbol {\Theta} _ {N} (k + 1) = \boldsymbol {\Theta} _ {N} (k) \\ \Delta V _ {N} (k) = \boldsymbol {H} (k) \boldsymbol {\Theta} _ {N} (k) + V _ {D N} (k) \end{array} \right.\tag{9. 9. 38}
$$

其中

$$
\boldsymbol {H} (k) = \left[ k T _ {s} \quad (k T _ {s}) ^ {2} \quad (k T _ {s}) ^ {3} \right]\tag{9. 9. 39}
$$

$\pmb { \Theta } _ { i }$ 的估计值可按递推最小三乘算法作递推计算

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} \hat {\boldsymbol {\Theta}} _ {i} (k + 1) = \hat {\boldsymbol {\Theta}} _ {i} (k) + \Gamma_ {i} (k + 1) \boldsymbol {P} _ {i} (k) \boldsymbol {H} ^ {\mathrm{T}} (k + 1) \times \left[ \Delta V _ {i} (k + 1) - \boldsymbol {H} (k + 1) \hat {\boldsymbol {\Theta}} _ {i} (k) \right] \\ \boldsymbol {P} _ {i} (k + 1) = \boldsymbol {P} _ {i} (k) - \Gamma_ {i} (k + 1) \boldsymbol {P} _ {i} (k) \boldsymbol {H} ^ {\mathrm{T}} (k + 1) \boldsymbol {H} (k + 1) \boldsymbol {P} _ {i} (k) \\ \Gamma_ {i} (k + 1) = [ 1 + \boldsymbol {H} (k + 1) \boldsymbol {P} _ {i} (k) \boldsymbol {H} ^ {\mathrm{T}} (k + 1) ] ^ {- 1} \\ i = E, N \quad k = 0, 1, 2, \dots \end{array} \right. \tag {9.}\tag{9.9.40}
$$

上述递推算法中， $\mathbf { \nabla } _ { \pmb { \mathcal { \Theta } } } \Theta _ { i } \left( 0 \right)$ 可任选，一般可选零向量 $\pmb { P } _ { i } \left( 0 \right) { = } { \pmb { I } } \alpha _ { i } , \pmb { I }$ 为单位向量， $\alpha _ { i }$ 为非常大的任选正标量。

按式(9.9.40)递推计算得的估计值是逐渐向真实值收敛的，这一过程可看做从量测信息中提炼估计值的浓缩过程，浓缩程度随着递推步数的增加而增加，估计值逐渐向真实值逼近。这一特点正好与量测值的三次曲线特性相一致，在递推的起始阶段，量测值仅仅反映了三次曲线的线性部分，从量测值中不能估计出所有参数，而这段时间正好是递推算法的启动阶段，所以递推算法与量测信息在时间上配合得十分默契。

对 $\mathbf { \dot { \Theta } } _ { \mathbf { \dot { \Theta } } } \mathbf { \Theta } _ { \mathbf { \dot { \Theta } } } \mathbf { \Theta } _ { \mathbf { \dot { \Theta } } } \mathbf { \Theta } _ { \mathbf { \dot { \Theta } } } \mathbf { \Theta } _ { \mathbf { \dot { \Theta } } } \mathbf { \Theta } _ { \mathbf { \dot { \Theta } } } \mathbf { \Theta } _ { \mathbf { \dot { \Theta } } } \mathbf { \Theta } _ { \mathbf { \dot { \Theta } } } \mathbf { \Theta } _ { \mathbf { \dot { \Theta } } } \mathbf { \Theta } _ { \mathbf { \dot { \Theta } } } \mathbf { \Theta } _ { \mathbf { \dot { \Theta } } } \mathbf { \Theta } _ { \mathbf { \dot { \Theta } } } \mathbf { \Theta } _ { \mathbf { \dot { \Theta } } } \mathbf { \Theta } _ { \mathbf { \dot { \Theta } } }$ ，作估计的另一种实用算法是改良卡尔曼滤波法(Panuska V, 1980) ，对应于式(9.9.37)和式 (9. 9. 38) ，算法如下

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} \hat {\boldsymbol {\Theta}} _ {i} (k + 1) = \hat {\boldsymbol {\Theta}} _ {i} (k) + \mathbf {K} _ {i} (k) [ \Delta V _ {i} (k) - \mathbf {H} (k) \hat {\boldsymbol {\Theta}} _ {i} (k) ] \\ \mathbf {K} _ {i} (k) = \mathbf {P} _ {i} (k) \mathbf {H} ^ {\mathrm{T}} (k) [ \mathbf {H} (k) \mathbf {P} _ {i} (k) \mathbf {H} ^ {\mathrm{T}} (k) + \hat {\Lambda} _ {i} (k + 1) ] ^ {- 1} \\ \mathbf {P} _ {i} (k + 1) = \mathbf {P} _ {i} (k) - \mathbf {K} _ {i} (k) [ \mathbf {H} (k) \mathbf {P} _ {i} (k) \mathbf {H} ^ {\mathrm{T}} (k) + \hat {\Lambda} _ {i} (k + 1) ] \mathbf {K} _ {i} ^ {\mathrm{T}} (k) \\ \hat {\Lambda} _ {i} (k + 1) = \hat {\Lambda} _ {i} (k) + [ e _ {i} ^ {2} (k) - \hat {\Lambda} _ {i} (k) ] / (k + 1) \\ e _ {i} (k) = \Delta V _ {i} (k) - \mathbf {H} (k) \hat {\boldsymbol {\Theta}} _ {i} (k) \\ i = E, N \quad k = 0, 1, 2, \dots \end{array} \right.\tag{9.9.41}
$$

式中， (0) (O) (O)均可任选。一般可选 ${ \pmb \Theta } _ { i } \left( 0 \right) = { \pmb 0 } , { \pmb P } _ { i } \left( 0 \right) = { \pmb I } _ { \alpha _ { i } } , { \pmb \alpha } _ { i }$ 为很大的正标量 (0)

推荐值为 0.1 (秦永元， 1990)

上述算法中，计算滤波增益时使用了新息 $e _ { i } ( k )$ ，带来的好处是:不必知道晃动引起速度干扰的统计特性，增益根据每一滤波时刻的新息作自适应加权确定，有利于算法的加速收敛。

由递推算法估计出 $a _ { j i } ( j = 1 , 2 , 3 ; i = E , N )$ 后，根据式(9.9.36)可确定出 $u _ { E } , u _ { N } , u _ { D }$ 及对准失准角初值:

$$
u _ {E} = \frac {2 a _ {2 N}}{g}\tag{9.9.42a}
$$

$$
u _ {N} = - \frac {2 a _ {2 E}}{g}\tag{9.9.42b}
$$

$$
u _ {U} = - \frac {6 a _ {3 N}}{g \omega_ {i e} \cos L} - \frac {2 a _ {2 E}}{g} \tan L\tag{9.9.42c}
$$

$$
\phi_ {E 0} = \frac {a _ {1 N}}{g}\tag{9.9.43a}
$$

$$
\phi_ {N 0} = - \frac {a _ {1 E}}{g}\tag{9.9.43b}
$$

$$
\phi_ {U 0} = \phi_ {N 0} \tan L - \frac {u _ {E} + \varepsilon_ {E}}{\omega_ {i e} \cos L}\tag{9. 9. 43c}
$$

对于东向等效漂移还未测出的情况

$$
\phi_ {U 0} \approx \phi_ {N 0} \tan L - \frac {u _ {E}}{\omega_ {i e} \cos L}\tag{9.9.43d}
$$

失准角初值的计算误差为

$$
\delta \phi_ {E 0} = \frac {\nabla_ {N}}{g}, \quad \delta \phi_ {N 0} = - \frac {\nabla_ {E}}{g}, \quad \delta \phi_ {U 0} = \frac {\varepsilon_ {E}}{\omega_ {i e} \cos L}
$$

这与指北方位惯导系统的对准精度是相同的。

求得上述诸参数后，代人式(9. 9. 32)可计算出对准失准角，按式(9. 9. 25)对姿态更新获得的姿态阵作一次性修正，精对准即告结束。

3. 双位直对准及测漂计算

![](images/82493c240dd5897ede3dcbced8e2c7de2f9c5a474f6dea71cf5566d896616710.jpg)  
9.9.4 双位置对准中的第一位置和第二位置

由式(9. 9. 43c)知，如果己知陀螺的等效东向漂移 $\varepsilon _ { E }$ ，则计算方位失准角初值时可计人 $\in _ { E } .$ ，从而可提高方位对准精度。采用双位置对准可测出 $\varepsilon _ { E }$ 具体方法是:将捷联惯性测量组合(IMU)装在可绕机体轴 ${ \mathcal { Z } } _ { b }$ 转动的转盘上，相对 IMU 的正常工作位置转盘转过 $9 0 ^ { \circ }$ 。作为第一位置，记作 $b ^ { ' }$ 在该位置上完成精对准，以求出等效北向漂移。再将转盘转回到正常工作位置，作为第二位置，记作 $^ { b , }$ 在该位置上完成精对准和所有陀螺的测漂。显然第二位置是基准位置，第一位置相对第二位置的转角为 $9 0 ^ { \circ } + \delta \eta \mathrm { . }$ ，其中 $\delta \eta$ 是由于旋转定位不准引起的转角误差，如图 9.9.4 所示。

由图得

$$
\boldsymbol {C} _ {b} ^ {b ^ {\prime}} = \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} - \sin \delta \eta & \cos \delta \eta & 0 \\ - \cos \delta \eta & - \sin \delta \eta & 0 \\ 0 & 0 & 1 \end{array} \right]\tag{9.9.44}
$$

由于陀螺组合固定在转盘上，所以陀螺漂移在 $b ^ { \prime }$ 系和 系内的分量相同，

$$
\boldsymbol {\varepsilon} ^ {b ^ {\prime}} = \boldsymbol {\varepsilon} ^ {b} = \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} \varepsilon_ {x} & \varepsilon_ {y} & \varepsilon_ {z} \end{array} \right] ^ {\mathrm{T}}
$$

在第一位置上，陀螺在导航坐标系 内的等效漂移为

$$
\boldsymbol {\varepsilon} _ {1} ^ {n} = \boldsymbol {C} _ {b} ^ {n} \boldsymbol {C} _ {b ^ {\prime}} ^ {b} \boldsymbol {\varepsilon} ^ {b ^ {\prime}} = \boldsymbol {C} _ {b} ^ {n} \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} - \sin \delta \eta & - \cos \delta \eta & 0 \\ \cos \delta \eta & - \sin \delta \eta & 0 \\ 0 & 0 & 1 \end{array} \right] \left[ \begin{array}{c} \varepsilon_ {x} \\ \varepsilon_ {y} \\ \varepsilon_ {z} \end{array} \right]
$$

由于 $\delta \eta$ 一般控制在 。左右 $\delta \eta$ 问和陀螺漂移均可看做小量，略去二阶小量后

$$
\boldsymbol {\varepsilon} _ {I} ^ {n} = \boldsymbol {C} _ {b} ^ {n} \left[ \begin{array}{c} - \varepsilon_ {y} \\ \varepsilon_ {x} \\ \varepsilon_ {z} \end{array} \right]\tag{9.9.45}
$$

在第二位置上，陀螺在导航坐标系 内的等效漂移为

$$
\boldsymbol {\varepsilon} _ {\mathrm{II}} ^ {n} = \boldsymbol {C} _ {b} ^ {n} \left[ \begin{array}{c} \varepsilon_ {x} \\ \varepsilon_ {y} \\ \varepsilon_ {z} \end{array} \right]\tag{9.9.46}
$$

初始对准时飞机停在外场接近水平，根据式(9.2.39)

$$
\boldsymbol {C} _ {b} ^ {n} \approx \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} \cos \Psi & \sin \Psi & 0 \\ - \sin \Psi & \cos \Psi & 0 \\ 0 & 0 & 1 \end{array} \right]
$$

上式代人式 (9. 9. 45) 和式 (9. 9. 46) ，得

$$
\boldsymbol {\varepsilon} _ {1} ^ {n} = \left[ \begin{array}{c} \varepsilon_ {x} \sin \Psi - \varepsilon_ {y} \cos \Psi \\ \varepsilon_ {x} \cos \Psi + \varepsilon_ {y} \sin \Psi \\ \varepsilon_ {z} \end{array} \right]\tag{9.9.47}
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {\varepsilon} _ {\mathrm{II}} ^ {n} = \left[ \begin{array}{c} \varepsilon_ {x} \cos \Psi + \varepsilon_ {y} \sin \Psi \\ - \varepsilon_ {x} \sin \Psi + \varepsilon_ {y} \cos \Psi \\ \varepsilon_ {z} \end{array} \right]\tag{9.9.48}
$$

比较式 (9.9.47) 和式 (9. 9. 48) ，得

$$
\varepsilon_ {\mathrm{IIE}} = \varepsilon_ {\mathrm{IN}}\tag{9.9.49}
$$

上式说明 第二位置上的等效东向漂移即为第一位置上的等效北向漂移。根据式(9. 9. 29b)(9. 9. 36b) 和式 (9. 9. 36d) ,

$$
\varepsilon_ {I N} = - \left(u _ {N} ^ {\mathrm{I}} + \phi_ {E 0} ^ {\mathrm{I}} \omega_ {i e} \sin L\right) = \frac {2 a _ {2 E} ^ {\mathrm{I}}}{g} - \frac {a _ {1 N} ^ {\mathrm{I}}}{g} \omega_ {i e} \sin L\tag{9.9.50}
$$

式中，上标 表示第一位置时的相应参数。

在第一位置上测出第二位置上的等效东向漂移 $\varepsilon _ { \mathrm { I I } E }$ 后，在第二位置的精对准中可按(9. 9. 43c)计算方位失准角的初值，从而提高了方位对准的精度。

对陀螺漂移的测漂计算在第二位置对准结束时进行，根据式(9.9.46) ，测漂公式为

$$
